**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian is a strong AI/ML candidate with impressive hands-on experience in RAG systems, LLM pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, and full-stack development — all directly relevant to Cloudflare's AI Engineer role. However, the role explicitly asks for 5+ years of experience with distributed systems, and Ruiqi is a mid-level candidate still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of professional experience, which is a significant gap. Additionally, the job is hybrid in Austin, TX, while the candidate is based in Canada and prefers remote/US/Canada, creating a location friction point that may require relocation or visa considerations.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG pipeline experience: built production-grade RAG system with LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation
- LangGraph multi-agent architecture experience with quality-gated routing in personal projects
- Full-stack AI development capability: FastAPI backend + React/TypeScript frontend + WebSocket + PostgreSQL + Docker
- TypeScript/JavaScript knowledge satisfies Cloudflare's primary language preference
- Published AI research (ACM MM '23) demonstrating depth in video-language models and multimodal QA
- Experience deploying AI solutions via Docker Compose with Redis semantic caching and SSE streaming
- Computer Vision expertise with YOLOv8, SAHI pipeline, and industrial defect detection (91% mAP@50)
- Strong academic credentials: 3.9 GPA MEng at University of Waterloo with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Cloud exposure with AWS and Azure listed in skills
- Experience benchmarking and evaluating AI models (RAGAS, ATS keyword scoring, LLM self-critique)

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 5+ years of distributed systems experience (candidate has ~1-2 years total professional experience)
- Deep distributed systems architecture knowledge at scale (Cloudflare operates one of the world's largest networks)
- Experience debugging and optimizing large-scale frontend applications specifically
- Cloudflare-specific technologies (Workers, Pages, R2, D1, AI Gateway)
- Production experience at enterprise/internet-scale systems

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (hybrid retrieval, reranking, streaming, Redis caching), quantified results (35% precision improvement, 0.84 context recall), and the fact it served 500+ pages of real documents — this directly maps to Cloudflare's need to ship production AI systems.
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as evidence of staying current with emerging AI tooling and architecting complex, maintainable AI workflows — directly relevant to Cloudflare's ETI team building novel AI products.
- Emphasize full-stack AI ownership: you've independently designed, built, and deployed complete AI-powered systems from model selection and evaluation through FastAPI backend to React/TypeScript frontend — Cloudflare needs engineers who can own the full stack.
- Address the experience gap proactively: frame your academic research publication (ACM MM '23), two AI-focused internships, and graduate specialization in LLM/RL/CV as equivalent depth to years of experience in AI specifically, even if total years are fewer.
- Discuss your model evaluation and benchmarking experience (RAGAS, ATS scoring, LLM self-critique) as alignment with Cloudflare's expectation of 'benchmarking various AI models and writing evals.'
- If location is raised, clarify your openness to relocation to Austin, TX or discuss your eligibility to work in the US — this is a critical blocker to address early in the process.

Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Us&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Cloudflare, we are on a mission to help build a better Internet. Today the company runs one of the world’s largest networks that powers millions of websites and other Internet properties for customers ranging from individual bloggers to SMBs to Fortune 500 companies. Internet properties powered by Cloudflare all have web traffic routed through its intelligent global network, which gets smarter with every request. As a result, they see significant improvement in performance and a decrease in spam and other attacks. Cloudflare was named to Entrepreneur Magazine’s Top Company Cultures list and ranked among the World’s Most Innovative Companies by Fast Company.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We realize people do not fit into neat boxes. We are looking for curious and empathetic individuals who are committed to developing themselves and learning new skills, and we are ready to help you do that. We cannot complete our mission without building a diverse and inclusive team. We hire the best people based on an evaluation of their potential and support them throughout their time at Cloudflare. Come join us!&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Available Locations: Austin, TX&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About the Department&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;Emerging Technologies &amp;amp; Incubation (ETI) is where new and bold products are built and released within Cloudflare. Rather than being constrained by the structures which make Cloudflare a massively successful business, we are able to leverage them to deliver entirely new tools and products to our customers. Cloudflare’s edge and network make it possible to solve problems at massive scale and efficiency which would be impossible for almost any other organization.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you&#39;ll do&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;In this role you will design, build, and ship AI-powered product features. You will often collaborate across disciplines by partnering with engineers, data scientists, designers, and stakeholders to integrate AI seamlessly into existing systems and workflows. As the landscape for AI continues to change you will be expected to stay current on emerging AI techniques and tooling, experiment rapidly, and bring forward ideas that are practical, maintainable, and impactful. Applying AI to existing products as well as new products will be an important part of this role and your ability to ship production-grade systems will be an expected requisite. On any given day you might be benchmarking various AI models, writing evals, collaborating with other AI teams on novel solutions, or developing a net-new product to release to Cloudflare customers.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Examples of desirable skills, knowledge and experience&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;5+ years of experience working with distributed systems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience designing and building frontend applications.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Solid understanding of cloud primitives and how they work together to build distributed systems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Knowledge of at least one modern strongly-typed programming language: we primarily use TypeScript.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience debugging, optimizing and identifying failure modes in large-scale frontend applications.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience developing and deploying AI solutions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3 id=&quot;SeniorSystemsEngineerWorkersRuntime-AbouttheDepartment&quot;&gt;&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What Makes Cloudflare Special?&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We’re not just a highly ambitious, large-scale technology company. We’re a highly ambitious, large-scale technology company with a soul. - Full-stack production deployment: FastAPI, Docker, PostgreSQL, WebSocket, React — demonstrates ability to own end-to-end production rollouts
- Canadian location: based in Waterloo, ON, perfectly matching the Canada-based remote role

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**

- DSPy framework experience
- HuggingFace ecosystem experience (not explicitly listed)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the Disanji RAG system: emphasize the production-grade nature — hybrid retrieval, RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall), Redis semantic caching, and Docker deployment — as direct evidence of productionizing GenAI applications.
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project: walk through the quality-gated routing architecture and dual-layer ATS + LLM self-critique evaluation to demonstrate multi-agent system design thinking.

- Show unique specialization value: the FDE team values ensemble diversity — position Computer Vision expertise (YOLOv8, SAHI, 91% mAP@50 industrial defect detection) as a differentiated specialization that strengthens the team.
- Prepare to discuss classical ML tooling: if asked about pandas/scikit-learn/PyTorch, be ready to reference coursework, academic projects, or self-study to fill the gap the resume doesn't explicitly address.
- Express customer-facing enthusiasm: even without formal consulting experience, highlight the research collaboration at Nanjing University and any mentoring, teaching, or presentation experiences from academic life to address the communication requirement.

<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

&lt;p data-pm-slice=&quot;1 1 []&quot;&gt;CSQ227R16&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The AI Forward Deployed Engineering (AI FDE) team is a highly specialized customer-facing AI team at Databricks. We deliver professional services engagements to help our customers build and productionize first-of-its-kind AI applications. We work cross-functionally to shape long-term strategic priorities and initiatives alongside engineering, product, and developer relations, as well as support internal subject matter expert (SME) teams. We view our team as an ensemble: we look for individuals with strong, unique specializations to improve the overall strength of the team. This team is the right fit for you if you love working with customers, teammates, and fueling your curiosity for the latest trends in GenAI, LLMOps, and ML more broadly. This role can be remote.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;2006&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop cutting-edge GenAI solutions, incorporating the latest techniques from our &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/research/mosaic&quot;&gt;Mosaic AI Research&lt;/a&gt; to solve customer problems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own production rollouts of consumer and internally facing GenAI applications.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Serve as a trusted technical advisor to customers across a variety of domains.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Present at conferences such as &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/dataaisummit&quot;&gt;Data + AI Summit&lt;/a&gt;, recognized as a thought leader internally and externally.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally with the product and engineering teams to influence priorities and shape the product roadmap.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;2367&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building GenAI applications, including RAG, multi-agent systems, Text2SQL, fine-tuning, etc., with tools such as HuggingFace, LangChain, and DSPy.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise in deploying production-grade GenAI applications, including evaluation and optimizations.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience, leveraging common machine learning and data science tools (i.e., pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch, etc.).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building production-grade machine learning deployments on AWS, Azure, or GCP.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline (Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Operations Research, etc.) or equivalent practical experience.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience communicating and/or teaching technical concepts to non-technical and technical audiences alike.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passion for collaboration, life-long learning, and driving business value through AI.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;[Preferred] Experience using the Databricks Intelligence Platform and Apache Spark™ to process large-scale distributed datasets.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Pay Range Transparency&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-size: 14px;&quot;&gt;Databricks is committed to fair and equitable compensation practices. The pay range for this role is listed below and represents the expected salary range for non-commissionable roles or on-target earnings for commissionable roles.&amp;nbsp; Actual compensation packages are based on several factors that are unique to each candidate, including but not limited to job-related skills, depth of experience, relevant certifications and training, and specific work location. Based on the factors above, Databricks anticpates utilizing the full width of the range. The total compensation package for this position may also include eligibility for annual performance bonus, equity, and the benefits listed above.&amp;nbsp;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Canada Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$162,400&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$223,384 CAD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. #### 3. Elastic AI Engineer at Elastic

- **Overall Score:** 6.1/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 5/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 8/10 | Salary: 7/10
- **Location:** United States
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse
- **Apply URL:** https://jobs.elastic.co/jobs?gh_jid=7607148&gh_jid=7607148

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML engineering skills — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, and agentic workflows — that align well with the core technical thrust of this Elastic AI Engineer role. However, the candidate has a critical gap in Elastic Stack experience (Elasticsearch, Kibana, ESRE), which is listed as a minimum 1-year hard requirement, and falls short of the 3–5 years of professional work experience required, with only ~6 months of relevant internship experience. The education credentials are excellent (MEng from Waterloo, BASc from UBC with Dean's Honors), and the location/salary preferences are broadly compatible with the role's remote-friendly US-based position and $94K–$179K compensation range.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG pipeline expertise: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3 dense/sparse vectors), FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation achieving 0.84 context recall
- Hands-on LangChain and LangGraph experience: used LangChain LCEL in production and architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing
- Agentic workflow design: built an AI Agent-powered Resume & Cover Letter Generator with multi-agent orchestration and dual-layer evaluation
- LLM integration experience: practical experience with LLM providers, embeddings (BGE-M3), and LLM self-critique patterns
- Python proficiency: primary language used across all AI/ML projects and internships
- Docker and containerization: deployed systems via Docker Compose across multiple projects
- Cloud familiarity: AWS and Azure listed in skills
- Full-stack capability: FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, PostgreSQL — enabling end-to-end agent system delivery
- Academic publication: co-authored paper at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video understanding
- Excellent academic credentials: 3.9 GPA MEng at University of Waterloo, 3.7 GPA BASc at UBC with Dean's Honor List
- Vector database experience: Milvus and ChromaDB usage in production and project contexts
- Redis semantic caching: implemented in production RAG deployment

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Elastic Stack experience (Elasticsearch, Kibana, Logstash, Beats) — minimum 1 year required, not found on resume
- Elasticsearch Relevance Engine (ESRE) — listed as critical knowledge, not found
- Jina AI — listed as critical knowledge, not found
- LangSmith — required for debugging multi-agent systems, not found
- Enterprise Agentic Platforms (Microsoft Copilot Studio, Salesforce Agentforce, ServiceNow AI Agents) — not found
- Model Context Protocol (MCP) — emerging standard listed as required, not found
- Kubernetes (Operators/Controllers) — required for cloud orchestration, not found
- Terraform — required for automated deployment, not found
- 3–5 years of professional work experience — candidate has ~6 months of internship experience
- Multi-Agent Orchestration at enterprise scale — limited to personal project scope

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Emphasize the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: highlight the hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3), FlashRank reranking, 35% precision improvement, and RAGAS evaluation score — this directly maps to the 'Enterprise Grounding' responsibility
- Showcase the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline in the Resume Generator project as concrete evidence of agentic workflow design, multi-agent orchestration, and quality-gated routing — directly addressing the 'Agentic Strategy & Design' requirement
- Address the Elastic Stack gap proactively: express willingness and ability to rapidly learn Elasticsearch/ESRE, drawing parallels to Milvus (vector search) and existing search/retrieval expertise
- Highlight the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video understanding to demonstrate research depth and ability to work on cutting-edge AI problems
- Discuss the full-stack deployment experience (FastAPI + Docker + PostgreSQL + WebSocket) to demonstrate ability to own end-to-end agent infrastructure
- Mention AWS and Azure familiarity in the context of scalable cloud infrastructure for high-concurrency enterprise agents
- Point to the 80%+ test coverage and automated RAGAS evaluation pipeline as evidence of engineering rigor and lifecycle management discipline
- Leverage the MEng specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and DevOps to signal readiness for the technical leadership and domain expert aspects of the role

&lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;Elastic, the Search AI Company, enables everyone to find the answers they need in real time, using all their data, at scale — unleashing the potential of businesses and people. The Elastic Search AI Platform, used by more than 50% of the Fortune 500, brings together the precision of search and the intelligence of AI to enable everyone to accelerate the results that matter. By taking advantage of all structured and unstructured data — securing and protecting private information more effectively — Elastic’s complete, cloud-based solutions for search, security, and observability help organizations deliver on the promise of AI.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;At Elastic, we have a simple goal: to solve the world&#39;s data problems with products that delight and inspire. As the company behind the popular open-source projects — Elasticsearch, Kibana, Logstash, and Beats — we help people around the world do great things with their data. From stock quotes to Twitter streams, Apache logs to WordPress blogs, our products are extending what&#39;s possible with data, delivering on the promise that good things come from connecting the dots. The Elastic family unites employees across 40+ countries into one coherent team, while the broader community spans across over 100 countries.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What is The Role&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Elastic IT team is moving beyond simple chat to the next frontier: Agentic Workflows. We are looking for an innovative &lt;strong&gt;Elastic AI Engineer&lt;/strong&gt; to join our team to build autonomous, enterprise-grounded agents that don&#39;t just answer questions—they complete complex business tasks to accelerate productivity across the entire organization.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The ideal candidate is an Elastic product expert (including but not limited to Agent Builder and Workflows), using the full power of the Elastic Stack to provide the &quot;brain&quot; and &quot;memory&quot; for our agentic ecosystem.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Are you ready to build the agents that supercharge enterprise efficiency? &lt;/strong&gt;Join us to enable agentic workflows that turn collective knowledge into instant action, empowering everyone at Elastic to achieve more.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You Will Be Doing:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Agentic Strategy &amp;amp; Design:&lt;/strong&gt; Invent and implement sophisticated agentic workflows that use reasoning and tools to complete end-to-end business processes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Enterprise Grounding:&lt;/strong&gt; Apply Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) and the Elasticsearch Relevance Engine (ESRE) to ensure agents are deeply grounded in enterprise knowledge for high-accuracy task completion.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;AI Model &amp;amp; Tool Integration:&lt;/strong&gt; Develop and fine-tune LLMs and integrate them with internal APIs and third-party SaaS tools to enable autonomous action.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Scalable Infrastructure:&lt;/strong&gt; Firm understanding of cloud-based environments (AWS, Azure, GCP) in order to support the high-concurrency demands of enterprise agents.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Lifecycle Management:&lt;/strong&gt; Oversee the training, deployment, and performance optimization of agents, ensuring they remain secure, reliable, and compliant.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Technical Leadership:&lt;/strong&gt; Act as a domain expert on the Elastic Stack, making technical recommendations that push the boundaries of AI-driven productivity.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Documentation:&lt;/strong&gt; Maintain comprehensive documentation of AI workflows, cloud infrastructure, and deployment processes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Security:&lt;/strong&gt; Implement standards for security and data privacy to protect sensitive information and ensure compliance with relevant regulations.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you bring:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;3-5 years of work experience in a relevant field.&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Minimum 1 year experience building with the Elastic Stack. &lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Knowledge of Elasticsearch Relevance Engine (ESRE), Jina AI, and advanced RAG patterns is critical.&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Proven success&lt;/strong&gt; in delivering independent GenAI projects, specifically those involving autonomous task completion or complex workflow automation.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Agentic Frameworks:&lt;/strong&gt; Familiarity with &lt;strong&gt;LangGraph&lt;/strong&gt;, &lt;strong&gt;LangChain&lt;/strong&gt;, and &lt;strong&gt;LangSmith&lt;/strong&gt; for building and debugging multi-agent systems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Expertise in Enterprise Agentic &amp;amp; Workflow Platforms:&lt;/strong&gt; Deep familiarity with leading agentic AI and workflow automation platforms (such as Microsoft Copilot Studio, Salesforce Agentforce, ServiceNow AI Agents.)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Market Trend Integration:&lt;/strong&gt; Proven ability to apply emerging market trends—such as &lt;strong&gt;Multi-Agent Orchestration and&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Model Context Protocol (MCP)&lt;/strong&gt;—to build high-impact, cost-optimized solutions that scale across the enterprise.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Programming:&lt;/strong&gt; Experience with &lt;strong&gt;Python&lt;/strong&gt; or &lt;strong&gt;TypeScript&lt;/strong&gt; for backend logic and agent orchestration.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Cloud &amp;amp; Orchestration:&lt;/strong&gt; Familiarity with &lt;strong&gt;Kubernetes&lt;/strong&gt; (Operators/Controllers), &lt;strong&gt;Docker&lt;/strong&gt;, and &lt;strong&gt;Terraform&lt;/strong&gt; for automated deployment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Model Expertise:&lt;/strong&gt; Hands-on experience with LLM providers.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Bonus Points&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Bachelor’s or Master’s degree in Computer Science or a related engineering field.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong communication skills with the ability to translate business requirements into technical agent architectures.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;A commitment to &lt;strong&gt;Ethical AI&lt;/strong&gt; and responsible development practices.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with containerization and orchestration (e.g., Docker, Kubernetes).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Knowledge of DevOps practices for model deployment and automation.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;br&gt;Compensation for this role is in the form of base salary.&amp;nbsp; This role does not have a variable compensation component.&amp;nbsp;&amp;nbsp;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;The typical starting salary range for new hires in this role is listed below.&amp;nbsp; In select locations (including Seattle WA, Los Angeles CA, the San Francisco Bay Area CA, and the New York City Metro Area), an alternate range may apply as specified below.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; We may ultimately pay more or less than the posted range, and the ranges may be modified in the future.&amp;nbsp;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;An employee&#39;s position within the salary range will be based on several factors including, but not limited to, relevant education, qualifications, certifications, experience, skills, geographic location, performance, and business or organizational needs.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Elastic believes that employees should have the opportunity to share in the value that we create together for our shareholders. Therefore, in addition to cash compensation, this role is currently eligible to participate in Elastic&#39;s stock program.&amp;nbsp; Our total rewards package also includes a company-matched 401k with dollar-for-dollar matching up to 6% of eligible earnings, along with a range of other benefits offered with a holistic emphasis on employee well-being.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;The typical starting salary range for this role is:&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$94,300&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$149,200 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;The typical starting salary range for this role in the select locations listed above is:&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$113,300&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$179,200 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Additional Information - We Take Care of Our People&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt;As a distributed company, diversity drives our identity. Whether you’re looking to launch a new career or grow an existing one, Elastic is the type of company where you can balance great work with great life. Your age is only a number. It doesn’t matter if you’re just out of college or your children are; we need you for what you can do.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;We strive to have parity of benefits across regions and while regulations differ from place to place, we believe taking care of our people is the right thing to do.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive pay based on the work you do here and not your previous salary&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Health coverage for you and your family in many locations&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Ability to craft your calendar with flexible locations and schedules for many roles&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generous number of vacation days each year&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Increase your impact - We match up to $2000 (or local currency equivalent) for financial donations and service&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Up to 40 hours each year to use toward volunteer projects you love&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Embracing parenthood with minimum of 16 weeks of parental leave&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;Different people approach problems differently. #### 4. AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 6.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 5/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 9/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** United States
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse
- **Apply URL:** https://databricks.com/company/careers/open-positions/job?gh_jid=8335860002

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the GenAI/RAG/LLM stack that Databricks' AI FDE team requires, including hands-on production RAG pipelines, LangChain, LangGraph, multi-agent systems, and cloud deployments on AWS/Azure. However, this is a senior-leaning role at a top-tier company expecting "extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience," and the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with only two short internships and no full-time industry tenure — a significant experience gap. The salary range also partially misaligns, as Databricks' posted range ($180K–$248K) exceeds the candidate's stated upper preference of $200K, though the lower bound overlaps.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Production-grade RAG pipeline experience with LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation — directly matching core job requirements
- Multi-agent system experience via LangGraph 4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing and ATS/LLM self-critique evaluation
- Cloud deployment experience on AWS and Azure, matching the job's requirement for production ML deployments on major cloud platforms
- Strong academic credentials: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specializations in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps — satisfying the graduate degree requirement
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on video-language models, demonstrating ability to engage with cutting-edge AI techniques and communicate technical work externally
- Full-stack engineering capability (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, PostgreSQL, Docker) enabling end-to-end production application ownership
- Computer Vision expertise with YOLOv8, SAHI, and industrial defect detection (91% mAP@50), broadening ML domain coverage
- Redis semantic caching and SSE streaming experience showing production-readiness mindset

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Databricks Intelligence Platform experience (explicitly preferred in job posting — not mentioned anywhere in resume)
- Apache Spark experience for large-scale distributed data processing (key Databricks technology — absent from resume)
- DSPy framework experience (listed alongside LangChain in job requirements — not found)
- HuggingFace ecosystem experience (listed as a key tool — not explicitly mentioned)
- Data science toolchain depth: pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch not listed in skills (common ML/DS tools expected for this role)
- Text2SQL experience (listed as a GenAI application type in requirements — not demonstrated)
- Fine-tuning LLMs experience (listed as a requirement — not evidenced)
- Customer-facing / professional services experience (FDE role requires serving as trusted technical advisor to enterprise customers — no evidence)
- Conference presentation or public thought leadership experience (role expects presenting at events like Data + AI Summit)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the Disanji RAG internship: emphasize the production-grade nature — hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3 in Milvus), FlashRank reranking, RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall), Redis semantic caching, and the 35% precision improvement over baseline. This directly mirrors what Databricks' AI FDE team builds for customers.
- Highlight the LangGraph multi-agent resume generator project as evidence of multi-agent system design skills — specifically the quality-gated routing architecture, dual-layer evaluation (ATS + LLM self-critique), and full-stack production deployment with 80%+ test coverage.
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate ability to engage with cutting-edge research and communicate complex AI concepts — a key expectation for a role that involves presenting at Data + AI Summit and advising enterprise customers.
- Address the Databricks/Spark gap proactively: express genuine enthusiasm for learning the Databricks Intelligence Platform and Apache Spark, and draw parallels to distributed data processing concepts encountered in academic coursework or cloud deployments.
- Emphasize the breadth of the ML stack (Computer Vision with YOLOv8, NLP/LLM with RAG, RL in coursework) to position yourself as a versatile AI engineer capable of serving customers across diverse domains — a key value proposition for the FDE team's 'ensemble' model.
- Frame the MEng at Waterloo (GPA 3.9, specializing in LLM, RL, DevOps, Computer Vision) as evidence of rapid, structured upskilling in the exact domains Databricks cares about, compensating for limited full-time industry tenure.

&lt;p data-pm-slice=&quot;1 1 []&quot;&gt;CSQ127R66&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The AI Forward Deployed Engineering (AI FDE) team is a highly specialized customer-facing AI team at Databricks. We deliver professional services engagements to help our customers build and productionize first-of-its-kind AI applications. We work cross-functionally to shape long-term strategic priorities and initiatives alongside engineering, product, and developer relations, as well as support internal subject matter expert (SME) teams. We view our team as an ensemble: we look for individuals with strong, unique specializations to improve the overall strength of the team. This team is the right fit for you if you love working with customers, teammates, and fueling your curiosity for the latest trends in GenAI, LLMOps, and ML more broadly. This role can be remote.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;2006&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop cutting-edge GenAI solutions, incorporating the latest techniques from our &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/research/mosaic&quot;&gt;Mosaic AI Research&lt;/a&gt; to solve customer problems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own production rollouts of consumer and internally facing GenAI applications.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Serve as a trusted technical advisor to customers across a variety of domains.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Present at conferences such as &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/dataaisummit&quot;&gt;Data + AI Summit&lt;/a&gt;, recognized as a thought leader internally and externally.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally with the product and engineering teams to influence priorities and shape the product roadmap.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;2367&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building GenAI applications, including RAG, multi-agent systems, Text2SQL, fine-tuning, etc., with tools such as HuggingFace, LangChain, and DSPy.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise in deploying production-grade GenAI applications, including evaluation and optimizations.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience, leveraging common machine learning and data science tools (i.e., pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch, etc.).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building production-grade machine learning deployments on AWS, Azure, or GCP.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline (Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Operations Research, etc.) or equivalent practical experience.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience communicating and/or teaching technical concepts to non-technical and technical audiences alike.&lt;/li&gt; #### 5. Senior AI Engineer - APM Experiences at Datadog

- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong AI/ML fundamentals — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph agentic workflows, and LLM evaluation — which align well with the core technical demands of this role. However, the position is explicitly Senior-level requiring 4+ years of backend or real-time ML systems experience, and Ruiqi is a mid-level candidate still completing their Master's degree with only ~6 months of relevant internship experience, creating a significant seniority gap. Additionally, the role is based in New York (hybrid) and Ruiqi is located in Canada, which introduces a location/work-authorization barrier that may be difficult to bridge.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on RAG pipeline experience with LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, and FlashRank reranking — directly relevant to the job's retrieval/indexing requirements
- Built and evaluated LLM/agent systems using RAGAS (0.84 context recall) and ATS keyword scoring, demonstrating fluency with offline evals for AI systems
- LangGraph multi-agent pipeline project (4-agent quality-gated routing) aligns with the job's requirement to build robust agent systems with planning strategies
- Full-stack production deployment experience: FastAPI, Docker, Redis semantic caching, PostgreSQL, WebSocket — shows ability to productionize AI features
- Strong academic credentials: GPA 3.9 MEng at University of Waterloo with specializations in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models, demonstrating applied ML science fundamentals
- Computer Vision experience with YOLOv8 and SAHI pipelines shows breadth in ML techniques beyond LLMs
- Familiarity with streaming architectures (SSE streaming, WebSocket, Redis caching) relevant to low-latency data systems

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Distributed tracing / APM experience (OpenTelemetry, Datadog APM, trace analysis) — core to the role
- Go or Java proficiency — Datadog's primary backend languages; candidate only lists Python, JS, C++
- Observability platform experience (metrics pipelines, logs, SLO/SLA, incident response)
- Microservices performance engineering (latency breakdowns, concurrency, resiliency patterns)
- Production on-call ownership and alerting/monitoring operations at scale
- 4+ years of professional backend or real-time ML systems experience (candidate has ~6 months internship experience)
- Anomaly detection and ranking/recommendation systems experience
- Experiment design and statistical analysis (confidence intervals, A/B testing)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the Disanji RAG system: emphasize the production-grade nature, hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 + Milvus), 35% precision improvement, and RAGAS evaluation pipeline — this directly mirrors the job's LLM/agent eval requirements
- Highlight the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline project with quality-gated routing as evidence of building robust, production-ready agentic systems with planning and guardrail mechanisms
- Connect your ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video-language models to the role's need for strong ML/applied science fundamentals and experience with complex data modalities
- Emphasize your full-stack deployment experience (FastAPI + Docker + Redis + PostgreSQL + WebSocket) to demonstrate you can own the full loop from prototype to production
- Address the seniority gap proactively: frame your academic research, publications, and rapid project delivery as evidence of senior-level technical depth despite fewer years of industry experience
- Express genuine interest in the APM/observability domain and articulate a learning plan for OpenTelemetry and distributed tracing — showing product-mindedness and initiative
- Mention your 80%+ test coverage on the Resume Generator project as evidence of engineering rigor and maintainability focus

Title: Senior AI Engineer - APM Experiences
Company: Datadog
Location: New York, New York, USA
Description: &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The opportunity&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog’s APM Experiences team owns the core product experience for &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.datadoghq.com/tracing/&quot;&gt;Application Performance Monitoring&lt;/a&gt; — including distributed tracing, service representation, and more. We’re building a new wave of AI-powered capabilities that help customers &lt;em&gt;detect, resolve, and prevent&lt;/em&gt; performance issues faster. In this role, you will lead end‑to‑end development of LLM- and Agent‑based features that can:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Debug and investigate application performance issues down to the root cause, as both a developer assistant and a fully autonomous agent&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proactively recommend performance and reliability-based optimizations to prevent the next incident&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Automatically create intelligent monitors and SLOs for the most important business flows and critical paths&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;This is a highly product‑minded engineering role: you’ll work from problem discovery and UX all the way to reliable, scalable production systems.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you’ll do&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Shape AI experiences for APM.&lt;/strong&gt; Design and ship LLM/agentic workflows that analyze traces, metrics, logs, and other telemetry to generate diagnoses, explanations, and guided fixes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Own the full loop.&lt;/strong&gt; Prototype quickly, define success metrics and evals, run experiments, iterate, and ultimately productionize for scale and reliability.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Build robust agent systems.&lt;/strong&gt; Develop tools, retrieval and planning strategies, and guardrails; manage prompts/evals; design fallbacks and human‑in‑the‑loop paths.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Integrate with Datadog’s platform.&lt;/strong&gt; Leverage surfaces like Trace Explorer, Service Catalog, monitors, and workflows to deliver end‑to‑end value in the APM UI.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Partner deeply.&lt;/strong&gt; Collaborate with PM, Design, and partner teams to build cohesive experiences.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Raise the bar on engineering.&lt;/strong&gt; Write performant, maintainable backend code, own services in production, and improve reliability for high‑throughput, low‑latency data systems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who you are&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Product‑minded engineer who ships AI to production&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;4+ years building backend or real-time ML systems; you value simplicity, correctness, and performance&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven experience delivering LLM/agent features to production (prompting, tooling, evals, safety/guardrails)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable owning user journeys, iterating from prototype → alpha → GA, and measuring impact with clear product metrics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Strong ML / applied science fundamentals&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Solid grasp of the ML lifecycle (task definition, dataset collection, modeling, evaluation, deployment, iteration) and statistics (experiment design, confidence intervals)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience choosing/modeling the right technique for the job (e.g., anomaly detection, ranking/recommendation, NLP), and knowing when a heuristic beats a model&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Fluency with offline/online evals for AI systems; can build reliable golden sets and automatic regressions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Distributed systems &amp;amp; observability savvy&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with microservices performance: tracing, latency breakdowns, concurrency, and resiliency patterns&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficient in Go, Java, or Python; strong API/service design; production ops (monitoring, alerting, on‑call rotation)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Nice to have&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands‑on with distributed tracing stacks (OpenTelemetry/Datadog APM), profilers, and logs/metrics pipelines&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Exposure to planning/agent frameworks, tool‑use orchestration, RAG, and retrieval/indexing for observability data&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Familiarity with SLO/SLA practices and incident response&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h4&gt;Benefits and Growth:&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Get to build tools for software engineers, just like yourself. And use the tools we build to accelerate our development.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Have a lot of influence on product direction and impact on the business.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work with skilled, knowledgeable, and kind teammates who are happy to teach and learn.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive global benefits.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous professional development.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;Datadog offers a competitive salary and equity package, and may include variable compensation. Actual compensation is based on factors such as the candidate&#39;s skills, qualifications, and experience. In addition, Datadog offers a wide range of best in class, comprehensive and inclusive employee benefits for this role including healthcare, dental, parental planning, and mental health benefits, a 401(k) plan and match, paid time off, fitness reimbursements, and a discounted employee stock purchase plan.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;The reasonably estimated yearly salary for this role at Datadog is:&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$187,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$240,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. - Full-stack engineering capability (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, Docker, PostgreSQL, WebSocket) enabling end-to-end product development
- Strong Python proficiency with backend systems including Redis caching, streaming APIs, and containerized deployments
- Excellent academic pedigree: MEng at University of Waterloo (3.9 GPA), BASc at UBC (3.7 GPA, Dean's Honor List 3 years)
- Computer Vision experience with YOLOv8 and SAHI pipelines, complementing the ML engineering skill set
- Automated evaluation pipeline experience (RAGAS) aligns with Databricks' emphasis on robust logging and evaluation harnesses

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- TensorFlow/PyTorch — not explicitly listed on resume despite being a core requirement
- LLM fine-tuning and pre-training experience — no evidence of model training from scratch or fine-tuning workflows
- Scalable ML platform/infrastructure experience (e.g., MLflow, Spark, Databricks platform itself)
- Hyperparameter tuning and model evaluation pipelines at production scale
- Text-to-SQL or code generation systems — key Databricks product use cases not evidenced
- Experience at high-velocity, high-growth tech companies (only short internships listed)
- Telemetry and observability systems for ML models in production

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system at Disanji: quantify the 35% retrieval precision improvement and 0.84 RAGAS context recall — these are exactly the kind of LLM quality metrics Databricks cares about for products like Databricks Assistant and AI/BI Genie
- Highlight the ACM MM '23 publication on video-language models to demonstrate research depth equivalent to industry experience, directly addressing the 'strong ML research background as equivalent qualification' clause in the JD
- Emphasize the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project with quality-gated routing and dual-layer evaluation — this maps directly to Databricks' Agent Bricks product and their focus on agentic AI systems
- Address the PyTorch/TensorFlow gap proactively: mention that deep learning coursework and CV projects (YOLOv8, DBVM) were implemented using these frameworks even if not explicitly listed on the resume
- Discuss the 80%+ test coverage and Docker-based deployment practices to demonstrate software engineering rigor that Databricks explicitly values
- Frame the MEng at Waterloo (graduating Oct 2025) as a strength — specializations in RL, LLM, and Computer Vision are directly aligned with Databricks' 2025 roadmap for expanding GenAI capabilities
- Be prepared to discuss willingness to relocate to San Francisco/Mountain View or negotiate a hybrid arrangement, as this is a potential dealbreaker for an otherwise strong technical fit

Title: Sr. Machine Learning Engineer 
Company: Databricks
Location: San Francisco, California

Over the past years, we’ve launched&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-databricks-assistant&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-databricks-assistant&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;Databricks Assistant&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/product/ai-bi/genie&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/product/ai-bi/genie&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;AI/BI Genie&lt;/a&gt;, and&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-agent-bricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-agent-bricks&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;Agent Bricks&amp;nbsp;&lt;/a&gt;working with product teams, and made significant strides in LLM quality for these products. These products are used by 100s of thousands of Databricks users every day. We are tackling challenging problems like code suggestion, error detection and correction, text-to-sql generation, automatic pipeline generation, knowledge QA and many others.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;As our GenAI products continue to evolve, we are seeking multiple &lt;strong&gt;&amp;nbsp;GenAI Engineers from junior levels to more senior levels&lt;/strong&gt; to drive the next phase of development. In 2025, we will focus on enhancing LLM quality, expanding GenAI capabilities across Databricks products, and strengthening our platform architecture to enable seamless AI interactions at scale.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Key Responsibilities&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape the direction of our applied AI areas and intelligence features in our products&lt;strong&gt;. &lt;/strong&gt;Drive the development and deployment of state-of-the-art AI models and systems that directly impact the capabilities and performance of Databricks&#39; products and services (e.g., Databricks Assistant and AI/BI Genie).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop novel data collection, fine-tuning, and LLM technologies that achieve optimal performance on specific tasks and domains.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and implement ML pipelines for data preprocessing, feature engineering, model training, hyperparameter tuning, and model evaluation, enabling rapid experimentation and iteration.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work closely with cross-functional teams, including AI researchers, ML engineers, and product teams, to deliver impactful AI solutions that enhance user productivity and satisfaction.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build scalable, reusable backend systems to support GenAI products across the company. Develop robust logging, telemetry, and evaluation harnesses to ensure reliable model performance.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What We’re Looking For&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;2-8 years of machine learning engineering experience in high-velocity, high-growth companies. Alternatively, a strong background in relevant ML research in academia will be considered as an equivalent qualification.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong track record of working with language modeling technologies. This could include the following: Developing generative and embedding techniques, modern model architectures, fine tuning / pre-training datasets, and evaluation benchmarks.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficiency in Python, TensorFlow/PyTorch, and scalable ML architectures.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Ability to drive end-to-end model development, from research and prototyping to deployment and monitoring.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong analytical and problem-solving skills, with a passion for improving AI-driven user experiences.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong coding and software engineering skills, and familiarity with software engineering principles around testing, code reviews and deployment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with LLM fine-tuning, prompt engineering, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a bonus.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Why Join Us?&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;At Databricks, we are building state-of-the-art AI solutions that redefine how users interact with data and our products. You’ll have the opportunity to shape the future of AI-driven products at Databricks, work with cutting-edge models, and collaborate with a world-class team of AI and ML experts.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;If you&#39;re excited about pushing the boundaries of AI in real-world applications, we’d love to hear from you!&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Please note we are open to employees working from our Mountain View, CA office for this position.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Pay Range Transparency&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-size: 14px;&quot;&gt;Databricks is committed to fair and equitable compensation practices. The pay range(s) for this role is listed below and represents the expected salary range for non-commissionable roles or on-target earnings for commissionable roles.&amp;nbsp; Actual compensation packages are based on several factors that are unique to each candidate, including but not limited to job-related skills, depth of experience, relevant certifications and training, and specific work location. Based on the factors above, Databricks anticipates utilizing the full width of the range. #### 7. Senior Machine Learning Engineer, Community Support Engineering  at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 5.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 6/10 | Salary: 6/10

- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse
- **Apply URL:** https://careers.airbnb.com/positions/7560504?gh_jid=7560504

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the role's AI/ML stack — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain, LangGraph, LLM evaluation, and agentic AI — which are core to Airbnb's CSP ML team. However, the role explicitly requires 5–9 years of ML engineering experience with ownership over large-scale systems, while the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of internship and project experience. Additionally, the position is US-Remote only and requires residency in a US state where Airbnb has a registered entity, while the candidate is currently based in Canada (Waterloo, ON), creating a significant location barrier.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG pipeline experience using LangChain LCEL, BM25 + dense/sparse hybrid retrieval, Milvus, and FlashRank reranking — directly aligned with the team's RAG/Search focus
- Built and evaluated LLM-powered agentic systems using LangGraph (4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing), matching the job's 'big plus' for Agentic AI experience
- Implemented LLM evaluation pipelines using RAGAS (0.84 context recall), aligning with the team's LLM evaluation and testing automation mandate
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models, demonstrating academic depth in AI/ML
- Master's degree in ECE from University of Waterloo with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision — meets the PhD/Master's education requirement
- Full-stack production deployment experience (FastAPI, Docker, Redis, PostgreSQL, WebSocket) enabling end-to-end ML system ownership
- Computer Vision expertise (YOLOv8, SAHI, 91% mAP@50) adds breadth to ML capabilities
- Familiarity with semantic caching (Redis) and streaming (SSE, WebSocket) for scalable ML service delivery

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 5–9 years of ML engineering experience — candidate has ~1–2 years of internship/project experience
- Ownership over large-scale production ML systems at industry scale (e.g., serving millions of users)
- LLM fine-tuning and optimization experience (not evidenced in resume)
- Feedback-based learning systems and guardrail implementation for LLMs
- Cross-functional leadership and collaboration at senior/staff engineer level
- US residency or work authorization in an eligible US state (candidate is currently in Canada)
- Experience with Airbnb-scale data complexity and marketplace ML systems

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 + Milvus), 35% precision improvement, and RAGAS evaluation pipeline — this maps directly to the team's RAG/Search and LLM evaluation mandates
- Highlight the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline project as concrete Agentic AI experience, explaining the quality-gated routing design and how it mirrors production-grade agentic customer support systems
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video-language models to demonstrate research depth and ability to work at the frontier of AI/ML
- Address the experience gap proactively: frame the breadth of end-to-end ownership (from data pipeline to deployment to evaluation) across multiple projects as evidence of senior-level thinking despite fewer years
- Clarify US work authorization and residency status early — this is a hard requirement for the remote-US role and must be resolved before progressing
- Emphasize full-stack ML deployment skills (FastAPI, Docker, Redis, WebSocket, PostgreSQL) to show ability to own ML systems end-to-end without needing additional engineering support
- If applicable, mention any exposure to LLM fine-tuning techniques or plans to develop this skill, as it is a listed team responsibility not currently evidenced on the resume

&lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;&quot;&gt;Airbnb was born in 2007 when two hosts welcomed three guests to their San Francisco home, and has since grown to over 5 million hosts who have welcomed over 2 billion guest arrivals in almost every country across the globe. Every day, hosts offer unique stays and experiences that make it possible for guests to connect with communities in a more authentic way.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Community You Will Join: &lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence are at the heart of the Airbnb product. From Trust to Payments, and from Customer Service to Marketing we rely on ML to ensure that guests and hosts have the best possible experience with Airbnb.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Community Support Products (CSP) Machine Learning team is the core team responsible for driving CSxAI (Customer Support x Artificial Intelligence) initiatives by adopting the Generative AI technologies to enable an intelligent, scalable and exceptional service experience. The team develops and enhances various AI models, ML services and tools including LLM fine-tuning and optimization, RAG/Search,&amp;nbsp; LLM evaluation and testing automation, feedback-based learning and guardrail for a wide range of applications in Airbnb.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The richness of Airbnb&#39;s data, the complexity of its marketplace and the variety innate in our product mean that we need to operate at the state of the art of AI practice. We are committed to investing in long term innovation to solve the complex problems we face, and to do that we need the very best experts in ML and AI to join us.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Difference You Will Make:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;We believe our current customer experiences in these domains are only scratching the surface of the innovations that are possible, and that science is at the heart of delivering a step-function change for our Guest and and Host on Airbnb.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You will build and leverage cutting edge AI technologies to transform Airbnb’s customer service by delivering personalized, easy-to-use and proactive customer service experience.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Many of the initiatives you’ll tackle are in their early conceptual stages. You will have the opportunity to shape these ideas from inception to production, turning visionary concepts into impactful realities.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;A Typical Day:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Envision, champion, and support the development of novel ML systems, product integrations, and performance optimizations to solve real-world problems&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Work cross-functionally with product, design, and other engineering counterparts to design and build efficient AI solutions for Airbnb CS products&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Learn and share the latest AI/ML technologies with the team.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Expertise:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;PhD/Master’s degree, preferably in CS, or equivalent experience&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;5-9 years of ML engineering experience, with ownership responsibility over large-scale software systems&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Background in the design and development of AI and ML systems and services, and a deep passion for building efficient and scalable ML-powered products&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Experience with LLM driven chatbot and Agentic AI products would be a big plus&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Excellent communication skills and the ability to work well within a team and with teams across the engineering, product &amp;amp; design organizations&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Location:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;This position is US - Remote Eligible. The role may include occasional work at an Airbnb office or attendance at offsites, as agreed to with your manager. While the position is Remote Eligible, you must live in a state where Airbnb, Inc. has a registered entity. &lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;https://careers.airbnb.com/&quot;&gt;Click here for the up-to-date list of excluded states. This list is continuously evolving, so please check back with us if the state you live in is on the exclusion list.&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt; If your position is employed by another Airbnb entity, your recruiter will inform you what states you are eligible to work from.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment To Inclusion &amp;amp; Belonging:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;Airbnb is committed to working with the broadest talent pool possible. We believe diverse ideas foster innovation and engagement, and allow us to attract creatively-led people, and to develop the best products, services and solutions. All qualified individuals are encouraged to apply.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We strive to also provide a disability inclusive application and interview process. If you are a candidate with a disability and require reasonable accommodation in order to submit an application, please contact us at: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;mailto:reasonableaccommodations@airbnb.com&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;reasonableaccommodations@airbnb.com&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;. Please include your full name, the role you’re applying for and the accommodation necessary to assist you with the recruiting process.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;We ask that you only reach out to us if you are a candidate whose disability prevents you from being able to complete our online application.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;How We&#39;ll Take Care of You:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p class=&quot;p1&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;Our job titles may span more than one career level. The base pay range is subject to change and may be modified in the future. This role may also be eligible for bonus, equity, benefits, and Employee Travel Credits. &amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$191,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$223,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**

- HuggingFace experience (not explicitly listed, though deep learning work implies some familiarity)
- Data science tooling depth: pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch not explicitly listed (only 'Deep Learning' mentioned broadly)
- Text2SQL experience (listed as a GenAI application type in job posting, not evidenced)
- Fine-tuning LLMs (not explicitly mentioned in resume)
- Customer-facing / consulting experience (FDE role requires serving as trusted technical advisor to enterprise clients)
- Conference speaking / thought leadership (no evidence of public presentations or conference talks)
- Extensive industry experience (only ~5 months of internship experience; role expects senior-level industry tenure)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the Disanji RAG internship: Quantify the impact — 35% retrieval precision improvement, 0.84 RAGAS context recall — and walk through the full architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 hybrid retrieval, FlashRank reranking, Redis semantic caching) to demonstrate production-grade GenAI deployment expertise that directly mirrors Databricks' customer use cases.
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project: Explain the quality-gated routing design and dual-layer evaluation (ATS keyword scoring + LLM self-critique) to showcase multi-agent system design thinking and evaluation rigor — two of the most emphasized skills in the job posting.
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication: Frame the Dual Branches Video Modeling research as evidence of both deep technical expertise and the ability to communicate complex AI concepts to technical audiences, which is critical for the FDE thought-leadership expectation.
- Address the Databricks/Spark gap proactively: Express genuine enthusiasm for learning the Databricks Intelligence Platform and Apache Spark, and draw parallels to your distributed data experience (Milvus vector DB, Kafka in skills, large-scale image pipelines) to show transferable foundations.
- Reframe academic projects as production experience: Emphasize the 80%+ test coverage, WebSocket real-time streaming, and Docker containerization in the Resume Generator project to counter concerns about limited industry tenure — these demonstrate production engineering discipline.
- Discuss the location situation directly: If open to relocation to London or if the role has any remote flexibility, address this upfront. If not, consider whether this role is worth pursuing given the hard location mismatch.

&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) (ALL LEVELS)&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Req ID: CSQ426R186&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Mission&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The AI Forward Deployed Engineering (AI FDE) team is a highly specialised customer-facing AI team at Databricks. We deliver professional services engagements to help our customers build and productionize first-of-its-kind AI applications. We work cross-functionally to shape long-term strategic priorities and initiatives alongside engineering, product, and developer relations, as well as support internal subject matter expert (SME) teams. We view our team as an ensemble: we look for individuals with strong, unique specialisations to improve the overall strength of the team. This team is the right fit for you if you love working with customers, teammates, and fueling your curiosity for the latest trends in GenAI, LLMOps, and ML more broadly.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop cutting-edge GenAI solutions, incorporating the latest techniques from our Mosaic AI research to solve customer problems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own production rollouts of consumer and internally facing GenAI applications&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Serve as a trusted technical advisor to customers across a variety of domains&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Present at conferences such as Data + AI Summit, recognized as a thought leader internally and externally&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally with the product and engineering teams to influence priorities and shape the product roadmap&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building GenAI applications, including RAG, multi-agent systems, Text2SQL, fine-tuning, etc., with tools such as HuggingFace, LangChain, and DSPy&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise in deploying production-grade GenAI applications, including evaluation and optimizations&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience, leveraging common machine learning and data science tools, i.e. pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch, etc.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building production-grade machine learning deployments on AWS, Azure, or GCP&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline (Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Operations Research, etc.) or equivalent practical experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience communicating and/or teaching technical concepts to non-technical and technical audiences alike&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passion for collaboration, life-long learning, and driving business value through AI&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;[Preferred] Experience using the Databricks Intelligence Platform and Apache Spark™ to process large-scale distributed datasets&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees.
