#### 9. AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 6.1/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 5/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 4/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Sydney, Australia
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the AI/GenAI stack required for this role — particularly in RAG systems, LangChain, multi-agent pipelines, and production deployment — which are core to the Databricks AI FDE position. However, the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree (graduating Oct 2025), and the role appears to expect extensive industry experience, customer-facing consulting skills, and familiarity with the Databricks platform and Apache Spark, none of which are evidenced in the resume. The location is a significant mismatch: the job is based in Sydney, Australia, while the candidate prefers remote/US/Canada, and although the posting says "open to remote locations," the Sydney base and APAC customer focus may create practical barriers.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG system experience: built production-grade RAG pipeline with LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation — directly matching core job requirements
- Multi-agent AI experience: architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing and dual-layer evaluation in a personal project
- Production deployment skills: Docker Compose, FastAPI, Redis semantic caching, SSE streaming — demonstrates ability to productionize GenAI applications
- Cloud platform exposure: AWS and Azure listed in skills, aligning with the job's requirement for cloud ML deployments
- Strong academic credentials: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps — exceeds the graduate degree requirement
- Published research: Co-authored paper at ACM MM '23 on video-language models, demonstrating thought leadership potential
- Computer Vision depth: YOLOv8, SAHI pipeline, and multimodal QA experience adds breadth beyond pure LLM work
- Full-stack capability: React/TypeScript, FastAPI, PostgreSQL, WebSocket — enables end-to-end ownership of GenAI applications

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Databricks Intelligence Platform experience (no mention of Databricks, MLflow, Delta Lake, or Lakehouse)
- Apache Spark experience (not mentioned anywhere in resume)
- DSPy framework (listed in job posting alongside LangChain/HuggingFace)
- HuggingFace ecosystem (not explicitly listed despite deep learning background)
- Data science tooling: pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch not mentioned (core ML stack expected)
- Text2SQL experience (listed as a GenAI application type in job requirements)
- Fine-tuning LLMs (not evidenced in resume)
- Customer-facing / consulting experience (FDE role is heavily client-facing)
- Conference speaking or public thought leadership (preferred for this role)
- Large-scale distributed data processing experience

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 dense/sparse in Milvus), FlashRank reranking, RAGAS evaluation pipeline, and the 35% precision improvement — this directly mirrors what Databricks FDE teams build for customers
- Highlight the LangGraph multi-agent project: the 4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing and dual-layer ATS + LLM self-critique evaluation shows architectural thinking beyond basic LLM wrappers
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate ability to communicate complex AI concepts — position it as a foundation for the thought leadership and conference speaking the role requires
- Address the Databricks/Spark gap proactively: express genuine enthusiasm for learning the Databricks platform and draw parallels between your distributed data pipeline experience (5,000+ image processing, Milvus vector DB) and large-scale data processing concepts
- Emphasize the full-stack ownership mindset: from model training (YOLOv8, 91% mAP@50) to deployment (Docker, FastAPI, Redis) to evaluation (RAGAS) — this end-to-end capability is critical for an FDE who must own production rollouts
- Connect the customer-facing gap by referencing cross-functional collaboration in research (Nanjing University co-development) and the practical business framing of your projects (ATS scoring, business value metrics)
- On location: clarify your openness to relocation to Australia or confirm remote work arrangement expectations upfront, as this is a potential dealbreaker that should be addressed early

#### 10. Staff AI Engineer - MCP Services at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 5.5/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 3/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 5/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Portugal, Remote
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong and directly relevant AI/ML skills — particularly in LangChain, LangGraph, RAG pipelines, agentic systems, and evaluation frameworks — which align well with the technical core of this role. However, the position is a Staff-level (senior) engineering role requiring demonstrated leadership, autonomy in high-ambiguity environments, and deep production-scale agentic systems experience, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with limited professional tenure. The location is also a mismatch: the role is based in Portugal (remote within Portugal), while the candidate prefers US/Canada remote, which may create eligibility barriers.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on LangChain and LangGraph experience directly matching the job's LLM orchestration framework requirements
- Built production-grade RAG pipelines with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + dense vectors), reranking, and streaming — highly relevant to MCP Services context
- Developed automated evaluation pipelines using RAGAS, directly aligning with the role's requirement to build evaluation frameworks for agentic systems
- Multi-agent pipeline architecture experience (4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing) maps to agentic tool surface design
- Strong full-stack engineering skills (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, Docker, PostgreSQL, WebSocket) supporting production deployment needs
- Published research in multimodal AI (ACM MM '23), demonstrating depth in applied AI
- High academic credentials: 3.9 GPA MEng at University of Waterloo with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Experience with semantic caching (Redis), vector databases (ChromaDB, Milvus), and embeddings (BGE-M3) relevant to agent-tool infrastructure

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Staff/senior-level engineering leadership experience — no evidence of leading teams, defining technical direction, or mentoring engineers
- MCP (Model Context Protocol) standard knowledge or experience building MCP-compatible tool surfaces
- Experience with observability/monitoring platforms (Datadog, metrics, logs, incident triage workflows)
- ReAct agentic loop design and evaluation experience not explicitly demonstrated
- Experience operating in large-scale production SaaS environments with cross-team alignment responsibilities
- Autonomous prioritization and direction-setting in high-ambiguity, fast-changing organizational contexts

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Emphasize the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: highlight the 35% retrieval precision improvement, RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall), and the full deployment stack — this directly mirrors Datadog's need for evaluation frameworks for agentic systems
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing in the resume generator project as concrete evidence of multi-agent orchestration and tool-use system design
- Discuss the dual-layer evaluation framework (ATS keyword scoring + LLM self-critique) as a demonstration of metrics design and data instrumentation for AI systems
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video understanding to establish research credibility and depth in applied AI
- Proactively address the Staff-level gap by framing your trajectory: strong academic foundation (3.9 GPA, MEng at Waterloo), rapid skill acquisition, and production deployments despite early career stage — position yourself as a high-growth candidate
- Express genuine interest in the MCP standard and agentic tool surfaces — mention willingness to quickly ramp on MCP protocol specifics given your existing LangChain/LangGraph foundation
- Address the location/work authorization question directly and early — clarify your eligibility to work remotely for a Portugal-based role if applicable

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Staff AI Engineer - MCP Services
Company: Datadog
Location: Portugal, Remote
Description: &lt;p&gt;Datadog’s MCP team is scaling the capabilities of our MCP Services — an interface that enables external agents like Claude and Cursor, as well as internal Datadog AI Agents, to interact with Datadog data. As a Staff Engineer, you’ll play a critical role in evolving this agent-friendly tool surface, enabling agentic workflows across metrics, logs, and incidents etc. You&#39;ll drive forward advanced evaluation frameworks for agentic systems and contribute to the next generation of agent-tool interaction models. This is a fast-evolving space with deep ambiguity and high impact — ideal for someone eager to help shape Datadog’s applied AI future.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead efforts to improve Datadog’s public-facing MCP server, enabling intelligent agents to discover and interact with our services&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and implement agentic tool surfaces tailored for evaluation and production use across a wide variety of AI agents&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build and maintain advanced evaluation pipelines for measuring agent performance on Datadog workflows (e.g., investigations, incident triage, metric queries)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Investigate and resolve failure cases by analyzing tool output, improving query parsing, and enhancing agent feedback mechanisms&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate across Applied AI and internal teams to align on shared standards for tool integration and data access&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experienced Staff-level engineer with a strong background in applied AI, agentic programming, and/or the following: LLM-powered automation pipelines, LLM orchestration frameworks (e.g., LangChain, LangGraph, CrewAI), Agent orchestration and tool-use systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable working in high ambiguity and fast-changing environments; able to define and prioritize direction autonomously&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Skilled in building evaluation frameworks for LLM agents or AI systems, including metrics design and data instrumentation&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Bonus: Experience working with the MCP standard or contributing to agent-compatible tooling surfaces, familiarity with building and evaluating ReAct agentic loops&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong systems thinking, able to reason across multiple agents, tools, and user scenarios&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passionate about pushing boundaries in agent-augmented software and eager to shape evolving interfaces&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Bonus: you’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generous and competitive benefits package&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous career development and pathing opportunities&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Employee-focused best in class onboarding&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Internal mentor and cross-departmental buddy program&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Friendly and inclusive workplace culture&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Remote&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. #### 11. AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 6.1/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 5/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 4/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Melbourne, Australia
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong alignment with the technical AI/GenAI skill set required for this Databricks AI FDE role — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain, LangGraph, multi-agent systems, and production deployment — which are core to the position. However, the role explicitly targets Melbourne, Australia, while the candidate is based in Canada and prefers remote/US/Canada locations; although the posting notes "open to remote locations," the geographic and timezone mismatch with an Australia-anchored team is a meaningful risk. Additionally, the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with limited industry experience (two short internships), whereas this customer-facing FDE role at Databricks typically expects extensive hands-on industry experience and strong client communication skills.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG pipeline expertise: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), reranking, and RAGAS evaluation achieving 0.84 context recall
- Hands-on LangChain and LangGraph experience across both internship and personal projects
- Multi-agent system design: architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing
- Production deployment experience with Docker, FastAPI, Redis, and streaming (SSE/WebSocket)
- Graduate-level education (MEng, GPA 3.9) from University of Waterloo in a quantitative discipline with AI/ML specialization
- Cloud platform exposure on both AWS and Azure
- Published academic research (ACM MM '23) demonstrating ability to communicate technical concepts
- Computer Vision experience with YOLOv8, SAHI, and industrial defect detection (91% mAP@50)
- Full-stack capability (React/TypeScript, PostgreSQL, Node.js) enabling end-to-end AI application ownership
- Automated evaluation pipeline experience (RAGAS) aligning with the job's emphasis on GenAI evaluation and optimization

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Databricks Intelligence Platform experience (no mention of Databricks, MLflow, Delta Lake, or Apache Spark)
- Apache Spark / large-scale distributed data processing
- DSPy framework (listed as a preferred tool in the job posting)
- HuggingFace ecosystem (fine-tuning, model hub, Transformers library — not explicitly mentioned)
- Text2SQL experience
- Fine-tuning LLMs (no evidence of fine-tuning workflows)
- Pandas / scikit-learn / PyTorch (standard ML stack not listed in resume skills)
- Customer-facing consulting or professional services experience
- Conference speaking or thought leadership track record
- Extensive industry data science experience (candidate has only short internships)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (hybrid retrieval, reranking, RAGAS evaluation, Docker deployment) as direct evidence of production-grade GenAI delivery — the core of the FDE role.
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as proof of multi-agent system design capability, and walk through the quality-gated routing architecture to demonstrate depth of GenAI engineering thinking.
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to address the thought leadership and technical communication requirement — frame it as experience translating complex AI research into publishable, audience-facing content.
- Address the Databricks gap proactively: express genuine interest in learning the Databricks platform and draw parallels between your Milvus/vector DB experience and Databricks' Mosaic AI / Vector Search offerings.
- Leverage the remote-open clause in the job posting to address the Melbourne location — emphasize flexibility, async communication skills, and willingness to overlap with APAC time zones if needed.
- Quantify impact wherever possible: 35% retrieval precision improvement, 0.84 context recall, 91% mAP@50 on defect detection — these metrics resonate strongly in a customer-value-driven FDE context.
- Mention AWS and Azure cloud experience to satisfy the cloud deployment requirement, and tie it to production readiness and scalability discussions Databricks customers care about.

Title: AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer)
Company: Databricks
Location: Melbourne, Australia
Description: &lt;p data-pm-slice=&quot;1 1 []&quot;&gt;CSQ227R47&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The AI Forward Deployed Engineering (AI FDE) team is a highly specialized customer-facing AI team at Databricks. We deliver professional services engagements to help our customers build and productionize first-of-its-kind AI applications. We work cross-functionally to shape long-term strategic priorities and initiatives alongside engineering, product, and developer relations, as well as support internal subject matter expert (SME) teams. We view our team as an ensemble: we look for individuals with strong, unique specializations to improve the overall strength of the team. This team is the right fit for you if you love working with customers, teammates, and fueling your curiosity for the latest trends in GenAI, LLMOps, and ML more broadly. Open to remote locations.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop cutting-edge GenAI solutions, incorporating the latest techniques from our &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/research/mosaic&quot;&gt;Mosaic AI Research&lt;/a&gt; to solve customer problems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own production rollouts of consumer and internally facing GenAI applications&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Serve as a trusted technical advisor to customers across a variety of domains&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Present at conferences such as &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/dataaisummit&quot;&gt;Data + AI Summit&lt;/a&gt;, recognized as a thought leader internally and externally&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally with the product and engineering teams to influence priorities and shape the product roadmap&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building GenAI applications, including RAG, multi-agent systems, Text2SQL, fine-tuning, etc., with tools such as HuggingFace, LangChain, and DSPy&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise in deploying production-grade GenAI applications, including evaluation and optimizations&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience, leveraging common machine learning and data science tools, i.e. pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch, etc.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building production-grade machine learning deployments on AWS, Azure, or GCP&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline (Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Operations Research, etc.) or equivalent practical experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience communicating and/or teaching technical concepts to non-technical and technical audiences alike&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passion for collaboration, life-long learning, and driving business value through AI&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;[Preferred] Experience using the Databricks Intelligence Platform and Apache Spark™ to process large-scale distributed datasets&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees. For specific details on the benefits offered in your region, please visit&amp;nbsp;&lt;a href=&quot;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&quot;&gt;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&lt;/a&gt;.&amp;nbsp;&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment to Diversity and Inclusion&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Databricks, we are committed to fostering a diverse and inclusive culture where everyone can excel. #### 12. Staff Machine Learning Engineer  at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 5.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 4/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 5/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** San Francisco, California
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, LLMs, and computer vision — that align well with Databricks' Applied AI team's technical focus. However, this is a Staff-level role requiring 2–8 years of ML engineering experience at high-growth companies, and the candidate is a current master's student (graduating Oct 2025) with only ~5 months of relevant internship experience, creating a significant seniority gap. The role is also office-based in San Francisco/Mountain View, CA, which conflicts with the candidate's remote/hybrid preference and Canadian location.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG pipeline experience: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), reranking, and RAGAS evaluation achieving 0.84 context recall
- Direct LLM engineering experience with LangChain LCEL, LangGraph multi-agent orchestration, and streaming architectures
- Published ML research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models, demonstrating academic research credibility
- Full-stack engineering competency (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, Docker, PostgreSQL, WebSocket) enabling end-to-end AI product development
- Computer vision expertise with YOLOv8, SAHI, and deep learning pipelines (91% mAP@50 on industrial defect detection)
- Strong academic credentials: GPA 3.9 MEng at University of Waterloo, GPA 3.7 BASc at UBC with Dean's Honor List (2022–2024)
- Familiarity with evaluation harnesses and automated testing (RAGAS, ATS scoring, LLM self-critique, 80%+ test coverage)
- Experience with scalable backend infrastructure: Redis semantic caching, Docker Compose, FastAPI + SSE streaming

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- TensorFlow or PyTorch proficiency (not explicitly listed in skills section — critical for the role)
- LLM fine-tuning and pre-training experience (only RAG/prompt engineering demonstrated, no fine-tuning work shown)
- Scalable ML platform/infrastructure experience (Databricks, Spark, MLflow, Delta Lake ecosystem)
- Text-to-SQL or code generation system experience (core Databricks product use cases)
- Hyperparameter tuning and model training pipeline design at scale
- Experience at a high-velocity, high-growth tech company (internships only, no full-time industry tenure)
- Telemetry and production monitoring systems for ML models
- Experience with embedding model development or generative model architecture design

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system at Disanji: quantify the 35% retrieval precision improvement and 0.84 RAGAS context recall as concrete evidence of LLM quality improvement — directly aligned with Databricks' stated 2025 focus on 'enhancing LLM quality'
- Highlight the ACM MM '23 publication on video-language models as proof of ML research depth, which the job posting explicitly accepts as equivalent to industry experience
- Emphasize the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline in the Resume Generator project as evidence of multi-agent GenAI system architecture — directly relevant to Databricks' Agent Bricks product
- Address the PyTorch/TensorFlow gap proactively: if you have used either in coursework (Reinforcement Learning, Deep Learning specializations listed), be ready to discuss specific projects
- Connect your evaluation harness experience (RAGAS, ATS scoring, LLM self-critique) to Databricks' need for 'robust logging, telemetry, and evaluation harnesses for reliable model performance'
- Frame your master's specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision at Waterloo as equivalent to the 'strong background in relevant ML research in academia' qualification path
- Be prepared to discuss your interest in text-to-SQL or code generation (Databricks Assistant use cases) and any relevant coursework or side projects you could pivot to demonstrate this

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Staff Machine Learning Engineer 
Company: Databricks
Location: San Francisco, California
Description: &lt;p&gt;P-1504&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Applied AI team at Databricks sits at the forefront of advancing GenAI-powered products. Over the past years, we’ve launched&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-databricks-assistant&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-databricks-assistant&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;Databricks Assistant&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/product/ai-bi/genie&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/product/ai-bi/genie&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;AI/BI Genie&lt;/a&gt;, and&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-agent-bricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/blog/introducing-agent-bricks&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;Agent Bricks&amp;nbsp;&lt;/a&gt;working with product teams, and made significant strides in LLM quality for these products. These products are used by 100s of thousands of Databricks users every day. We are tackling challenging problems like code suggestion, error detection and correction, text-to-sql generation, automatic pipeline generation, knowledge QA and many others.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;As our GenAI products continue to evolve, we are seeking multiple &lt;strong&gt;&amp;nbsp;GenAI Engineers from junior levels to more senior levels&lt;/strong&gt; to drive the next phase of development. In 2025, we will focus on enhancing LLM quality, expanding GenAI capabilities across Databricks products, and strengthening our platform architecture to enable seamless AI interactions at scale.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Key Responsibilities&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape the direction of our applied AI areas and intelligence features in our products&lt;strong&gt;. &lt;/strong&gt;Drive the development and deployment of state-of-the-art AI models and systems that directly impact the capabilities and performance of Databricks&#39; products and services (e.g., Databricks Assistant and AI/BI Genie).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop novel data collection, fine-tuning, and LLM technologies that achieve optimal performance on specific tasks and domains.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and implement ML pipelines for data preprocessing, feature engineering, model training, hyperparameter tuning, and model evaluation, enabling rapid experimentation and iteration.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work closely with cross-functional teams, including AI researchers, ML engineers, and product teams, to deliver impactful AI solutions that enhance user productivity and satisfaction.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build scalable, reusable backend systems to support GenAI products across the company. Develop robust logging, telemetry, and evaluation harnesses to ensure reliable model performance.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What We’re Looking For&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;2-8 years of machine learning engineering experience in high-velocity, high-growth companies. Alternatively, a strong background in relevant ML research in academia will be considered as an equivalent qualification.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong track record of working with language modeling technologies. This could include the following: Developing generative and embedding techniques, modern model architectures, fine tuning / pre-training datasets, and evaluation benchmarks.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficiency in Python, TensorFlow/PyTorch, and scalable ML architectures.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Ability to drive end-to-end model development, from research and prototyping to deployment and monitoring.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong analytical and problem-solving skills, with a passion for improving AI-driven user experiences.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong coding and software engineering skills, and familiarity with software engineering principles around testing, code reviews and deployment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with LLM fine-tuning, prompt engineering, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a bonus.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Why Join Us?&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;At Databricks, we are building state-of-the-art AI solutions that redefine how users interact with data and our products. You’ll have the opportunity to shape the future of AI-driven products at Databricks, work with cutting-edge models, and collaborate with a world-class team of AI and ML experts.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;If you&#39;re excited about pushing the boundaries of AI in real-world applications, we’d love to hear from you!&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Please note we are open to employees working from our Mountain View, CA office for this position.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Pay Range Transparency&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-size: 14px;&quot;&gt;Databricks is committed to fair and equitable compensation practices. The pay range(s) for this role is listed below and represents the expected salary range for non-commissionable roles or on-target earnings for commissionable roles.&amp;nbsp; Actual compensation packages are based on several factors that are unique to each candidate, including but not limited to job-related skills, depth of experience, relevant certifications and training, and specific work location. Based on the factors above, Databricks anticipates utilizing the full width of the range. The total compensation package for this position may also include eligibility for annual performance bonus, equity, and the benefits listed above. For more information regarding which range your location is in visit our page &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/sites/default/files/2024-08/us-pay-zone-mapping.pdf&quot;&gt;here&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Local Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$190,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$285,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees. #### 13. Senior Machine Learning Engineer, Listing and Host Tools Data and AI at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 5.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 3/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 7/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Remote-USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse
- **Apply URL:** https://careers.airbnb.com/positions/7623458?gh_jid=7623458

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG systems, LLMs, LangChain, Computer Vision (YOLO), and full-stack deployment — that align well with the technical flavor of this role. However, the position is explicitly a *Senior* ML Engineer role requiring 5+ years of industry experience, and the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with only ~4 months of relevant industry internship experience, creating a significant seniority gap. Additionally, several key enterprise-scale technologies required by Airbnb (TensorFlow/PyTorch, Spark, Airflow, Kubernetes, Hive/data warehouse) are absent from the resume, and there is no evidence of A/B testing, large-scale ML infrastructure, or productionized ML models at the scale Airbnb operates.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG and LLM engineering experience — built production-grade RAG pipeline with hybrid retrieval, reranking, and streaming at Disanji Technology
- Dual NLP + Computer Vision background satisfying a key 'must have' requirement — LangChain/LLM work covers NLP, YOLOv8 work covers CV
- Published ML research at ACM MM '23 (multimodal video QA), demonstrating academic depth in applied ML
- Hands-on experience with end-to-end ML pipelines including data ingestion, model training, evaluation (RAGAS), and deployment via Docker/FastAPI
- Strong programming skills across Python, C++, Java, JavaScript — matching the required language stack
- Kafka listed in skills, satisfying one of the 3+ required technologies from the job's tech stack list
- High academic pedigree — UBC (BEng, 3.7 GPA, Dean's Honor List x3) and University of Waterloo MEng (3.9 GPA) in ECE with ML/AI specialization
- Full-stack deployment experience (FastAPI, React, PostgreSQL, Docker, WebSocket) supports the API/service-building responsibilities in the JD
- LangGraph multi-agent pipeline project demonstrates advanced agentic AI architecture skills

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- TensorFlow or PyTorch (neither explicitly listed on resume — critical gap for an ML engineering role)
- Apache Spark (large-scale data processing — not mentioned)
- Apache Airflow or equivalent workflow orchestration (not mentioned)
- Kubernetes (container orchestration at scale — not mentioned)
- Data warehouse experience (Hive, Snowflake, BigQuery, etc. — not mentioned)
- A/B testing methodology and experimentation frameworks
- Scala (preferred language in JD — not present)
- Large-scale ML model productionization and serving infrastructure (e.g., feature stores, model registries, serving skew minimization)
- Personalization and recommendation systems experience
- Anomaly detection domain experience
- Gradient boosted trees / classical ML algorithms (XGBoost, LightGBM — not evidenced)
- 5+ years of industry experience (candidate has ~4 months of internship experience)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (hybrid retrieval, reranking, streaming, Redis caching, Docker deployment) as evidence of ML productionization ability — this directly maps to the JD's 'develop, productionize, and operate ML models and pipelines' requirement
- Highlight the dual NLP + CV background explicitly since it is a 'must have': connect LangChain/LLM work to NLP and YOLOv8/SAHI/DBVM work to CV, and mention the ACM MM '23 publication as proof of research-grade multimodal understanding
- Discuss the RAGAS automated evaluation pipeline as a proxy for ML best practices (model evaluation, quality metrics) and draw parallels to A/B testing and model selection discipline that Airbnb requires
- Emphasize the 80%+ test coverage in the AI Agent project to demonstrate TDD awareness, and discuss how you would extend this to ML model testing and serving validation
- Address the seniority gap proactively: frame the MEng at Waterloo (3.9 GPA) as accelerated applied ML training, and position the combination of academic depth + industry internships + independent projects as equivalent to broader industry exposure
- If you have any experience with PyTorch or TensorFlow (even in coursework or projects), add it to the resume immediately — this is a critical gap for the '3+ technologies' requirement
- Prepare to discuss Kafka (already listed) in depth as your strongest match from the required tech stack list, and be ready to speak to your ability to quickly ramp on Spark/Airflow given your data pipeline experience

&lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;&quot;&gt;Airbnb was born in 2007 when two hosts welcomed three guests to their San Francisco home, and has since grown to over 5 million hosts who have welcomed over 2 billion guest arrivals in almost every country across the globe. Every day, hosts offer unique stays and experiences that make it possible for guests to connect with communities in a more authentic way.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Community You Will Join: &lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Airbnb is powered by our Host and Guest communities. Our teams work on everything from driving improvements for the end-to-end Host and Guest experience to launching new product verticals from the ground up. Our job is to build world-class, comprehensive platforms and features to create high-quality experiences for our outstanding community of Hosts and Guests.&lt;br&gt;Listings and Host Tools Data and AI (LHT-DnA) team is one of the 4 teams of the Marketplace-DnA org: The LHT-DnA team supports host personalization products and provides data driven solutions to achieve superior host experience on Airbnb. These products include but are not limited to managing your space (MYS), host quality standards etc. We own data pipelines and ML models and will build services for serving that are used in the above areas.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Difference You Will Make:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;There is a huge opportunity to improve the Host and Guest experience by leveraging open source, third party, and home grown ML models. As a senior engineer, you will partner closely with our data science, product partners, and other ML + data engineers on the team to execute on these opportunities in order to improve the Host and Guest product experience on Airbnb.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;A Typical Day:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work with large scale structured and unstructured data, build and continuously improve cutting edge Machine Learning models for Airbnb product, business and operational use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work collaboratively with cross-functional partners including software engineers, product managers, operations and data scientists, identify opportunities for business impact, understand, refine, and prioritize requirements for machine learning models, drive engineering decisions, and quantify impact.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Prototype machine learning use cases for use in the product, and work with stakeholders to iterate on requirements.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop, productionize, and operate Machine Learning models and pipelines at scale, including both batch and real-time use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and build services, API to enable serving LLM solutions driven data to product use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Expertise:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;5+ years of industry experience in applied Machine Learning, inclusive MS or PhD in relevant fields.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Must have experience in both Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong programming (Scala / Python / Java/ C++ or equivalent) and data engineering skills&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Deep understanding of Machine Learning best practices (eg. training/serving skew minimization, A/B test, feature engineering, feature/model selection), algorithms (eg. gradient boosted trees, neural networks/deep learning, optimization, state-of-art NLP and CV algorithms) and domains (eg. natural language processing, computer vision, personalization and recommendation, anomaly detection)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with 3 or more of these technologies: Tensorflow, PyTorch, Kubernetes, Spark, Airflow (or equivalent), Kafka (or equivalent), data warehouse (eg. Hive)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Industry experience building end-to-end Machine Learning infrastructure and/or building and productionizing Machine Learning models, as well as integrating to product use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Exposure to architectural patterns of a large, high-scale software applications (e.g., well-designed APIs, high volume data pipelines, efficient algorithms, models)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with test driven development, familiar with A/B testing, incremental delivery and deployment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Location:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;This position is US - Remote Eligible. The role may include occasional work at an Airbnb office or attendance at offsites, as agreed to with your manager. While the position is Remote Eligible, you must live in a state where Airbnb, Inc. has a registered entity. &lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;https://careers.airbnb.com/&quot;&gt;Click here for the up-to-date list of excluded states. Additionally, the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree, whereas Databricks' FDE team typically expects extensive hands-on industry experience and customer-facing consulting skills that are not yet evident in the resume.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG system experience: built production-grade RAG pipeline with LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation — directly matching core job requirements
- Multi-agent AI architecture: designed a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing and dual-layer evaluation, aligning with the multi-agent systems requirement
- Production deployment experience: deployed via Docker Compose, FastAPI, Redis semantic caching, and SSE streaming, demonstrating production-grade GenAI deployment skills
- Cloud platform exposure: listed AWS and Azure in skills, matching the requirement for ML deployments on major cloud providers
- Graduate-level education in a quantitative discipline: pursuing MEng in ECE at University of Waterloo with specializations in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps
- Published research: co-authored a paper at ACM MM '23 on video-language models, demonstrating academic depth and ability to communicate technical concepts
- Computer Vision expertise: trained YOLOv8 models achieving 91% mAP@50, with SAHI pipeline for drone footage detection
- Full-stack AI application development: FastAPI + React/TypeScript + WebSocket + PostgreSQL + Docker stack with 80%+ test coverage
- Strong GPA: 3.9 MEng and 3.7 BEng with Dean's Honor List recognition, indicating strong quantitative aptitude

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Databricks Intelligence Platform experience (no mention of Databricks, Delta Lake, MLflow, or Lakehouse)
- Apache Spark experience for large-scale distributed data processing
- DSPy framework (listed as a preferred tool alongside LangChain)
- HuggingFace ecosystem (not explicitly mentioned despite deep learning background)
- pandas / scikit-learn / PyTorch explicitly listed (common ML tools expected for data science experience)
- Text2SQL experience (listed as a GenAI application type in the job)
- Fine-tuning LLMs (not demonstrated in resume)
- Customer-facing consulting or professional services experience
- French or German language fluency (preferred for Paris location)
- Conference speaking or thought leadership experience

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (hybrid retrieval, reranking, RAGAS evaluation, 35% precision improvement) as direct evidence of production-grade GenAI deployment expertise
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as proof of multi-agent system design skills, including quality-gated routing and dual-layer ATS + LLM self-critique evaluation
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate ability to communicate complex AI concepts to technical audiences and contribute to the broader AI research community
- Emphasize the breadth of AI/ML specializations (LLM, Computer Vision, Reinforcement Learning, DevOps) from both UBC and Waterloo to show alignment with Databricks' cross-functional, curiosity-driven culture
- Address the Databricks platform gap proactively: express eagerness to learn Databricks, Delta Lake, MLflow, and Apache Spark, and highlight fast ramp-up ability evidenced by quickly adopting LangGraph, RAGAS, and Milvus in projects
- Mention AWS and Azure experience and willingness to deepen cloud ML deployment skills in the context of Databricks' cloud-native platform
- If applicable, mention any French language skills or willingness to relocate/work in European time zones to address the location concern directly

&lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building GenAI applications, including RAG, multi-agent systems, Text2SQL, fine-tuning, etc., with tools such as HuggingFace, LangChain, and DSPy&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise in deploying production-grade GenAI applications, including evaluation and optimizations&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; Description: &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) (ALL LEVELS)&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Req ID: CSQ426R237&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Mission&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The AI Forward Deployed Engineering (AI FDE) team is a highly specialised customer-facing AI team at Databricks. We deliver professional services engagements to help our customers build and productionize first-of-its-kind AI applications. We work cross-functionally to shape long-term strategic priorities and initiatives alongside engineering, product, and developer relations, as well as support internal subject matter expert (SME) teams. We view our team as an ensemble: we look for individuals with strong, unique specialisations to improve the overall strength of the team. This team is the right fit for you if you love working with customers, teammates, and fueling your curiosity for the latest trends in GenAI, LLMOps, and ML more broadly.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;We welcome remote applicants located near our offices. The preferred locations (in priority order) are London (UK), Munich (Germany), Paris (France), and Amsterdam (NL).&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop cutting-edge GenAI solutions, incorporating the latest techniques from our Mosaic AI research to solve customer problems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own production rollouts of consumer and internally facing GenAI applications&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Serve as a trusted technical advisor to customers across a variety of domains&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Present at conferences such as Data + AI Summit, recognized as a thought leader internally and externally&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally with the product and engineering teams to influence priorities and shape the product roadmap&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building GenAI applications, including RAG, multi-agent systems, Text2SQL, fine-tuning, etc., with tools such as HuggingFace, LangChain, and DSPy&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise in deploying production-grade GenAI applications, including evaluation and optimizations&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience, leveraging common machine learning and data science tools, i.e. pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch, etc.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building production-grade machine learning deployments on AWS, Azure, or GCP&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline (Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Operations Research, etc.) or equivalent practical experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience communicating and/or teaching technical concepts to non-technical and technical audiences alike&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passion for collaboration, life-long learning, and driving business value through AI&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;[Preferred] Experience using the Databricks Intelligence Platform and Apache Spark™ to process large-scale distributed datasets&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;We require fluency in English and welcome candidates who also speak French or German&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees. For specific details on the benefits offered in your region, please visit&amp;nbsp;&lt;a href=&quot;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&quot;&gt;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&lt;/a&gt;.&amp;nbsp;&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment to Diversity and Inclusion&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Databricks, we are committed to fostering a diverse and inclusive culture where everyone can excel. We take great care to ensure that our hiring practices are inclusive and meet equal employment opportunity standards. Individuals looking for employment at Databricks are considered without regard to age, color, disability, ethnicity, family or marital status, gender identity or expression, language, national origin, physical and mental ability, political affiliation, race, religion, sexual orientation, socio-economic status, veteran status, and other protected characteristics.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Compliance&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;If access to export-controlled technology or source code is required for performance of job duties, it is within Employer&#39;s discretion whether to apply for a U.S. government license for such positions, and Employer may decline to proceed with an applicant on this basis alone.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

#### 16. AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 5.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 5/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 3/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Munich, Germany
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the AI/GenAI engineering requirements — particularly in RAG systems, LangChain, LangGraph, multi-agent pipelines, and production deployment — which are core to this Databricks FDE role. However, the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree (graduating Oct 2025), with limited industry experience (two short internships), whereas Databricks' FDE role demands "extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience" and customer-facing consulting gravitas. The most significant blocker is location: the candidate prefers remote/US/Canada, while the role is based in Munich, Germany (or London/Paris/Amsterdam), and there is no mention of German or French language skills, which are explicitly preferred.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG system experience: built production-grade RAG pipeline with LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation — directly matching core job requirements
- Multi-agent AI architecture: architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing and dual-layer evaluation, demonstrating hands-on GenAI application building
- Production deployment skills: deployed via Docker Compose, FastAPI, Redis semantic caching, and AWS/Azure cloud platforms
- Strong academic credentials: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps — exceeds the graduate degree requirement
- Published research: co-authored paper at ACM MM '23 on video-language models, showing research depth and ability to communicate technical concepts
- Computer Vision expertise: trained YOLOv8 models achieving 91% mAP@50, built SAHI detection pipelines — adds unique specialization valued by the FDE team ensemble model
- Full-stack capability: React/TypeScript, FastAPI, WebSocket, PostgreSQL — enables end-to-end GenAI application ownership

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Databricks Intelligence Platform experience (explicitly preferred — no mention on resume)
- Apache Spark experience (core Databricks technology — not found)
- Data science tooling depth: pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch not explicitly listed (implied but not demonstrated)
- DSPy framework experience (listed in job requirements alongside LangChain)
- Text2SQL experience (listed as a GenAI application type in requirements)
- Fine-tuning LLMs (mentioned in job requirements — not evidenced on resume)
- German or French language fluency (preferred for Munich/Paris offices)
- Customer-facing consulting or professional services experience
- HuggingFace experience (listed in job requirements — not found on resume)
- Large-scale distributed data processing experience

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (hybrid retrieval, reranking, streaming, RAGAS evaluation), quantified results (35% precision improvement, 0.84 context recall), and production deployment — this directly mirrors what Databricks FDE does for customers.
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as evidence of multi-agent GenAI architecture skills, and explain the quality-gated routing design decision to demonstrate systems thinking.
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate ability to communicate complex AI concepts to technical audiences — a key FDE competency for conference presentations and customer advisory.
- Proactively address the Databricks/Spark gap: express genuine enthusiasm for the Databricks ecosystem and mention any self-study or willingness to rapidly upskill on Spark and the Databricks Intelligence Platform.
- Address the location/relocation question directly and early — clarify whether you are open to relocating to Munich or another European hub, as this is a significant blocker for this specific role.
- Emphasize the breadth of your AI specializations (CV with YOLOv8, LLMs, RL, DevOps) to align with Databricks' FDE team 'ensemble' philosophy of unique, complementary specializations.
- Mention your Dean's Honor List recognition and 3.9 GPA to reinforce academic excellence, and connect your Waterloo MEng coursework in LLM and RL to Databricks' Mosaic AI research focus.

#### 17. Staff AI Engineer - MCP Services at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 5.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 3/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 3/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Lisbon, Portugal
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the AI/ML stack required for this role — particularly in LangChain, LangGraph, RAG pipelines, and LLM evaluation frameworks — which are core to Datadog's MCP Services team. However, this is a Staff-level engineering position requiring senior leadership, autonomous direction-setting, and deep production-scale agentic systems experience, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with limited professional tenure. The location is also a significant mismatch: the role is based in Lisbon, Portugal (hybrid), while the candidate prefers remote work in the US or Canada.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on experience with LangChain and LangGraph — directly matching the job's LLM orchestration framework requirements
- Built production-grade RAG pipelines with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + dense vectors), reranking, and streaming — highly relevant to MCP tool surface work
- Implemented automated evaluation pipelines using RAGAS, aligning with the job's emphasis on building evaluation frameworks for agentic systems
- 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project demonstrates practical agentic workflow design and quality-gated routing
- Full-stack capability (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, PostgreSQL, Docker, WebSocket) supports end-to-end system ownership
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models, showing academic depth in applied AI
- High GPA (3.9 MEng, 3.7 BASc) from strong Canadian engineering programs (UWaterloo, UBC)
- Experience with Redis semantic caching and vector databases (Milvus, ChromaDB) relevant to scalable AI infrastructure

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Staff/senior-level engineering leadership experience (leading teams, defining technical direction autonomously)
- MCP (Model Context Protocol) standard knowledge or experience building MCP-compatible tool surfaces
- Experience with observability/monitoring platforms (Datadog, metrics, logs, incident triage workflows)
- ReAct agentic loop design and evaluation
- Production-scale distributed systems experience at enterprise level
- Cross-functional technical leadership and stakeholder alignment at Staff Engineer scope
- Experience with agent-tool interaction models beyond personal/intern projects

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the RAGAS-based evaluation pipeline from the Disanji internship — this directly maps to Datadog's need for 'advanced evaluation pipelines for measuring agent performance'; quantify the 0.84 context recall and 35% retrieval precision improvement
- Emphasize the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline architecture in the Resume Generator project, specifically the quality-gated routing logic, as evidence of agentic workflow design thinking
- Discuss the hybrid retrieval system (BM25 + BGE-M3 dense/sparse vectors in Milvus) as a demonstration of production-grade AI infrastructure knowledge relevant to Datadog's data access layers
- Frame the ACM MM '23 publication on video-language models as evidence of research-grade applied AI depth and ability to contribute to novel agent-tool interaction models
- Acknowledge the Staff-level gap proactively but position the trajectory: MEng at UWaterloo (3.9 GPA), published research, production RAG deployment, and multi-agent system design all within a compressed timeline
- Express genuine interest in the MCP standard and willingness to ramp quickly — reference existing familiarity with agent-tool interfaces through LangGraph tool nodes as a foundation
- If open to relocation, address the Lisbon location directly; if not, inquire about any remote flexibility given the strong technical fit

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Staff AI Engineer - MCP Services
Company: Datadog
Location: Lisbon, Portugal
Description: &lt;p&gt;Datadog’s MCP team is scaling the capabilities of our MCP Services — an interface that enables external agents like Claude and Cursor, as well as internal Datadog AI Agents, to interact with Datadog data. As a Staff Engineer, you’ll play a critical role in evolving this agent-friendly tool surface, enabling agentic workflows across metrics, logs, and incidents etc. You&#39;ll drive forward advanced evaluation frameworks for agentic systems and contribute to the next generation of agent-tool interaction models. This is a fast-evolving space with deep ambiguity and high impact — ideal for someone eager to help shape Datadog’s applied AI future.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead efforts to improve Datadog’s public-facing MCP server, enabling intelligent agents to discover and interact with our services&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and implement agentic tool surfaces tailored for evaluation and production use across a wide variety of AI agents&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build and maintain advanced evaluation pipelines for measuring agent performance on Datadog workflows (e.g., investigations, incident triage, metric queries)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Investigate and resolve failure cases by analyzing tool output, improving query parsing, and enhancing agent feedback mechanisms&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate across Applied AI and internal teams to align on shared standards for tool integration and data access&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experienced Staff-level engineer with a strong background in applied AI, agentic programming, and/or the following: LLM-powered automation pipelines, LLM orchestration frameworks (e.g., LangChain, LangGraph, CrewAI), Agent orchestration and tool-use systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable working in high ambiguity and fast-changing environments; able to define and prioritize direction autonomously&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Skilled in building evaluation frameworks for LLM agents or AI systems, including metrics design and data instrumentation&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Bonus: Experience working with the MCP standard or contributing to agent-compatible tooling surfaces, familiarity with building and evaluating ReAct agentic loops&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong systems thinking, able to reason across multiple agents, tools, and user scenarios&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passionate about pushing boundaries in agent-augmented software and eager to shape evolving interfaces&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generous and competitive benefits package&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous career development and pathing opportunities&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Employee-focused best in class onboarding&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Internal mentor and cross-departmental buddy program&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Friendly and inclusive workplace culture&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. Learn more about #DatadogLife on &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.instagram.com/datadoghq/?hl=en&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Instagram&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/datadog&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;LinkedIn,&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.datadoghq.com/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Datadog Learning Center. &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;215&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Equal Opportunity at Datadog:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog is proud to offer &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.eeoc.gov/sites/default/files/2023-06/22-088_EEOC_KnowYourRights6.12ScreenRdr.pdf&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;equal employment opportunity&lt;/a&gt; to everyone regardless of race, color, ancestry, religion, sex, national origin, sexual orientation, age, citizenship, marital status, disability, gender identity, veteran status, and other characteristics protected by law. We also consider qualified applicants regardless of criminal histories, consistent with legal requirements. Title: Staff AI Engineer - MCP Services

As a Staff Engineer, you’ll play a critical role in evolving this agent-friendly tool surface, enabling agentic workflows across metrics, logs, and incidents etc. You&#39;ll drive forward advanced evaluation frameworks for agentic systems and contribute to the next generation of agent-tool interaction models. This is a fast-evolving space with deep ambiguity and high impact — ideal for someone eager to help shape Datadog’s applied AI future.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead efforts to improve Datadog’s public-facing MCP server, enabling intelligent agents to discover and interact with our services&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and implement agentic tool surfaces tailored for evaluation and production use across a wide variety of AI agents&lt;/li&gt; - Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline in the Resume Generator project as evidence of agentic orchestration design, quality-gated routing, and multi-agent coordination — core to Stripe Assistant's architecture
