<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

&lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who we are&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Stripe&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone’s reach while doing the most important work of your career.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About the team&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Stripe Assistant team is transforming how users interact with Stripe by building an intelligent and proactive assistant that not only answers users’ queries but efficiently resolves issues and provides valuable business insights. We leverage LLMs and agentic systems to elevate the user experience across Stripe—from the dashboard to support surfaces—and we enable other teams to build and integrate their AI agents on our platform. We’re evolving from a helpful support tool to a trusted pilot that anticipates, optimizes, and executes on behalf of our users.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you’ll do&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt;As a Senior Machine Learning Engineer on the Stripe Assistant team, you’ll own the end-to-end ML and agent architecture that makes Stripe Assistant safe, reliable, and deeply useful. You’ll set the strategy for how the Assistant executes high-trust actions, delivers accurate analytical answers across Stripe and the broader web, orchestrates capabilities across many tools and agents, and grounds responses in authoritative Stripe and user data—so users can resolve issues quickly and confidently.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You’ll drive conversation continuity and personalization across surfaces, evolve the Assistant into a proactive partner that anticipates user needs, and deepen its presence in the dashboard to streamline critical workflows. You’ll establish rigorous evaluation and SLOs and deliver step‑change improvements in quality, latency, cost, and availability—paving the way for configurable levels of autonomy and, ultimately, a dependable operating layer over a merchant’s Stripe account.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Responsibilities&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;Our team operates fluidly and here are some problems you may tackle:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Establish trustworthy, human-in-the-loop execution for high-trust “write” actions—prioritizing user control, transparency, accountability, and auditability so customers can delegate with confidence.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Define and evolve the Assistant’s capability and governance model across hundreds of tools and agents, balancing power, permissions, and consistency at scale.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Raise answer quality and usefulness by grounding in authoritative Stripe knowledge and live user data, building cross-surface memory and personalization, and making the Assistant proactive and present in the dashboard.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Explore and apply optimal machine learning methods to improve Stripe Assistant’s overall performance, including but not limited to fine-tuning LLMs with RLHF, synthetic data generation, optimizing RAG pipelines via domain‑specific embedding and retriever fine‑tuning, and automatic prompt tuning, etc.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Make quality and reliability a product: set and meet SLOs, build rigorous evaluation and benchmarking loops, and drive sustained improvements in latency, cost, and availability.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead as a tech lead: mentor and grow engineers, uphold high bars for code quality, security, observability, and operational rigor, and align cross‑functionally to ship safely and fast.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h2&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who you are&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt;We’re looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Minimum requirements&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;5+ years in AI/ML and backend engineering.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Applied LLM experience: RAG/embeddings, tool use/function calling, agentic planning/orchestration, fine-tuning, code generation, evaluations, etc.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficient in Python (Ruby is a plus); strong distributed systems fundamentals.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience working closely with product management, design, other engineers, and other cross-functional partners.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Preferred qualifications&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience operating ML systems at global scale with stringent SLOs—balancing reliability, latency, and cost—with privacy, security, and compliance by design.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building products where AI/ML is core; as well as balancing short-term product priorities with long-term AI/ML improvements.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Track record building ML platforms, especially those that enable multiple teams to collaborate together.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong technical leadership and communication: mentoring and elevating engineers, elevating AI/ML awareness and posture within organizations, setting architectural direction, and driving alignment in ambiguity.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;Join us to build a trustworthy, proactive AI operating layer for every Stripe merchant—advancing safety, reliability, and insight at global scale. If you’re ready to help take Stripe Assistant from copilot to full autopilot and shape how businesses connect with Stripe, we’d love to hear from you.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;

</details>

Description: &lt;p&gt;At Datadog, we’re on a mission to bring security and observability together for modern organizations. &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/blog/bits-ai-security-analyst/&quot;&gt;Bits AI for Security&lt;/a&gt; is our AI agent that leverages LLMs to help analysts investigate alerts, understand threats, and respond faster. We’re looking for a product-minded AI Engineer to help us build the next generation of AI-driven security capabilities.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work closely with users, security experts, PMs, and data scientists on cutting edge AI agents that drive real value for our customers&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop tools, retrieval and planning strategies, and guardrails; manage prompts/evals; design fallbacks and human‑in‑the‑loop paths&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate with Product, Design, and partner teams to build cohesive experiences&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Prototype and see what works well, iterate closely with our users and experts, and then ship&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Follow the latest research on GenAI and AI agents&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have a BS/MS/PhD in Computer Science, Engineering, Artificial Intelligence or a related scientific field or equivalent experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re a backend engineer with at least 4 years of professional experience (Go, Python, Java, etc.) who values code simplicity and performance&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have relevant work experience with LLMs, large-scale systems, machine learning, or adjacent fields. You’ve shipped software that uses LLMs to production&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You want to work in a fast, high-growth startup environment that values its engineers&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You are a good communicator and can drive hard technical conversations towards a pragmatic solution&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Bonus Point:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Excited to work in the security domain, with an understanding of how analysts investigate threats and alerts&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Fluency with offline/online evals for AI systems; can build reliable golden sets and automatic regressions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience scaling products in a high-growth SaaS environment&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan (ESPP)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous professional development, product training, and career pathing&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Intradepartmental mentor and buddy program for in-house networking&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;An inclusive company culture, ability to join our Community Guilds (Datadog employee resource groups)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Access to Inclusion Talks, our Internal panel discussions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Free, global mental health benefits for employees and dependents age 6+&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive global benefits&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. Learn more about #DatadogLife on &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.instagram.com/datadoghq/?hl=en&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Instagram&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/datadog&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;LinkedIn,&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.datadoghq.com/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Datadog Learning Center. &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;215&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Equal Opportunity at Datadog:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog is proud to offer &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.eeoc.gov/sites/default/files/2023-06/22-088_EEOC_KnowYourRights6.12ScreenRdr.pdf&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;equal employment opportunity&lt;/a&gt; to everyone regardless of race, color, ancestry, religion, sex, national origin, sexual orientation, age, citizenship, marital status, disability, gender identity, veteran status, and other characteristics protected by law. We also consider qualified applicants regardless of criminal histories, consistent with legal requirements. Here are our &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/candidate-legal-notices/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Candidate Legal Notices&lt;/a&gt; for your reference.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog endeavors to make our Careers Page accessible to all users. If you would like to contact us regarding the accessibility of our website or need assistance completing the application process, please complete &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSeoJeduEZ2NdZF_65uZmWUrCM-zee7yADXX1s6Xr9zAGq8iKA/viewform&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;this form&lt;/a&gt;. This form is for accommodation requests only and cannot be used to inquire about the status of applications.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;662&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Privacy and AI Guidelines:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;662&quot;&gt;Any information you submit to Datadog as part of your application will be processed in accordance with Datadog’s&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/applicant-candidate-privacy/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/applicant-candidate-privacy/&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;Applicant and Candidate Privacy Notice&lt;/a&gt;. For information on our AI policy, please visit &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/interviewing-at-datadog-ai-guidelines/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Interviewing at Datadog AI Guidelines&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

#### 21. Senior AI Engineer - APM Features at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 5.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 8/10 | Experience: 4/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 3/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Paris, France
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the AI/LLM engineering requirements — hands-on RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph agentic workflows, evaluation frameworks (RAGAS), and production deployments are all directly relevant to what Datadog's APM Features team is building. However, the role is explicitly "Senior" and Paris-based (hybrid), while the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree and strongly prefers remote work in the US/Canada — these are significant mismatches on seniority, location, and work authorization. The candidate lacks observability/APM domain experience (distributed tracing, OpenTelemetry, metrics/logs pipelines) which is central to the role's context.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG pipeline experience: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), reranking, and streaming at Disanji Technology Institute
- LangGraph agentic workflow experience: architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing in the Resume Generator project
- LLM evaluation expertise: built automated evaluation pipelines using RAGAS (0.84 context recall), directly matching the job's requirement for evaluation loops and benchmarks
- Full-stack production deployment: FastAPI + Docker + PostgreSQL + Redis + WebSocket stack with 80%+ test coverage demonstrates ability to own backend services end-to-end
- Published AI research: co-authored paper at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video understanding, showing applied ML fundamentals and scientific rigor
- Computer Vision depth: YOLOv8, SAHI pipeline, and industrial defect detection (91% mAP@50) show strong applied ML breadth
- High academic credentials: 3.9 GPA Master's at University of Waterloo with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- LangChain LCEL proficiency: used in production RAG system with hybrid retrieval and streaming — directly relevant to Datadog's LLM tooling needs

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Observability/APM domain knowledge: no experience with distributed tracing, OpenTelemetry, Datadog APM, or telemetry data (traces, metrics, logs pipelines)
- Senior-level engineering tenure: candidate is mid-level with ~1 year of internship experience; role requires demonstrated senior ownership of production AI systems
- A/B testing and experiment design at production scale: no evidence of structured A/B testing or statistical experiment design in production environments
- SLO/SLA practices and incident response: no mention of reliability engineering, on-call, or incident management experience
- AI coding tool usage (Cursor, Claude Code, etc.): not mentioned in resume — the job explicitly asks for demonstrated use of AI coding assistants
- Distributed systems fundamentals: no explicit experience with Kafka at scale, distributed tracing, or large-scale telemetry indexing beyond listing Kafka in skills
- EMEA work authorization / ability to work in Paris, France: candidate is Canada/US-based with no indication of EU work eligibility

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the Disanji RAG system: emphasize the production-grade nature — hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3 in Milvus), FlashRank reranking, Redis semantic caching, and the 35% precision improvement. This directly mirrors Datadog's need to synthesize noisy telemetry data into actionable insights.
- Highlight the RAGAS evaluation pipeline: Datadog explicitly wants engineers who can design evaluation loops and benchmarks. The 0.84 context recall metric and automated evaluation framework show you already think in terms of measurable AI quality.
- Showcase the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline: the quality-gated routing and dual-layer evaluation (ATS scoring + LLM self-critique) in the Resume Generator project is a concrete example of building agentic workflows with guardrails — a core job requirement.
- Bridge to observability: acknowledge the APM/distributed tracing gap honestly, but connect your experience processing large document corpora and telemetry-like data streams to the domain. Express genuine interest in learning OpenTelemetry and Datadog APM.
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication: this demonstrates you can apply scientific rigor to AI systems — experiment design, evaluation, and publishing results — which aligns with Datadog's emphasis on correctness and evidence-driven iteration.
- Address the seniority gap proactively: frame your rapid progression (Dean's Honor List, 3.9 GPA MEng, published research, production deployments) as evidence of accelerated growth, and express willingness to take on senior responsibilities.
- Discuss location flexibility: if open to relocation to Paris or EU work authorization is possible, address this directly. If not, inquire about any remote exceptions for EMEA roles given the team is in early formation.

Title: Senior AI Engineer - APM Features
Company: Datadog
Location: Paris, France
Description: &lt;p&gt;The APM Features team builds intelligent troubleshooting experiences that help customers quickly understand and resolve performance issues in complex distributed systems. We work at the intersection of observability, product, and AI, transforming large volumes of noisy telemetry data into clear explanations, insights, and actionable conclusions.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;This team is in an early, highly exploratory phase of applying LLMs and agentic workflows to real production APM problems. Engineers collaborate closely to prototype, test, and iterate on ideas, learning from experimentation and focusing on useful, reliable customer outcomes. Correctness, clarity, and product impact are central to how we work.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;As one of the first AI Engineers in EMEA for this team, you’ll help shape how AI engineering is practiced locally, working closely with peers to influence the future of AI-powered troubleshooting at Datadog.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships that it builds, the creativity it brings to the table, and the collaboration of being together. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our employees can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you’ll do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and build AI-powered troubleshooting features for APM workflows using LLMs and agentic systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Help users diagnose and resolve performance issues by synthesizing large volumes of observability data, including traces, metrics, and logs&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Prototype, experiment, and iterate on AI-driven experiences, using evidence and user feedback to guide decisions and focus on real user value&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Define inputs, outputs, and success criteria for LLM-based systems operating in evolving and sometimes ambiguous environments&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build agentic workflows with strong guardrails, balancing autonomy, safety, correctness, and reliability&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead features end-to-end in collaboration with peers and partners, from problem discovery through production and iteration&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and maintain evaluation loops, including offline evaluations, benchmarks, and A/B tests&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Write and own production backend services, contributing to reliable, scalable systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who you are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;A senior, product-minded engineer with experience shipping AI systems to production&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable working in evolving problem spaces and proactively identifying meaningful opportunities to build&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands-on experience with LLMs or agentic systems, including prompting, tooling, evaluation, and guardrails&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience using AI coding tools such as Cursor, Claude Code, or similar, with the ability to reflect on what worked, what didn’t, and why&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;A strong sense for correctness, failure modes, and how to measure and improve quality in AI systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable experimenting, learning from outcomes, and iterating thoughtfully&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Solid ML and applied science fundamentals, including experiment design and statistics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Bonus points:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;These are helpful but not required - we don’t expect candidates to have experience with everything listed below.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Exposure to agent frameworks, tool-use orchestration, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and indexing large-scale telemetry data&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Familiarity with SLO/SLA practices and incident response&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands-on experience with distributed tracing systems (OpenTelemetry, Datadog APM), profilers, or logs and metrics pipelines&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;Distributed systems fundamentals and familiarity with observability concepts.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h4&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan (ESPP)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous professional development, product training, and career pathing&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Intradepartmental mentor and buddy program for in-house networking&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;An inclusive company culture, ability to join our Community Guilds (Datadog employee resource groups)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Access to Inclusion Talks, our Internal panel discussions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Free, global mental health benefits for employees and dependents age 6+&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive global benefits&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. #### 22. Staff Machine Learning Engineer - Community Support Engineering at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 4.7/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 7/10 | Location: 8/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Remote - USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the AI/ML stack required for this role — particularly in RAG systems, LLM pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, and agentic AI — which are directly relevant to Airbnb's CSP ML team. However, this is a Staff-level role requiring 6–9+ years of ML engineering experience with ownership over large-scale systems, and the candidate is a mid-level engineer currently completing a Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of internship/project experience. The experience gap is the dominant mismatch and makes this role a significant reach regardless of technical skill alignment.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on RAG pipeline experience: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), reranking, and RAGAS evaluation — directly mirrors Airbnb's RAG/Search focus
- LLM and Agentic AI experience: architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing, dual-layer evaluation, and ATS scoring — aligns with the 'LLM-driven chatbot and Agentic AI' plus requirement
- Strong academic credentials: Master's in ECE from University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specializations in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision; Bachelor's from UBC with Dean's Honor List recognition
- Full-stack ML deployment skills: FastAPI, Docker, Redis semantic caching, WebSocket, PostgreSQL — demonstrates ability to ship end-to-end ML-powered products
- Published research: ACM MM '23 publication on video-language models shows depth in multimodal AI and academic rigor
- Remote-compatible location: based in Canada with preference for remote/hybrid, and the role is US Remote Eligible (Canada eligibility depends on entity registration)

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 6–9+ years of ML engineering experience (candidate has ~1–2 years of internship/project experience)
- Ownership over large-scale production ML systems at the scale of a major tech platform (millions of users)
- LLM fine-tuning and optimization experience (not evidenced in resume)
- Feedback-based learning systems and guardrail design for production LLMs
- Cross-functional leadership and staff-level technical mentorship/influence
- Experience with enterprise-scale customer support AI or conversational AI products
- Deep expertise in ML infrastructure and scalability (e.g., distributed training, model serving at scale)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end ownership — from hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 + Milvus) to RAGAS evaluation (0.84 context recall) to Docker deployment with Redis caching — this directly mirrors Airbnb's RAG/Search work
- Highlight the LangGraph agentic pipeline project: the 4-agent architecture with quality-gated routing and dual-layer LLM self-critique evaluation maps precisely to Airbnb's CSxAI chatbot and agentic AI initiatives
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research depth and ability to work on novel, early-stage AI problems — Airbnb notes many initiatives are in 'early conceptual stages'
- Acknowledge the Staff-level seniority gap proactively and reframe: position yourself as a high-velocity learner with production AI experience who can grow into the role, and ask about growth trajectories or whether a Senior MLE role might be a better entry point
- Address the US remote eligibility question early: clarify work authorization status and whether you are open to or in the process of obtaining US work authorization, as this is a hard requirement for the role

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Staff Machine Learning Engineer - Community Support Engineering
Company: Airbnb
Location: Remote - USA
Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;&quot;&gt;Airbnb was born in 2007 when two hosts welcomed three guests to their San Francisco home, and has since grown to over 5 million hosts who have welcomed over 2 billion guest arrivals in almost every country across the globe. Every day, hosts offer unique stays and experiences that make it possible for guests to connect with communities in a more authentic way.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Community You Will Join: &lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence are at the heart of the Airbnb product. From Trust to Payments, and from Customer Service to Marketing we rely on ML to ensure that guests and hosts have the best possible experience with Airbnb.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Community Support Products (CSP) Machine Learning team is the core team responsible for driving CSxAI (Customer Support x Artificial Intelligence) initiatives by adopting the Generative AI technologies to enable an intelligent, scalable and exceptional service experience. The team develops and enhances various AI models, ML services and tools including LLM fine-tuning and optimization, RAG/Search,&amp;nbsp; LLM evaluation and testing automation, feedback-based learning and guardrail for a wide range of applications in Airbnb.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The richness of Airbnb&#39;s data, the complexity of its marketplace and the variety innate in our product mean that we need to operate at the state of the art of AI practice. We are committed to investing in long term innovation to solve the complex problems we face, and to do that we need the very best experts in ML and AI to join us.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Difference You Will Make:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;We believe our current customer experiences in these domains are only scratching the surface of the innovations that are possible, and that science is at the heart of delivering a step-function change for our Guest and and Host on Airbnb.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You will build and leverage cutting edge AI technologies to transform Airbnb’s customer service by delivering personalized, easy-to-use and proactive customer service experience.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Many of the initiatives you’ll tackle are in their early conceptual stages. You will have the opportunity to shape these ideas from inception to production, turning visionary concepts into impactful realities.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;A Typical Day:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Envision, champion, and support the development of novel ML systems, product integrations, and performance optimizations to solve real-world problems&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Work cross-functionally with product, design, and other engineering counterparts to design and build efficient AI solutions for Airbnb CS products&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Learn and share the latest AI/ML technologies with the team.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Expertise:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;PhD/Master’s degree, preferably in CS, or equivalent experience&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;6/9+ years of ML engineering experience, with ownership responsibility over large-scale software systems&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Background in the design and development of AI and ML systems and services, and a deep passion for building efficient and scalable ML-powered products&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;p&gt;Experience with LLM driven chatbot and Agentic AI products would be a big plus&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Excellent communication skills and the ability to work well within a team and with teams across the engineering, product &amp;amp; design organizations&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Location:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;This position is US - Remote Eligible. The role may include occasional work at an Airbnb office or attendance at offsites, as agreed to with your manager. While the position is Remote Eligible, you must live in a state where Airbnb, Inc. has a registered entity. &lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;https://careers.airbnb.com/&quot;&gt;Click here for the up-to-date list of excluded states. This list is continuously evolving, so please check back with us if the state you live in is on the exclusion list.&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt; If your position is employed by another Airbnb entity, your recruiter will inform you what states you are eligible to work from.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment To Inclusion &amp;amp; Belonging:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;Airbnb is committed to working with the broadest talent pool possible. We believe diverse ideas foster innovation and engagement, and allow us to attract creatively-led people, and to develop the best products, services and solutions. All qualified individuals are encouraged to apply.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We strive to also provide a disability inclusive application and interview process. If you are a candidate with a disability and require reasonable accommodation in order to submit an application, please contact us at: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;mailto:reasonableaccommodations@airbnb.com&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;reasonableaccommodations@airbnb.com&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;. Please include your full name, the role you’re applying for and the accommodation necessary to assist you with the recruiting process.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;We ask that you only reach out to us if you are a candidate whose disability prevents you from being able to complete our online application.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id=&quot;te-floating-button-container&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;How We&#39;ll Take Care of You:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p class=&quot;p1&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;Our job titles may span more than one career level. The actual base pay is dependent upon many factors, such as: training, transferable skills, work experience, business needs and market demands. The base pay range is subject to change and may be modified in the future. This role may also be eligible for bonus, equity, benefits, and Employee Travel Credits. &amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$204,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$255,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

</details>

#### 23. Senior Applied AI Engineer at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 5.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 4/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 3/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Belgrade, Serbia
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates solid foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG systems, LLMs, LangChain, and computer vision — which partially align with Databricks' Applied AI focus. However, the role is a Senior-level position requiring 2–8 years of production ML engineering experience at scale, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with limited industry tenure (two short internships). The most critical gap is the location: the role is based in Belgrade, Serbia, while the candidate strongly prefers remote/US/Canada positions, and Databricks has not indicated remote eligibility for this posting.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG pipeline engineering experience with LangChain, Milvus, BM25, and BGE-M3 embeddings — directly relevant to Databricks' AI product work
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models, demonstrating academic depth in ML
- Hands-on production deployment experience with Docker, FastAPI, Redis, and PostgreSQL
- Proficiency in Python and modern ML frameworks (LangGraph, ChromaDB, YOLO, deep learning)
- High academic GPA (3.9 MEng, 3.7 BEng) from strong Canadian universities with specializations in CV, RL, and LLMs
- End-to-end ML pipeline experience including data processing, model training, evaluation (RAGAS), and serving
- Software engineering best practices: testing (80%+ coverage), REST APIs, WebSocket, CI/CD-ready Docker setups

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- AutoML systems experience (classification, regression, forecasting, recommendations at scale)
- Classical statistical modeling and forecasting (time-series, probabilistic models beyond deep learning)
- Experience with Databricks platform, Apache Spark, Delta Lake, or MLflow
- Large-scale distributed ML infrastructure (Tier 0 production environments, model serving at scale)
- Mathematical optimization algorithms beyond standard ML libraries
- Experience at high-velocity, high-growth tech companies (not just short internships)
- Model monitoring and observability in production
- Open-source contributions or conference presentations in ML community

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the ACM MM '23 publication on Dual Branches Video Modeling — this demonstrates research-grade ML work and ability to contribute to the broader AI community, which Databricks explicitly values.
- Emphasize the end-to-end RAG system at Disanji: quantify the 35% retrieval precision improvement and 0.84 RAGAS context recall as evidence of production ML engineering with measurable outcomes.
- Discuss the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as evidence of architecting complex, multi-component AI systems with quality-gated routing — analogous to AutoML pipeline design.
- Address the seniority gap proactively: frame the MEng at Waterloo (graduating Oct 2025) as accelerated specialization in LLMs, CV, and RL, and position yourself as targeting a mid-level Applied AI Engineer role rather than the Senior title.
- Acknowledge willingness to relocate or clarify remote work expectations upfront — the Belgrade location is a significant mismatch with stated preferences and must be addressed early.
- Leverage familiarity with vector databases (Milvus, ChromaDB) and hybrid retrieval to connect to Databricks' Vector Search and data intelligence platform capabilities.
- Mention the YOLOv8 industrial defect detection work (91% mAP@50) as evidence of applied CV in real-world, production-adjacent settings beyond academic benchmarks.

Title: Senior Applied AI Engineer
Company: Databricks
Location: Belgrade, Serbia
Description: &lt;p&gt;P-1439&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;As a Senior Applied ML/AI Engineer at Databricks, you will apply machine learning and optimization algorithms to improve the usability and efficiency of the current AutoML and several other user-facing products that will benefit from better classification, regression, forecasting, and recommendations, either classical or based on deep learning. From statistical models all the way down to deep and foundational models, feature augmentation and auto-tuning, our Applied ML/AI team works on some of the most complex, most interesting problems facing businesses, making Databricks&#39; infrastructure and products as performant and cost-efficient as possible. This is a high-impact problem as our customers look at us to deliver the most out of their data.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build features and run end-to-end systems in a small team of experienced engineers and data scientists.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape the direction of our applied ML investment by engaging with engineering and product teams across the company.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Drive the development and deployment of state-of-the-art ML/AI models and systems that directly impact the capabilities and performance of Databricks’ products, infrastructure, and services.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Architect and implement robust, scalable ML infrastructure, including model training and serving components to support seamless integration of AI/ML models into production environments.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work on novel modeling techniques in the field of ML for forecasting.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Possibilities to contribute to the broader AI community by presenting at conferences and actively participating in open source projects, enhancing Databricks’ reputation as an industry leader.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;What we look for:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;2-8 years of machine learning engineering experience in high-velocity, high-growth companies&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong understanding of both computer systems and statistics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience developing AI/ML systems at scale in production&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong track record of ML modeling that goes beyond using standard libraries.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong coding and software engineering skills, and familiarity with software engineering principles around testing, code reviews, and deployment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;A large breadth of knowledge or willingness to develop mathematical modelling beyond the ML.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Nice to have: experience deploying, scaling, and monitoring models in production; understanding of the unique infrastructure challenges posed by training and serving predictions in Tier 0 environments.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Why Join Us?&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Databricks, we are building state-of-the-art AI solutions that redefine how users interact with data and our products. You’ll have the opportunity to shape the future of AI-driven products at Databricks, work with cutting-edge models, and collaborate with a world-class team of AI and ML experts.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;If you&#39;re excited about pushing the boundaries of AI in real-world applications, we’d love to hear from you!&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees. For specific details on the benefits offered in your region, please visit&amp;nbsp;&lt;a href=&quot;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&quot;&gt;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&lt;/a&gt;.&amp;nbsp;&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment to Diversity and Inclusion&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Databricks, we are committed to fostering a diverse and inclusive culture where everyone can excel. We take great care to ensure that our hiring practices are inclusive and meet equal employment opportunity standards. Individuals looking for employment at Databricks are considered without regard to age, color, disability, ethnicity, family or marital status, gender identity or expression, language, national origin, physical and mental ability, political affiliation, race, religion, sexual orientation, socio-economic status, veteran status, and other protected characteristics.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Compliance&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;If access to export-controlled technology or source code is required for performance of job duties, it is within Employer&#39;s discretion whether to apply for a U.S. government license for such positions, and Employer may decline to proceed with an applicant on this basis alone.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

#### 24. Staff AI Engineer - MCP Services at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 4.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 3/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 2/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Madrid, Spain; Paris, France
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, and LLM orchestration — that align well with the technical core of this role. However, this is a Staff-level position requiring senior leadership, autonomous direction-setting, and deep production-scale agentic systems experience, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with limited professional tenure. The location is a critical mismatch: the role is based in Madrid or Paris (hybrid), while the candidate prefers remote work in the US or Canada, making this a near-disqualifying constraint.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on experience building production-grade RAG pipelines using LangChain LCEL, BM25 + dense vector hybrid retrieval, and reranking — directly relevant to MCP's agentic tool surfaces
- Practical use of LangGraph for multi-agent orchestration (4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing) aligns with the role's agentic programming requirements
- Built automated evaluation pipelines using RAGAS (0.84 context recall), demonstrating familiarity with LLM agent evaluation frameworks
- Strong academic background with a 3.9 GPA MEng from University of Waterloo, specializing in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models, showing depth in applied AI
- Full-stack engineering capability (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, PostgreSQL, Docker) supports building end-to-end agentic tool surfaces
- Experience with semantic caching (Redis), streaming (SSE/WebSocket), and containerized deployment (Docker Compose) reflects production-readiness mindset

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- MCP (Model Context Protocol) standard experience — no evidence of familiarity with MCP server design or agent-compatible tooling surfaces
- Staff/senior-level engineering leadership — no evidence of leading cross-functional teams, defining technical direction, or mentoring engineers
- Observability/monitoring domain knowledge — no experience with Datadog, metrics pipelines, log analysis, or incident triage workflows
- ReAct agentic loop design and evaluation — not explicitly mentioned despite being a listed bonus requirement
- Large-scale production systems experience — current experience is intern/research-level, not Staff-level production ownership
- Agent-tool interaction model design at scale — experience is project/prototype level, not enterprise-scale deployment

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the LangChain LCEL RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the production-grade nature (500+ pages, Docker Compose deployment, Redis semantic caching, SSE streaming) and the 35% retrieval precision improvement — this directly mirrors MCP's need for reliable agent-tool interfaces
- Highlight the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing as evidence of agentic orchestration design thinking �� draw parallels to MCP's multi-agent tool surface architecture
- Discuss the RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall) as a concrete example of building LLM evaluation frameworks with measurable metrics — a core responsibility of this role
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research depth and ability to contribute to cutting-edge applied AI, which signals potential for Staff-level technical thought leadership
- Proactively address the seniority gap: frame the breadth of end-to-end ownership (RAG + evaluation + full-stack + deployment) across multiple projects as evidence of punching above mid-level, and express eagerness to grow into Staff-level responsibilities
- Address the location mismatch directly if given the opportunity — clarify openness to relocation or inquire about any remote exceptions, as this is the single largest barrier to candidacy

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Staff AI Engineer - MCP Services
Company: Datadog
Location: Madrid, Spain; Paris, France
Description: &lt;p&gt;Datadog’s MCP team is scaling the capabilities of our MCP Services — an interface that enables external agents like Claude and Cursor, as well as internal Datadog AI Agents, to interact with Datadog data. As a Staff Engineer, you’ll play a critical role in evolving this agent-friendly tool surface, enabling agentic workflows across metrics, logs, and incidents etc. You&#39;ll drive forward advanced evaluation frameworks for agentic systems and contribute to the next generation of agent-tool interaction models. This is a fast-evolving space with deep ambiguity and high impact — ideal for someone eager to help shape Datadog’s applied AI future.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead efforts to improve Datadog’s public-facing MCP server, enabling intelligent agents to discover and interact with our services&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and implement agentic tool surfaces tailored for evaluation and production use across a wide variety of AI agents&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build and maintain advanced evaluation pipelines for measuring agent performance on Datadog workflows (e.g., investigations, incident triage, metric queries)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Investigate and resolve failure cases by analyzing tool output, improving query parsing, and enhancing agent feedback mechanisms&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate across Applied AI and internal teams to align on shared standards for tool integration and data access&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experienced Staff-level engineer with a strong background in applied AI, agentic programming, and/or the following: LLM-powered automation pipelines, LLM orchestration frameworks (e.g., LangChain, LangGraph, CrewAI), Agent orchestration and tool-use systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable working in high ambiguity and fast-changing environments; able to define and prioritize direction autonomously&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Skilled in building evaluation frameworks for LLM agents or AI systems, including metrics design and data instrumentation&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Bonus: Experience working with the MCP standard or contributing to agent-compatible tooling surfaces, familiarity with building and evaluating ReAct agentic loops&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong systems thinking, able to reason across multiple agents, tools, and user scenarios&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passionate about pushing boundaries in agent-augmented software and eager to shape evolving interfaces&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generous and competitive benefits package&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous career development and pathing opportunities&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Employee-focused best in class onboarding&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Internal mentor and cross-departmental buddy program&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Friendly and inclusive workplace culture&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. Learn more about #DatadogLife on &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.instagram.com/datadoghq/?hl=en&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Instagram&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/datadog&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;LinkedIn,&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.datadoghq.com/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Datadog Learning Center. &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;215&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Equal Opportunity at Datadog:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog is proud to offer &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.eeoc.gov/sites/default/files/2023-06/22-088_EEOC_KnowYourRights6.12ScreenRdr.pdf&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;equal employment opportunity&lt;/a&gt; to everyone regardless of race, color, ancestry, religion, sex, national origin, sexual orientation, age, citizenship, marital status, disability, gender identity, veteran status, and other characteristics protected by law. We also consider qualified applicants regardless of criminal histories, consistent with legal requirements. #### 25. Senior Staff Machine Learning Engineer - Guest & Host at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 4.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 5/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 8/10 | Salary: 4/10
- **Location:** United States
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian is a strong early-career candidate with solid AI/ML foundations — particularly in RAG systems, LLMs, and computer vision — but is fundamentally mismatched against this Senior Staff ML Engineer role at Airbnb, which demands 12+ years of industry experience. The candidate is currently completing a Master's degree (graduating Oct 2025) with only two short internships and one research assistantship, placing them at least a decade short of the seniority requirement. While the technical skill overlap is promising for a junior/mid-level AI role, the experience gap and absence of key production ML infrastructure tools (TensorFlow, PyTorch, Kubernetes, Airflow) make this a poor fit for this specific posting. Additionally, the candidate's salary expectation ($100K–$200K) falls well below the job's pay range ($244K–$305K), which is calibrated for a senior staff-level professional.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG and LLM engineering experience — built production-grade RAG pipeline with LangChain, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, and FlashRank reranking
- Hands-on computer vision experience with YOLOv8, SAHI, and multimodal video understanding (published at ACM MM '23)
- Full-stack ML deployment skills: FastAPI, Docker, Redis, PostgreSQL, WebSocket, React/TypeScript
- Relevant AI agent architecture experience with LangGraph multi-agent pipelines
- Strong academic credentials: GPA 3.9 MEng at University of Waterloo, GPA 3.7 BASc at UBC with Dean's Honor List
- Python, Java, C++ proficiency aligns with the job's programming language requirements
- Kafka listed in skills, which is a specific technology mentioned in the job posting
- Remote-eligible position aligns with candidate's preferred remote/hybrid work style
- Located in Canada with US remote eligibility preference — compatible with US-remote role depending on entity registration

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 12+ years of industry ML experience (candidate has ~4 months of internship experience)
- TensorFlow or PyTorch (not listed anywhere in resume)
- Kubernetes (not mentioned)
- Airflow or equivalent orchestration platform (not mentioned)
- A/B testing and experimentation frameworks
- Training/serving skew minimization and production ML best practices at scale
- Gradient boosted trees and classical ML algorithms at production scale
- Experience leading cross-functional (XFN) stakeholder initiatives
- High-scale ML model productionization and maintenance
- Feature engineering and model selection at enterprise scale

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji — emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement and the automated RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall) as evidence of rigorous ML evaluation practices
- Showcase the ACM MM '23 publication on Deep Video Understanding as proof of research-level ML depth and ability to contribute to state-of-the-art model development
- Emphasize the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as evidence of modern AI system architecture skills relevant to Airbnb's AI product ambitions for Guest/Host experiences
- Discuss the end-to-end deployment experience (FastAPI + Docker + Redis + PostgreSQL) to demonstrate ability to productionize ML models, not just prototype them
- Be transparent about career stage and frame the conversation around growth trajectory — strong academic record (3.9 GPA, Dean's Honor List) and rapid skill acquisition in LLMs/RAG could be compelling for a more junior ML role at Airbnb

Title: Senior Staff Machine Learning Engineer - Guest & Host
Company: Airbnb
Location: United States
Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;&quot;&gt;Airbnb was born in 2007 when two hosts welcomed three guests to their San Francisco home, and has since grown to over 5 million hosts who have welcomed over 2 billion guest arrivals in almost every country across the globe. Every day, hosts offer unique stays and experiences that make it possible for guests to connect with communities in a more authentic way.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Community You Will Join:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Users, Listings, and Marketplaces team supports features across the application related to Guests, Hosts and Listings. These products include but are not limited to management tools for listings, enforcement/quality systems, and merchandising systems. The team works closely with product and design to ship highly visible product functionality and drive innovation within the product.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Difference You Will Make:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;There is huge potential for AI to how guests book and how Hosts manage listings at Airbnb. You will improve the Guest and Host experience by improving the intelligence of our product through state of the art machine learning models. This will involve close partnerships and influencing stakeholders across tech, product, and design organizations to identify, scope, and deliver AI features. While you will be leading efforts with XFN stakeholders supported by a team of engineers, you’ll also be working hand in hand with the team to prototype, evaluate, and implement models.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;A Typical day:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Your contributions take a variety of shapes:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Leverage structured and unstructured data to build and continuously improve state of the art Machine Learning models for Airbnb product, business and operational use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work collaboratively with engineers, product managers, data scientists, and operations to identify problems and deploy ML capabilities.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Stay up to date with SoT models and prototype machine learning product features and iterate with product and design.&amp;nbsp;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Drive best practices, develop playbooks, and create a repeatable process for shipping quality machine learning features.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands-on development, productionization, and maintenance of machine learning models and pipelines at scale.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Expertise:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Industry experience building and shipping end to end machine learning features&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;12+ years of industry experience in applied Machine Learning, with experience in both Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;A Bachelor’s, Master’s or PhD in CS/ML or related field&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong programming (Python / Java/ C++ or equivalent) skills with data and backend engineering expertise&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Deep understanding of Machine Learning best practices (eg. training/serving skew minimization, A/B test, feature engineering, feature/model selection) and algorithms (eg. gradient boosted trees, neural networks/deep learning, optimization)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with technologies such as: Tensorflow, PyTorch, Kubernetes, Airflow (or equivalent), Kafka (or equivalent)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise with architectural patterns of a large, high-scale software applications (e.g., well-designed APIs, high volume data pipelines, efficient algorithms, models)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Location:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;This position is US - Remote Eligible. The role may include occasional work at an Airbnb office or attendance at offsites, as agreed to with your manager. While the position is Remote Eligible, you must live in a state where Airbnb, Inc. has a registered entity. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;https://careers.airbnb.com/&quot;&gt;Click here&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt; for the up-to-date list of excluded states. This list is continuously evolving, so please check back with us if the state you live in is on the exclusion list. If your position is employed by another Airbnb entity, your recruiter will inform you what states you are eligible to work from.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment To Inclusion &amp;amp; Belonging:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;Airbnb is committed to working with the broadest talent pool possible. We believe diverse ideas foster innovation and engagement, and allow us to attract creatively-led people, and to develop the best products, services and solutions. All qualified individuals are encouraged to apply.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We strive to also provide a disability inclusive application and interview process. Please include your full name, the role you’re applying for and the accommodation necessary to assist you with the recruiting process.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;We ask that you only reach out to us if you are a candidate whose disability prevents you from being able to complete our online application.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;How We&#39;ll Take Care of You:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p class=&quot;p1&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;Our job titles may span more than one career level. This role may also be eligible for bonus, equity, benefits, and Employee Travel Credits. &amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$244,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$305,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

#### 26. Senior AI Engineer - APM Experiences at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 5.5/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 4/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 3/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Paris, France
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong AI/ML fundamentals with hands-on LLM, RAG, and agentic pipeline experience that aligns well with the technical core of this role. However, the position is a Senior-level role requiring 4+ years of backend/ML systems experience, and the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with limited professional tenure. The location is a critical mismatch — the role is based in Paris, France (hybrid), while the candidate prefers remote work in the US or Canada, creating a significant barrier.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG pipeline experience: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), reranking, and RAGAS evaluation — directly relevant to the role's retrieval/indexing requirements
- LangChain and LangGraph expertise: architected multi-agent pipelines with quality-gated routing, matching the job's agentic workflow requirements
- Full-stack AI delivery: demonstrated ability to ship end-to-end AI features (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, Docker, PostgreSQL) from prototype to production
- Evaluation pipeline experience: built automated RAGAS evaluation achieving 0.84 context recall and dual-layer ATS/LLM self-critique evaluation — aligns with the job's emphasis on evals and success metrics
- Computer Vision and Deep Learning background: YOLOv8, SAHI, multimodal QA (DBVM) — shows breadth of ML applied science fundamentals
- Academic publication: co-authored paper at ACM MM '23, demonstrating research rigor
- Strong educational pedigree: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps
- Streaming and real-time systems: FastAPI + SSE streaming, Redis semantic caching, WebSocket — relevant to low-latency data system requirements
- Python proficiency: primary language used across all AI/ML projects, matching one of the job's preferred languages

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Distributed tracing / observability tooling (OpenTelemetry, Datadog APM, distributed tracing stacks) — no evidence of APM or tracing experience
- Go or Java proficiency — job lists Go/Java/Python; candidate only demonstrates Python for backend AI work
- Microservices performance engineering: latency breakdowns, concurrency patterns, resiliency — not evidenced in resume
- Production ops experience: monitoring, alerting, on-call rotation — no mention of owning services in production at scale
- SLO/SLA practices and incident response — not mentioned
- Anomaly detection and ranking/recommendation systems — not evidenced
- Statistics and experiment design (confidence intervals, A/B testing) — not explicitly mentioned
- 4+ years of professional backend/ML systems experience — candidate has ~1 year of internship experience
- High-throughput, low-latency distributed systems experience at production scale

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system at Disanji: emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement, hybrid BM25+BGE-M3 retrieval, FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation pipeline — this directly mirrors the job's retrieval/indexing and eval requirements
- Highlight the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project: discuss quality-gated routing, ATS keyword scoring, and LLM self-critique as evidence of building robust agent systems with guardrails and evaluation loops
- Connect the streaming architecture (FastAPI + SSE, Redis semantic caching, WebSocket) to Datadog's need for high-throughput, low-latency data systems
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video understanding to demonstrate ML research depth and ability to work with complex, multi-signal data — analogous to analyzing traces, metrics, and logs
- Address the seniority gap proactively: emphasize the breadth of end-to-end ownership (problem discovery → architecture → deployment → evaluation) across multiple projects despite early career stage
- Express genuine interest in the observability/APM domain and willingness to rapidly learn distributed tracing (OpenTelemetry, Datadog APM) — frame existing debugging and performance mindset as a foundation
- Discuss the YOLOv8 industrial defect detection project (91% mAP@50) as evidence of ML lifecycle ownership: data pipeline, training, evaluation, and deployment
- Clarify location flexibility or willingness to relocate to Paris if applicable — this is a critical factor to address early in the process
