<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Senior AI Engineer - APM Experiences
Company: Datadog
Location: Paris, France
Description: &lt;p&gt;Datadog’s APM Experiences team owns the core product experience for&amp;nbsp;&lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.datadoghq.com/tracing/&quot;&gt;Application Performance Monitoring&lt;/a&gt; — including distributed tracing, service representation, and more. We’re building a new wave of AI-powered capabilities that help customers &lt;em&gt;detect, resolve, and prevent&lt;/em&gt; performance issues faster. In this role, you will lead end‑to‑end development of LLM- and Agent‑based features that can:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Debug and investigate application performance issues down to the root cause, as both a developer assistant and a fully autonomous agent&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proactively recommend performance and reliability-based optimizations to prevent the next incident&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Automatically create intelligent monitors and SLOs for the most important business flows and critical paths&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;This is a highly product‑minded engineering role: you’ll work from problem discovery and UX all the way to reliable, scalable production systems.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships that it builds, the creativity it brings to the table, and the collaboration of being together. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our employees can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you’ll do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape AI experiences for APM. Design and ship LLM/agentic workflows that analyze traces, metrics, logs, and other telemetry to generate diagnoses, explanations, and guided fixes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own the full loop. Prototype quickly, define success metrics and evals, run experiments, iterate, and ultimately productionize for scale and reliability.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build robust agent systems. Develop tools, retrieval and planning strategies, and guardrails; manage prompts/evals; design fallbacks and human‑in‑the‑loop paths.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Integrate with Datadog’s platform. Leverage surfaces like Trace Explorer, Service Catalog, monitors, and workflows to deliver end‑to‑end value in the APM UI.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Partner deeply. Collaborate with PM, Design, and partner teams to build cohesive experiences.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Raise the bar on engineering. Write performant, maintainable backend code, own services in production, and improve reliability for high‑throughput, low‑latency data systems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who you are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Product‑minded engineer who ships AI to production&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;4+ years building backend or real-time ML systems; you value simplicity, correctness, and performance&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven experience delivering LLM/agent features to production (prompting, tooling, evals, safety/guardrails)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable owning user journeys, iterating from prototype → alpha → GA, and measuring impact with clear product metrics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Strong ML / applied science fundamentals&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Solid grasp of the ML lifecycle (task definition, dataset collection, modeling, evaluation, deployment, iteration) and statistics (experiment design, confidence intervals)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience choosing/modeling the right technique for the job (e.g., anomaly detection, ranking/recommendation, NLP), and knowing when a heuristic beats a model&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Fluency with offline/online evals for AI systems; can build reliable golden sets and automatic regressions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Distributed systems &amp;amp; observability savvy&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with microservices performance: tracing, latency breakdowns, concurrency, and resiliency patterns&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficient in Go, Java, or Python; strong API/service design; production ops (monitoring, alerting, on‑call rotation)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Bonus: you’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Bonus Point:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands‑on with distributed tracing stacks (OpenTelemetry/Datadog APM), profilers, and logs/metrics pipelines&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Exposure to planning/agent frameworks, tool‑use orchestration, RAG, and retrieval/indexing for observability data&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Familiarity with SLO/SLA practices and incident response&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h4&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan (ESPP)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous professional development, product training, and career pathing&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Intradepartmental mentor and buddy program for in-house networking&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;An inclusive company culture, ability to join our Community Guilds (Datadog employee resource groups)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Access to Inclusion Talks, our Internal panel discussions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Free, global mental health benefits for employees and dependents age 6+&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive global benefits&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. #### 27. Senior AI Engineer - AI Developer Workflows at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 5.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 3/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 2/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Tel Aviv, Israel
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong AI/ML technical alignment with the role — particularly in LLM, RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, and agentic systems — which maps well to Datadog's AI developer workflow focus. However, the role explicitly requires 5+ years of professional backend engineering experience and is a Senior-level position, while the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with only ~6 months of relevant internship experience. The most critical blocker is location: the job is based in Tel Aviv, Israel (hybrid), while the candidate's preferences are Remote/US/Canada, making this a near-disqualifying geographic mismatch.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG pipeline experience using LangChain LCEL, BM25, BGE-M3, Milvus, and FlashRank reranking — directly relevant to Datadog's AI agent workflows
- Published ACM MM '23 paper on multimodal video-language models, demonstrating research depth in LLMs and AI
- LangGraph multi-agent pipeline project (4-agent resume generator) shows practical agentic system design experience
- Full-stack capability (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, PostgreSQL, Docker) enables end-to-end feature ownership
- Automated evaluation pipeline experience (RAGAS, ATS scoring, LLM self-critique) aligns with the 'evals' requirement in the job posting
- Strong academic credentials: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specialization in RL, LLM, CV, and DevOps
- Experience deploying LLM systems to production via Docker Compose, Redis semantic caching, and SSE streaming

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 5+ years of professional backend engineering experience (candidate has ~6 months of internship experience)
- Experience building developer tools such as IDEs, static code analysis tools, or SDLC tooling
- MCP (Model Context Protocol) integration experience
- Experience with large-scale distributed systems and large datasets at production scale
- Observability/monitoring platform experience (Datadog-specific domain knowledge)
- Demonstrated community ambassador or technical leadership experience
- Experience with AI coding agents like Cursor, GitHub Copilot, or Claude Code integrations

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production RAG system at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval, reranking, streaming, Redis caching) and the 35% precision improvement — this directly mirrors Datadog's AI agent workflow goals
- Lead with the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline project: explain the quality-gated routing architecture and dual-layer evaluation (ATS + LLM self-critique) as evidence of agentic system design thinking
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research credibility in multimodal AI and video-language models, positioning yourself as someone who follows and contributes to the field
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively but reframe: emphasize the density and quality of AI-specific experience (RAG, agents, evals) over raw years, and highlight the MEng specialization in LLM and RL as accelerating your trajectory
- Express specific interest in Datadog's observability-meets-AI vision — connect your experience building evaluation pipelines (RAGAS) to the idea of bringing observability to AI systems, which is core to Datadog's product direction
- Address the location mismatch directly if asked: clarify openness to relocation or explore whether a remote arrangement is negotiable given the role's hybrid tag

Title: Senior AI Engineer - AI Developer Workflows
Company: Datadog
Location: Tel Aviv, Israel
Description: &lt;p&gt;We’re building a new team building to bring the power of Datadog to developers within AI-focused Developer workflows such as AI agents like Cursor, Claude code, GitHub Copilot and Datadog’s native AI agents.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;We’re looking for product-minded engineers who are excited about developer tools, SDLC improvements and AI in general to help us quickly define and ship features and tools that make all Datadog customers 10x developers.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships that it builds, the creativity it brings to the table, and the collaboration of being together. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our employees can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work on agents, prompts, evals, MCPs and direct agent tools from ideation to productization, for allowing Datadog to help companies and developers to maximize the benefit of their integration with Datadog&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Help conceptualize and build innovative User flows and experiences within Datadog’s user space and different extensions&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Prototype and see what works well in the real world, iterate closely with our users, and then ship&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Follow the latest research on LLM prompt optimization, agents, and code editing.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Support other Datadog product teams to help them build and productionize their developer-focused AI Workflows. For example, you’ll work with the Live Debugger team to create an agentic production debugging experience, delivering on the vision of&amp;nbsp; “one click to reproduce” for production errors.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re a backend engineer with at least 5 years of professional experience who values code simplicity and performance. You’ve shipped software that uses LLMs to production&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You are passionate about Generative AI and its ability to help software engineers be more productive&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have relevant experience with Large Language Models, large-scale systems and data sets, machine learning, or adjacent fields&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You are a great community ambassador and can drive hard technical conversations towards a pragmatic solution&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You want to work in a fast, high-growth startup environment that respects its engineers and customers&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have experience writing classic or AI oriented developer tools(such as IDEs, static code analysis, SDLC tools etc).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have a BS/MS/PhD in a Computer Science, Engineering or related scientific field or equivalent experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan (ESPP)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Continuous professional development, product training, and career pathing&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Intradepartmental mentor and buddy program for in-house networking&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;An inclusive company culture, ability to join our Community Guilds (Datadog employee resource groups)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Access to Inclusion Talks, our Internal panel discussions&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Free, global mental health benefits for employees and dependents age 6+&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Competitive global benefits&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;em&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; <details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Senior Machine Learning Engineer - GenAI Platform 
Company: Databricks
Location: San Francisco, California
Description: &lt;p&gt;P-984&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Founded in late 2020 by a small group of machine learning engineers and researchers, Mosaic AI enables companies to securely fine-tune, train and deploy custom AI models on their own data, for maximum security and control. Compatible with all major cloud providers, the Mosaic AI platform provides maximum flexibility for AI development. Introduced in 2023, Mosaic AI’s pretrained transformer models have established a new standard for open source, commercially usable LLMs and have been downloaded over 3 million times. Mosaic AI is committed to the belief that a company’s AI models are just as valuable as any other core IP, and that high-quality AI models should be available to all.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Now part of Databricks since July 2023, we are passionate about enabling our customers to solve the world&#39;s toughest problems — from making the next mode of transportation a reality to accelerating the development of medical breakthroughs. We do this by building and running the world&#39;s best data and AI platform so our customers can use deep data insights to improve their business. We leap at every opportunity to solve technical challenges, striving to empower our customers with the best data and AI capabilities.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Summary:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Databricks Mosaic AI is hiring experienced machine learning platform engineers to build out our customer-facing generative AI platform for the ML development lifecycle including data generation, training, evaluation, serving, and agent-building.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You will thrive in this role if you have a strong sense of end-to-end ownership and enjoy translating user requirements into product interfaces and building the backend distributed systems to power those interfaces. In this role, you will have the opportunity to contribute to all areas of our stack spanning from user-facing features to low-level GPU orchestration.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You will:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Play a key role in the end-to-end design and implementation of our product which is a platform for powering use cases across training and serving of generative AI models&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work closely with both customers and internal ML researchers to identify key areas of development for our generative AI platform&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Have strong end-to-end product ownership, translating product requirements into user interfaces and backend distributed system design and own end-to-end implementation&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and build the core platform infrastructure that supports our customer-facing product features&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Ensure the reliability, security, and scalability of the backend distributed systems that power all aspects of our product&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;We look for:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;4+ years of hands-on programming experience with at least one modern language such as Python, Scala, Go, or C++&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong sense of distributed systems design and experience building large-scale systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building ML platform systems for applications in the ML model development lifecycle such as data preparation, model training, model evaluation, and model serving&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Direct experience developing ML models is a plus but not required&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong sense of end-to-end product ownership as well as intuition for both robust system design and product usability&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Effective communication skills and the ability to articulate complex technical ideas to cross-disciplinary internal and external stakeholders.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;We value candidates who are curious about all parts of the company&#39;s success and are willing to learn new technologies along the way.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Pay Range Transparency&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-size: 14px;&quot;&gt;Databricks is committed to fair and equitable compensation practices. The pay range(s) for this role is listed below and represents the expected salary range for non-commissionable roles or on-target earnings for commissionable roles.&amp;nbsp; Actual compensation packages are based on several factors that are unique to each candidate, including but not limited to job-related skills, depth of experience, relevant certifications and training, and specific work location. Based on the factors above, Databricks anticipates utilizing the full width of the range. The total compensation package for this position may also include eligibility for annual performance bonus, equity, and the benefits listed above. For more information regarding which range your location is in visit our page &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/sites/default/files/2024-08/us-pay-zone-mapping.pdf&quot;&gt;here&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Local Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$166,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$225,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees. For specific details on the benefits offered in your region, please visit&amp;nbsp;&lt;a href=&quot;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&quot;&gt;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&lt;/a&gt;.&amp;nbsp;&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment to Diversity and Inclusion&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Databricks, we are committed to fostering a diverse and inclusive culture where everyone can excel. We take great care to ensure that our hiring practices are inclusive and meet equal employment opportunity standards. - Built and evaluated LLM agent workflows using LangGraph with quality-gated routing and ATS/LLM self-critique evaluation layers

- Published research in multimodal video-language models (ACM MM '23), demonstrating ML fundamentals and academic rigor
- Full-stack production deployment experience: FastAPI, Docker, PostgreSQL, Redis, WebSocket — showing ability to ship end-to-end systems
- Computer Vision experience (YOLOv8, SAHI) and deep learning background complement the ML lifecycle understanding required
- Strong academic credentials: GPA 3.9 MEng at University of Waterloo with specializations in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Experience with semantic caching (Redis), vector databases (Milvus, ChromaDB), and embedding models (BGE-M3) — bonus-point skills for this role

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 6+ years of backend engineering experience (candidate has ~1–2 years internship-level experience)
- Go, Java (production-grade), or .NET backend proficiency — the role explicitly requires one of these; candidate's Java is listed but not demonstrated in production context
- Distributed systems and microservices experience: tracing, latency breakdowns, concurrency, resiliency patterns
- Observability/APM tooling experience (OpenTelemetry, Datadog APM, distributed tracing stacks)
- Production on-call operations mindset and monitoring/alerting experience
- Developer tooling experience (IDEs, static analysis, compilers, code transformation pipelines)
- Experience with large-scale portfolio automation (tracer feature rollouts, schema migrations at scale)
- Familiarity with Datadog APM integrations or similar APM ecosystems

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (LangChain LCEL, hybrid BM25 + BGE-M3 retrieval, FlashRank reranking, Redis semantic caching, FastAPI + SSE streaming) and the 35% precision improvement — this directly mirrors the role's AI tooling mandate
- Highlight the RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall) and the dual-layer ATS + LLM self-critique evaluator in the resume generator project as concrete examples of building eval harnesses that prevent silent quality drift
- Discuss the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing as evidence of human-in-the-loop agent workflow design — connect this to Datadog's need for engineers to trust AI output in production
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video-language models to demonstrate ML fundamentals and ability to work on novel, research-grade AI problems
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively but frame the Master's specialization in LLM/RL/DevOps at Waterloo as accelerated, focused preparation — and express willingness to grow into the distributed systems and observability requirements
- Express genuine interest in Datadog's APM domain and mention willingness to learn OpenTelemetry/distributed tracing stacks, connecting it to existing FastAPI + Docker deployment experience

<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

&lt;p&gt;IDM is the team that works on Datadog APM integrations: the parts that connect Datadog to the tools and services customers use. The team’s job is to make it easier for engineers to build and maintain integrations over time, and to keep quality and reliability high.&amp;nbsp;You will build AI-assisted tools that help with day-to-day engineering work, like drafting code changes, suggesting fixes, and checking results with tests and other automated checks. The goal is building tools that engineers can trust in real production work. You’ll also work closely with other teams, understand how they work, and build solutions that fit their process.&amp;nbsp;You will define what “good” means for these tools, measure results, and improve them over time. You’ll set up evaluation and testing so the AI output stays correct and doesn’t silently get worse. This is a senior role with high ownership, from early prototypes to production and ongoing support.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build agent workflows that take an integration need from plan to implementation and validation with humans approving at the right checkpoints&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Create systems that synthesize context from codebases, docs, specs, telemetry, and historical incidents to make changes that match Datadog conventions and customer expectations&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generate and evolve integration code and tests, including end-to-end scenarios that reflect real customer workloads and product features&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design evaluation harnesses that prevent silent regressions: golden sets, scenario baselines, semantic checks, performance thresholds, and release gating&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build portfolio-level automation: proactive updates for upstream breaking changes, tracer feature rollouts across the catalog, migrations to new schemas/semantics, and targeted coverage expansion&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Partner tightly with PM, support engineers, and integration-owning teams to make the system adoptable, trustworthy, and embedded in daily engineering workflows&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Product-minded engineer who ships AI to production&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;6+ years building backend systems (Go, Java, or .NET) with strong focus on simplicity, correctness, and performance&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven experience delivering LLM/agent features to production (prompting, tooling, evals, safety/guardrails)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable navigating ambiguity, iterating from prototype to production, and measuring impact with clear metrics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Strong ML fundamentals&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Solid grasp of the ML lifecycle (task definition, dataset construction, modeling, evaluation, deployment, iteration) and statistics for experiments&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Fluency with offline/online evals: golden sets, automated regressions, and evaluation harnesses that prevent silent quality drift&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Distributed systems &amp;amp; observability savvy&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with microservices performance: tracing, latency breakdowns, concurrency, resiliency patterns&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Production operations mindset: monitoring, alerting, and participating in on‑call rotations where applicable&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Bonus Point:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands‑on with distributed tracing stacks (OpenTelemetry/Datadog APM), profilers, and logs/metrics pipelines&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with planning/agent frameworks, tool‑use orchestration, RAG, and retrieval/indexing over large context&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building developer tools (IDEs, static analysis, compilers, code transformation pipelines) or code-generation systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Familiarity with semantic conventions / schema migrations, scenario-based testing, and cross-language porting at scale&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan (ESPP)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous professional development, product training, and career pathing&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Intradepartmental mentor and buddy program for in-house networking&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;An inclusive company culture, ability to join our Community Guilds (Datadog employee resource groups)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Access to Inclusion Talks, our Internal panel discussions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Free, global mental health benefits for employees and dependents age 6+&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive global benefits&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Remote&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. Title: Staff Applied AI/ ML Engineer 
Company: Gusto, Inc.
Location: Denver, CO;San Francisco, CA;New York, NY;Seattle, WA;Toronto, Ontario, CAN - Remote
Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p style=&quot;line-height: 1.2;&quot;&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Gusto&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;At Gusto, we&#39;re on a mission to grow the small business economy. We handle the hard stuff—like payroll, health insurance, 401(k)s, and HR—so owners can focus on their craft and customers. With teams in Denver, San Francisco, and New York, we’re proud to support more than 400,000 small businesses across the country, and we’re building a workplace that represents and celebrates the customers we serve. Learn more about our &lt;/span&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://gusto.com/about/careers/total-rewards&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Total Rewards philosophy&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;h2&gt;About the role&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt;Gusto’s AI/ML Eng team leverages our rich dataset to guide product direction and decision-making by building the right solutions at scale. We’re looking for a senior technical leader (individual contributor) who will design robust AI/ML systems and services through close partnership with seasoned leaders across Engineering, Product, Design, Data Science, Marketing, and Customer Excellence. This means you will also define the strategy along with shipping impactful models and services to evolve our AI/ML capabilities and practices.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;We have several open roles across two embedded areas:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Unified Service Platform optimizes our customer experience, creates personalized engagement moments, and drives automated or AI enhanced assistance. We develop AI tools and platform components (ex. embedding algorithms) to improve performance and customer satisfaction.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Growth builds AI/ML-powered experiences that drive relevant, timely product adoption. Our team enables contextual customer interactions through growth-oriented capabilities like propensity modeling and recommendations.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h2&gt;Here’s what you’ll do day-to-day&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and develop scalable, production-grade AI/ML models and services for complex business and customer experience problems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead technical strategy and architecture for key AI/ML domains, including model lifecycle, observability, safety, and evaluation.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally to identify high-leverage AI/ML opportunities, translate them into actionable roadmaps, and deliver measurable outcomes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build, harden, and operate platform capabilities that power customer journey experiences (e.g., personalization, assistance, routing, content selection).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Stay current with the latest AI/ML research; prototype, evaluate, and productionize new algorithms and techniques from academia and industry.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Establish robust practices for validation, deployment, monitoring, explainability, and ongoing performance management.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Communicate findings, roadmaps, tradeoffs, and impact clearly to executives and non-technical partners.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Mentor engineers and scientists, fostering a culture of technical excellence and pragmatic innovation.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h2&gt;Here’s what we’re looking for&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;8+ years of hands-on experience building and deploying end-to-end AI/ML systems in industry or academia.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Deep expertise in one or more advanced ML areas (e.g., supervised/unsupervised learning, deep learning, NLP, LLMs, retrieval/RAG, reinforcement learning).&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Our Growth positions would require a background in growth-oriented solutions (e.g., propensity scoring, recommendation systems, forecasting, lead scoring) and experimentation.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven track record delivering large, impactful AI/ML projects into production environments with measurable business and customer impact.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience monitoring AI agents with an attention to safety/guardrails, evaluation frameworks, and sentiment/quality analysis is necessary for the Unified Service Platform roles but not required for our Growth positions.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficiency with modern ML frameworks and tooling (e.g., PyTorch, TensorFlow, Hugging Face) and cloud platforms (e.g., GCP, AWS, or Azure).&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands-on experience building LLM-based applications and agentic AI workflows (e.g., Claude, Gemini, OpenAI), including prompt/retrieval design and evaluation, is a bonus.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong programming skills in Python and sound software engineering fundamentals (testing, code review, CI/CD, reliability).&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Demonstrated leadership in cross-disciplinary settings and the ability to influence direction through technical depth and clarity.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Excellent communication skills and strong product/business judgment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Ph.D. or Master’s degree in Computer Science, Machine Learning, Statistics, Mathematics, or a related field is a plus.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;Our cash compensation amount for this role is targeted at $230k-280k for San Francisco, New York, and Seattle $205k - 255k in Los Angeles, $185k-234k in Denver, and 200k-250k CAD for Toronto. Final offer amounts are determined by multiple factors including candidate experience and expertise and may vary from the amounts listed above.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Gusto has physical office spaces in Denver, San Francisco, and New York City. Employees who are based in those locations will be expected to work from the office on designated days approximately &lt;strong&gt;2-3 days &lt;/strong&gt;per week (or more depending on role). The same office expectations apply to all Symmetry roles, Gusto&#39;s subsidiary, whose physical office is in Scottsdale.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Note: The San Francisco office expectations encompass both the San Francisco and San Jose metro areas.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;When approved to work from a location other than a Gusto office, a secure, reliable, and consistent internet connection is required. This includes non-office days for hybrid employees.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Our customers come from all walks of life and so do we. We hire great people from a wide variety of backgrounds, not just because it&#39;s the right thing to do, but because it makes our company stronger. If you share our values and our enthusiasm for small businesses, you will find a home at Gusto.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Gusto is proud to be an equal opportunity employer. We do not discriminate in hiring or any employment decision based on race, color, religion, national origin, age, sex (including pregnancy, childbirth, or related medical conditions), marital status, ancestry, physical or mental disability, genetic information, veteran status, gender identity or expression, sexual orientation, or other applicable legally protected characteristic. Gusto considers qualified applicants with criminal histories, consistent with applicable federal, state and local law. Gusto is also committed to providing reasonable accommodations for qualified individuals with disabilities and disabled veterans in our job application procedures. We want to see our candidates perform to the best of their ability. If you require a medical or religious accommodation at any time throughout your candidate journey, please fill out &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSdEfl7Bp-Twz7LLlhGlha46CrxZ9Kacgakmarf3K2TbnaEgKQ/viewform&quot;&gt;this form&lt;/a&gt; and a member of our team will get in touch with you.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Gusto takes security and protection of your personal information very seriously. Please review our &lt;a href=&quot;https://gusto.com/about/careers/fraudulent-activity-disclaimer&quot;&gt;Fraudulent Activity Disclaimer&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Personal information collected and processed as part of your Gusto application will be subject to&amp;nbsp;&lt;a href=&quot;https://gusto.com/about/careers/applicant-privacy-notice&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Gusto&#39;s Applicant Privacy Notice&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

- Address the Kubernetes gap proactively — mention willingness and ability to quickly learn K8s given Docker Compose experience and DevOps specialization background
- Highlight full-stack capability (FastAPI + React/TypeScript + PostgreSQL + Docker + WebSocket) as evidence of end-to-end ownership, which aligns with the team's expectation of engineers owning products from requirements to deployment
- Note the location mismatch directly — the role is in-office in Bengaluru, India, and the candidate is in Canada preferring remote/hybrid; this should be addressed upfront to avoid wasted effort

Title: Senior Machine Learning Engineer
Company: Cloudflare
Location: In-Office
Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Us&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Cloudflare, we are on a mission to help build a better Internet. Today the company runs one of the world’s largest networks that powers millions of websites and other Internet properties for customers ranging from individual bloggers to SMBs to Fortune 500 companies. Cloudflare protects and accelerates any Internet application online without adding hardware, installing software, or changing a line of code. Internet properties powered by Cloudflare all have web traffic routed through its intelligent global network, which gets smarter with every request. As a result, they see significant improvement in performance and a decrease in spam and other attacks. Cloudflare was named to Entrepreneur Magazine’s Top Company Cultures list and ranked among the World’s Most Innovative Companies by Fast Company.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We realize people do not fit into neat boxes. We are looking for curious and empathetic individuals who are committed to developing themselves and learning new skills, and we are ready to help you do that. We cannot complete our mission without building a diverse and inclusive team. We hire the best people based on an evaluation of their potential and support them throughout their time at Cloudflare. Come join us!&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;h4&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Location: Bengaluru India&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;h4&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About the team&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Data Intelligence &amp;amp; Analytics organization builds the core data platform and internal products that power decision-making across the company. We design and operate large-scale data systems, own the company’s data lake, ingestion infrastructure, and platform tooling, and develop end-to-end applications that transform complex datasets into fast, reliable, business-critical products used daily by go-to-market, product, and engineering teams. Our work sits at the intersection of data platforms, distributed systems, and product development, giving engineers the opportunity to own meaningful problems across the stack and build systems that truly run the business.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h4&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About the role&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;p&gt;We are looking for an experienced Machine Learning Engineer to join our Bengaluru team and help scale the next generation of our AI/ML models. In this role, you will build and operate the pipelines behind AI-driven applications, Agents, Chatbots that power teams across the company, including go-to-market, engineering, and product. Because the products we build are initiated and owned by the team, you will be involved end-to-end — from shaping requirements and designing systems to implementation, deployment, and long-term ownership.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You will focus on building scalable, reliable services and application backends, partnering closely with data and full-stack engineers to deliver new features and operate the pipelines and platforms behind our products. Our stack spans multiple languages and technologies, including Python, React, and TypeScript, and continues to evolve. Many of our applications also incorporate strong AI components, giving you opportunities to work with modern AI infrastructure and tools such as vector databases, MCP-based services, and Workers AI.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you&#39;ll do&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Deploy, manage &amp;amp; support ML Applications &amp;amp; Services on Kubernetes&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Understand MLOps landscape i.e tooling, tech stack, source systems etc. and work on introducing new tools and solutions for ML &amp;amp; AI initiatives.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Partner and align with Data Scientists, Data Engineers and internal teams to deliver ML solutions in a globally distributed environment.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead development of efficiencies to boost model training to deployment lead times&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Understand business/product strategy and high-level roadmap and align analysis efforts to enable them with data insights and help achieve their strategic goals.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Leverage Cloudflare products and services for AI &amp;amp; ML initiatives and applications&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Use software engineering best practices to publish model scores/insights/learnings at scale within the company.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Examples of desirable skills, knowledge and experience&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;M.S or Ph.D in Computer Science, Statistics, Mathematics, or other quantitative fields.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;5+ years of ML Engineering experience with proven industry experience in a large scale environment (PBs scale &amp;amp; globally distributed teams) &lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong experience in scientific computing using Python with Scikit-Learn &amp;amp; PyTorch or Tensorflow.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong experience working with Docker &amp;amp; Kubernetes to build and deploy applications and systems.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience working with ML Platform tools (AirFlow, Argo Workflows, ArgoCD) preferred.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience working with Data Scientists to deploy Machine Learning applications systems for training, inference and observability.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with Full-stack Web technologies and languages (FastAPI, Streamlit, JavaScript/TypeScript, Cloudflare Workers, etc.) preferred with the ability to quickly learn and contribute across a multi-language stack.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with Terraform, Google Cloud Platform (or any other public cloud equivalent) etc.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience working with CI/CD systems, version control (Git, Bitbucket, etc.), testing (Pytest, etc.) and DevOps tools.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with Databases such as BigQuery, Postgres, SQLite and ETL/ELT practices&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong cross-functional collaboration experience with data engineering and data analysts teams within the function.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;div&gt;Proficiency in large language models and the frameworks like Langchain, LangGraph, etc. necessary for implementing GenAI applications, such as chatbots and related use cases.&lt;/div&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong communication and presentation skills catered to different audiences within the company.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Demonstrated ability to design scalable, reliable, and observable systems, with experience influencing architecture and improving platform foundations.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong problem-solving and communication skills, with a track record of working effectively with cross-functional partners and driving projects through ambiguous problem spaces.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience mentoring engineers, leading by example in code and design, and contributing to a culture of technical excellence.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What Makes Cloudflare Special?&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We’re not just a highly ambitious, large-scale technology company. We’re a highly ambitious, large-scale technology company with a soul. Fundamental to our mission to help build a better Internet is protecting the free and open Internet.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/protecting-free-expression-online/&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Project Galileo&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;: Since 2014, we&#39;ve equipped more than 2,400 journalism and civil society organizations in 111 countries with powerful tools to defend themselves against attacks that would otherwise censor their work, technology already used by Cloudflare’s enterprise customers--at no cost.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/athenian/&quot;&gt;Athenian Project&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;: In 2017, we created the Athenian Project to ensure that state and local governments have the highest level of protection and reliability for free, so that their constituents have access to election information and voter registration. Since the project, we&#39;ve provided services to more than 425 local government election websites in 33 states.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://1.1.1.1/&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;1.1.1.1&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;: We released&lt;/span&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://1.1.1.1/&quot;&gt; &lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;1.1.1.1&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt; to help fix the foundation of the Internet by building a faster, more secure and privacy-centric public DNS resolver. This is available publicly for everyone to use - it is the first consumer-focused service Cloudflare has ever released. Here’s the deal - we don’t store client IP addresses never, ever. We will continue to abide by our&lt;/span&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/1.1.1.1/privacy/public-dns-resolver&quot;&gt; privacy commitment&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt; and ensure that no user data is sold to advertisers or used to target consumers.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Sound like something you’d like to be a part of? We’d love to hear from you!&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;This position may require access to information protected under U.S. export control laws, including the U.S. Export Administration Regulations. Please note that any offer of employment may be conditioned on your authorization to receive software or technology controlled under these U.S. export laws without sponsorship for an export license.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Cloudflare is proud to be an equal opportunity employer. &amp;nbsp;We are committed to providing equal employment opportunity for all people and place great value in both diversity and inclusiveness. &amp;nbsp;All qualified applicants will be considered for employment without regard to their, or any other person&#39;s, perceived or actual&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;race, color, religion, sex, gender, gender identity, gender expression, sexual orientation, national origin, ancestry, citizenship, age, physical or mental disability, medical condition, family care status, or any other basis protected by law. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We are an AA/Veterans/Disabled Employer.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Cloudflare provides reasonable accommodations to qualified individuals with disabilities. &amp;nbsp;Please tell us if you require a reasonable accommodation to apply for a job. #### 32. Staff Machine Learning Engineer, Listings and Host Tools Data and AI at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 4.5/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 7/10 | Location: 6/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Remote-USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG systems, LLMs, LangChain, Computer Vision (YOLO), and full-stack deployment — which partially align with Airbnb's ML engineering needs. However, this is a Staff-level role requiring 8+ years of industry experience, and the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with only ~6 months of relevant internship experience, representing a critical seniority gap. Additionally, the candidate lacks key enterprise ML stack technologies explicitly required by the job (TensorFlow/PyTorch, Spark, Airflow, Kubernetes, Hive), and the role requires US residency for remote eligibility while the candidate is currently based in Canada.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG and LLM engineering experience — built production-grade RAG pipeline with hybrid retrieval, reranking, and streaming at Disanji Technology
- Dual NLP + Computer Vision background satisfies a key 'must have' requirement
- Published research in multimodal video understanding (ACM MM '23) demonstrates academic ML depth
- Hands-on experience with end-to-end ML pipelines including data ingestion, model training, evaluation (RAGAS), and deployment
- Full-stack API and service design skills (FastAPI, WebSocket, REST APIs) align with the role's API/serving requirements
- Experience with Docker, Redis, PostgreSQL, and cloud platforms (AWS, Azure) shows infrastructure awareness
- High academic performance (GPA 3.9 MEng, 3.7 BASc, Dean's Honor List) from strong Canadian universities
- LangGraph multi-agent orchestration and agentic AI experience is cutting-edge and relevant

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 8+ years of industry experience (candidate has ~6 months of internship experience)
- TensorFlow or PyTorch (not explicitly listed in resume skills)
- Apache Spark for large-scale data processing
- Apache Airflow or equivalent workflow orchestration
- Kubernetes for container orchestration
- Data warehouse technologies (e.g., Hive, BigQuery, Redshift)
- A/B testing methodology and experimentation frameworks
- Scala programming language
- Large-scale ML model productionization at industry scale (millions of users)
- Personalization and recommendation systems experience
- Gradient boosted trees and classical ML algorithms at scale
- US residency / work authorization in a US state where Airbnb operates

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production RAG system built at Disanji — emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement, RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall), and real-world deployment with Docker/FastAPI/Redis as evidence of ML productionization skills
- Lead with the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video-language modeling to satisfy the 'must have NLP + CV' requirement and demonstrate research-grade ML depth beyond typical internship candidates
- Discuss the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline project to showcase agentic AI architecture, quality-gated routing, and modern LLM engineering — positioning yourself at the frontier of applied AI
- Proactively address the experience gap by framing your MEng specialization (RL, LLM, DevOps, Performance Testing) as equivalent to additional applied experience, and emphasize the accelerated learning curve demonstrated by your publication and production deployments
- Mention familiarity with AWS and Azure to signal cloud infrastructure awareness, and express readiness to quickly learn Spark, Airflow, and Kubernetes given your Docker and pipeline engineering background
- Address the US location requirement directly — clarify your eligibility and timeline for US work authorization (e.g., OPT if applicable) to remove recruiter uncertainty early in the process

Title: Staff Machine Learning Engineer, Listings and Host Tools Data and AI

Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;&quot;&gt;Airbnb was born in 2007 when two hosts welcomed three guests to their San Francisco home, and has since grown to over 5 million hosts who have welcomed over 2 billion guest arrivals in almost every country across the globe. Every day, hosts offer unique stays and experiences that make it possible for guests to connect with communities in a more authentic way.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Community You Will Join: &lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Listings and Host Tools Data and AI (DnA) team: This team supports host personalization products and provides data driven solutions to achieve superior host experience on Airbnb. These products include but are not limited to managing your space (MYS), host tools etc. We own data pipelines and ML models and will build services for serving that are used in the above areas.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Difference You Will Make:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;There is a huge opportunity to improve the Host and Guest experience by leveraging open source, third party, and home grown ML models. As an ML engineer, you will partner closely with our data science, product partners, and other ML + data engineers on the team to execute on these opportunities in order to improve the Host and Guest product experience on Airbnb.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;A Typical Day:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work with large scale structured and unstructured data, build and continuously improve cutting edge Machine Learning models for Airbnb product, business and operational use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work collaboratively with cross-functional partners including software engineers, product managers, operations and data scientists, identify opportunities for business impact, understand, refine, and prioritize requirements for machine learning models, drive engineering decisions, and quantify impact.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Prototype machine learning use cases for use in the product, and work with stakeholders to iterate on requirements.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop, productionize, and operate Machine Learning models and pipelines at scale, including both batch and real-time use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and build services, API to enable serving ML model driven data to product use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Expertise:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;8+ years of industry experience in applied Machine Learning, inclusive MS or PhD in relevant fields.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Must have experience in both Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong programming (Scala / Python / Java/ C++ or equivalent) and data engineering skills&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Deep understanding of Machine Learning best practices (eg. training/serving skew minimization, A/B test, feature engineering, feature/model selection), algorithms (eg. gradient boosted trees, neural networks/deep learning, optimization, state-of-art NLP and CV algorithms) and domains (eg. natural language processing, computer vision, personalization and recommendation, anomaly detection)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with 3 or more of these technologies: Tensorflow, PyTorch, Kubernetes, Spark, Airflow (or equivalent), data warehouse (eg. Hive)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Industry experience building end-to-end Machine Learning infrastructure and/or building and productionizing Machine Learning models, as well as integrating to product use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Exposure to architectural patterns of a large, high-scale software applications (e.g., well-designed APIs, high volume data pipelines, efficient algorithms, models)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with test driven development, familiar with A/B testing, incremental delivery and deployment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Location:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;This position is US - Remote Eligible. - **Overall Score:** 5.3/10

- **Employment Type:** Not specified

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG systems, LLMs, computer vision, and full-stack deployment — which partially align with Databricks' applied AI focus. However, the role is specifically targeting ML for Systems & Infrastructure (cluster management, query compilation, scheduling/optimization), which is a niche area the candidate has no demonstrated experience in. Additionally, the role is Senior-level requiring 2–8 years of production ML experience at high-growth companies, while the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with limited industry tenure. The on-site/Mountain View requirement is also a significant mismatch given the candidate's preference for remote/Canada-based work.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG and LLM engineering experience with production-grade deployment (FastAPI, Docker, Redis, streaming)

- Solid software engineering practices: testing (80%+ coverage), Docker, REST APIs, WebSocket, PostgreSQL
- High academic achievement: GPA 3.9 (MEng) and 3.7 (BEng) with Dean's Honor List recognition
- Breadth of ML knowledge spanning computer vision, deep learning, reinforcement learning, and LLMs
- Experience with vector databases (Milvus, ChromaDB) and hybrid retrieval systems
- Multi-agent orchestration experience with LangGraph pipelines

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- ML for Systems & Infrastructure (cluster management, query compilation, scheduling optimization)
- Operations Research (OR) and combinatorial optimization
- Experience at high-velocity, high-growth tech companies (Databricks-scale)
- Distributed systems and large-scale data infrastructure (Apache Spark, Delta Lake, MLflow)
- Model monitoring and serving in Tier 0 / mission-critical production environments
- Mathematical modeling beyond standard ML libraries (custom optimization algorithms)
- Experience with Databricks platform or similar data lakehouse technologies
- Statistical modeling and systems performance analysis at scale

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji — emphasize the end-to-end pipeline architecture, hybrid retrieval design, and measurable 35% precision improvement as evidence of applied ML engineering rigor
- Discuss the ACM MM '23 publication on DBVM to demonstrate research credibility and ability to contribute to the broader AI community, which Databricks explicitly values
- Emphasize software engineering discipline: 80%+ test coverage, Docker-based deployment, streaming APIs, and Redis caching show production-readiness beyond typical academic candidates
- Connect computer vision and deep learning experience (YOLOv8, SAHI, DBVM) to Databricks' interest in novel modeling techniques, framing willingness to learn ML-for-systems as a natural extension
- Address the seniority gap proactively — frame the combination of high-GPA graduate education, research publication, and two internships as accelerated growth, and express eagerness to work on infrastructure-scale ML problems
- If open to relocation, explicitly state willingness to work from Mountain View/SF office to remove the location barrier

Title: Senior Applied AI Engineer – ML for Systems & Infrastructure
Company: Databricks
Location: San Francisco, California
Description: &lt;p&gt;P-1380&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;As a Senior Applied AI Engineer at Databricks, you will apply machine learning, scheduling and optimization algorithms to improve the efficiency and performance of our engineering systems and infrastructure. From cluster management all the way down to query compilation, our Applied AI team works on some of the hardest, most interesting problems facing the business, making Databricks infrastructure and products as performant and cost-efficient as possible. This is a high impact problem as our customers look at us to deliver the most optimized workloads.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build end-to-end systems from the ground up in a small team of experienced people.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape the direction of our applied ML areas of investment by engaging with engineering and product teams across the company.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Drive the development and deployment of state-of-the-art AI models and systems that directly impact the capabilities and performance of Databricks&#39; products, infrastructure and services.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Architect and implement robust, scalable ML infrastructure, including data storage and&amp;nbsp; processing, model training and serving components, and monitoring and reporting systems to support seamless integration of AI/ML models into production environments.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work on novel modeling techniques in the field of ML for Systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Contribute to the broader AI community by publishing research, presenting at conferences, and actively participating in open-source projects, enhancing Databricks&#39; reputation as an industry leader.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;2-8 years of machine learning engineering experience in high velocity, high-growth companies&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong understanding of both computer systems and statistics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Large breadth of knowledge or interest in mathematical modeling beyond ML (OR, combinatorial optimization&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience developing AI/ML systems at scale in production&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong track record of ML modeling that goes beyond using standard libraries.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong coding and software engineering skills, and familiarity with software engineering principles around testing, code reviews and deployment.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience deploying, scaling and monitoring models in production; deep understanding of the unique infrastructure challenges posed by training and serving predictions in Tier 0 environments.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Why Join Us?&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;At Databricks, we are building state-of-the-art AI solutions that redefine how users interact with data and our products. You’ll have the opportunity to shape the future of AI-driven products at Databricks, work with cutting-edge models, and collaborate with a world-class team of AI and ML experts.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;If you&#39;re excited about pushing the boundaries of AI in real-world applications, we’d love to hear from you!&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Please note we are open to employees working from our Mountain View, CA office for this position.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Pay Range Transparency&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-size: 14px;&quot;&gt;Databricks is committed to fair and equitable compensation practices. The pay range(s) for this role is listed below and represents the expected salary range for non-commissionable roles or on-target earnings for commissionable roles.&amp;nbsp; Actual compensation packages are based on several factors that are unique to each candidate, including but not limited to job-related skills, depth of experience, relevant certifications and training, and specific work location. Based on the factors above, Databricks anticipates utilizing the full width of the range. The total compensation package for this position may also include eligibility for annual performance bonus, equity, and the benefits listed above. For more information regarding which range your location is in visit our page &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.databricks.com/sites/default/files/2024-08/us-pay-zone-mapping.pdf&quot;&gt;here&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Local Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$166,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$210,250 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees. For specific details on the benefits offered in your region, please visit&amp;nbsp;&lt;a href=&quot;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&quot;&gt;https://www.mybenefitsnow.com/databricks&lt;/a&gt;.&amp;nbsp;&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment to Diversity and Inclusion&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Databricks, we are committed to fostering a diverse and inclusive culture where everyone can excel. We take great care to ensure that our hiring practices are inclusive and meet equal employment opportunity standards. Individuals looking for employment at Databricks are considered without regard to age, color, disability, ethnicity, family or marital status, gender identity or expression, language, national origin, physical and mental ability, political affiliation, race, religion, sexual orientation, socio-economic status, veteran status, and other protected characteristics.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Compliance&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;If access to export-controlled technology or source code is required for performance of job duties, it is within Employer&#39;s discretion whether to apply for a U.S. government license for such positions, and Employer may decline to proceed with an applicant on this basis alone.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
