#### 34. Specialist Solutions Architect - Data Scientist / ML Engineer (Financial Services) at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 5.1/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 3/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 7/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Remote - Georgia; Remote - Illinois; Remote - New York; Remote - North Carolina; Remote - Texas
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse
- **Apply URL:** https://databricks.com/company/careers/open-positions/job?gh_jid=8434243002

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment in GenAI/ML tooling — particularly RAG pipelines, LangChain, vector databases, and LLM deployment — which directly maps to the GenAI advisory responsibilities of this SSA role. However, the role demands 5+ years of hands-on industry ML experience and 2+ years of customer-facing pre/post-sales experience, both of which the candidate falls significantly short of, with only ~4 months of relevant internship experience and no customer-facing or solutions architecture background. The education credentials are excellent (MEng at Waterloo, BASc at UBC with a 3.9 GPA), but the experience gap and absence of Databricks-specific tooling (Spark, MLflow, Delta Lake) are critical mismatches for this senior specialist role.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG pipeline expertise: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), reranking, and streaming — directly relevant to the GenAI advisory responsibilities
- Hands-on LangChain and LangGraph experience, including multi-agent orchestration and quality-gated routing pipelines
- Vector database proficiency with Milvus and ChromaDB, aligning with the role's emphasis on enterprise GenAI architectures
- Cloud infrastructure exposure on AWS and Azure, matching the ML Engineer track requirements
- Published research (ACM MM '23) on video-language models, demonstrating academic depth in ML
- Excellent academic credentials: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) and BASc at UBC (GPA 3.7, Dean's Honor List)
- Full-stack deployment skills (FastAPI, Docker, PostgreSQL, Redis) supporting production-grade ML application delivery
- Computer Vision experience with YOLOv8 and SAHI, broadening ML domain coverage
- Automated evaluation pipeline experience using RAGAS, relevant to model monitoring and MLOps

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Apache Spark / PySpark for large-scale distributed data processing (key Databricks preferred skill)
- Databricks platform experience (MLflow, Delta Lake, Unity Catalog, Databricks Lakehouse)
- MLOps tooling: drift monitoring, model registry, experiment tracking at production scale
- Customer-facing pre-sales or post-sales solutions architecture experience
- Financial services domain knowledge
- Fine-tuning LLMs (LoRA, PEFT, RLHF) — not evidenced in resume
- HuggingFace ecosystem usage not explicitly mentioned
- Experience communicating technical concepts to non-technical executive stakeholders
- 5+ years of industry ML experience (candidate has ~4 months internship + academic projects)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: highlight the hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 + Milvus), FlashRank reranking, RAGAS evaluation (0.84 context recall), and 35% precision improvement — this directly mirrors the GenAI advisory work described in the role
- Emphasize the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project with quality-gated routing and dual-layer ATS + LLM self-critique evaluation, showcasing ability to architect complex, production-ready AI systems
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate ability to communicate complex ML concepts to technical audiences and establish credibility as an emerging ML thought leader
- Highlight AWS and Azure exposure alongside Docker, FastAPI, and PostgreSQL deployments to address the cloud ML infrastructure requirement
- Proactively address the experience gap by emphasizing the depth and production-readiness of your projects relative to your career stage, and express a clear learning roadmap for Databricks tooling (Spark, MLflow, Delta Lake)
- Mention willingness to pursue Databricks certifications quickly and ability to meet the 3-month onboarding outcome expectations given strong foundational ML and engineering skills

&lt;p data-pm-slice=&quot;1 1 []&quot;&gt;FEQ427R31&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Mission&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;As a Specialist Solutions Architect (SSA) - Data Scientist / ML Engineer, you will be the trusted technical ML expert to both Databricks customers and the Field Engineering organization. You will work with Solution Architects to guide customers in architecting production-grade ML applications on Databricks, while aligning their technical roadmap with the continually evolving Databricks Data Intelligence Platform. You will continue to strengthen your technical skills through applying cutting edge technologies in GenAI, MLOps, and ML more broadly, expanding your impact through mentorship, and establishing yourself as a ML thought leader.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Architect production level ML workloads for customers using our unified platform, including end-to-end ML pipelines, training/inference optimization, integration with cloud-native services, MLOps, etc.&amp;nbsp;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Provide advanced technical support to Solution Architects during the technical sale ranging from feature engineering, training, tracking, serving to model monitoring all within a single platform, as well as participating in the larger ML SME community in Databricks&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally with the product and engineering teams to represent the voice of the customer, define priorities and influence the product roadmap, helping with the adoption of Databricks’ ML offerings&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build, scale, and optimize customer data science workloads and apply best in class MLOps to productionize these workloads across a variety of domains&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Serve as the trusted technical advisor for customers developing GenAI solutions, such as RAG architectures on enterprise knowledge repos, querying structured data with natural language, content generation, and monitoring&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;5+ years of hands-on industry ML experience in at least one of the following:&lt;/li&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;ML Engineer: Build and maintain production-grade cloud (AWS/Azure/GCP) infrastructure that supports the deployment of ML applications, including drift monitoring.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Data Scientist: Experience with the latest techniques in natural language processing including vector databases, fine-tuning LLMs, and deploying LLMs with tools such as HuggingFace, Langchain, and OpenAI&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline (Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Operations Research, etc.) or equivalent practical experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience communicating and/or teaching technical concepts to non-technical and technical audiences alike&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passion for collaboration, life-long learning, and driving business value through ML&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;[Preferred] 2+ years customer-facing experience in a pre-sales or post-sales role&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;[Preferred] Experience working with Apache Spark to process large-scale distributed datasets&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Can meet expectations for technical training and role-specific outcomes within 3 months of hire&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;This role can be remote, but we prefer that you be located in the job listing area and can travel up to 30% when needed.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; #### 35. AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 4.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 9/10 | Location: 4/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Remote - India
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse
- **Apply URL:** https://databricks.com/company/careers/open-positions/job?gh_jid=8099751002

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment in GenAI and RAG systems, with hands-on experience in LangChain, LangGraph, vector databases, and production-grade AI pipelines — directly matching several core technical requirements. However, the role demands a minimum of 5+ years of relevant Data Science/consulting experience, and the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with only ~1 year of internship experience, which is a critical gap. Additionally, the role is posted for Remote - India, which conflicts with the candidate's preferred locations of Remote US/Canada, creating a significant location mismatch.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on RAG pipeline development using LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), Milvus, and FlashRank reranking — directly matching the GenAI application requirement
- Built and evaluated production-grade RAG systems with RAGAS achieving 0.84 context recall, demonstrating evaluation and optimization expertise
- Multi-agent system experience via LangGraph 4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on video-language models, showing academic depth in AI/ML
- Graduate degree (MEng) in Electrical and Computer Engineering from University of Waterloo with 3.9 GPA — exceeds education requirement
- Cloud platform exposure on AWS and Azure, aligning with production ML deployment requirements
- Full-stack deployment experience with FastAPI, Docker, PostgreSQL, Redis, and WebSocket — relevant for production rollouts
- Computer Vision expertise (YOLOv8, SAHI) adds breadth to AI specialization
- Strong academic record: Dean's Honor List 2022–2024 at UBC

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 5+ years of relevant Data Science or consulting experience (candidate has ~1 year of internship experience)
- Databricks Intelligence Platform experience (not mentioned anywhere in resume)
- Apache Spark experience (not mentioned)
- DSPy framework experience (not mentioned)
- Text2SQL experience (not mentioned)
- Fine-tuning LLMs (not explicitly mentioned)
- HuggingFace ecosystem experience (not mentioned)
- pandas / scikit-learn / PyTorch hands-on experience (not listed in skills)
- Customer-facing consulting or professional services experience
- Conference speaking or thought leadership experience

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system built at Disanji — emphasize the hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 dense/sparse in Milvus), FlashRank reranking, and the 35% precision improvement, as this directly mirrors Databricks' GenAI productionization focus
- Highlight the RAGAS evaluation pipeline achieving 0.84 context recall — Databricks values rigorous GenAI evaluation and optimization, and this is a concrete, quantified result
- Discuss the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline in the Resume Generator project to demonstrate multi-agent system design skills, including quality-gated routing and dual-layer ATS + LLM self-critique evaluation
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication on video-language models to establish credibility as a researcher and communicator of complex AI concepts — relevant to the thought leadership and conference presentation expectations
- Proactively address the experience gap by emphasizing the depth and production-readiness of your projects, and express willingness to rapidly learn Databricks-specific tools (Spark, MLflow, Delta Lake, Mosaic AI)
- Mention AWS and Azure exposure and Docker-based production deployments to reinforce cloud ML deployment competency
- Address the location mismatch directly if contacted — clarify your geographic situation and openness to the India-remote designation if applicable

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;CSQ326R35&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Mission&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The AI Forward Deployed Engineering (AI FDE) team is a highly specialized customer-facing AI team at Databricks. We deliver professional services engagements to help our customers build and productionize first-of-its-kind AI applications. We work cross-functionally to shape long-term strategic priorities and initiatives alongside engineering, product, and developer relations, as well as support internal subject matter expert (SME) teams. We view our team as an ensemble: we look for individuals with strong, unique specializations to improve the overall strength of the team. This team is the right fit for you if you love working with customers, teammates, and fueling your curiosity for the latest trends in GenAI, LLMOps, and ML more broadly. This role can be remote.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The impact you will have:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop cutting-edge GenAI solutions, incorporating the latest techniques from our Mosaic AI research to solve customer problems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own production rollouts of consumer and internally facing GenAI applications&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Serve as a trusted technical advisor to customers across a variety of domains&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Present at conferences such as Data + AI Summit, recognized as a thought leader internally and externally&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate cross-functionally with the product and engineering teams to influence priorities and shape the product roadmap&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What we look for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building GenAI applications, including RAG, multi-agent systems, Text2SQL, fine-tuning, etc., with tools such as HuggingFace, LangChain, and DSPy&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Minimum of 5+ years of relevant experience as a Data Scientist preferably working in a consulting role&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Expertise in deploying production-grade GenAI applications, including evaluation and optimizations&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience, leveraging common machine learning and data science tools, i.e. pandas, scikit-learn, PyTorch, etc.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building production-grade machine learning deployments on AWS, Azure, or GCP&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline (Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Operations Research, etc.) or equivalent practical experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience communicating and/or teaching technical concepts to non-technical and technical audiences alike&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Passion for collaboration, life-long learning, and driving business value through AI&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;[Preferred] Experience using the Databricks Intelligence Platform and Apache Spark™ to process large-scale distributed datasets&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Databricks&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Databricks is the data and AI company. More than 10,000 organizations worldwide — including Comcast, Condé Nast, Grammarly, and over 50% of the Fortune 500 — rely on the Databricks Data Intelligence Platform to unify and democratize data, analytics and AI. Databricks is headquartered in San Francisco, with offices around the globe and was founded by the original creators of Lakehouse, Apache Spark™, Delta Lake and MLflow. To learn more, follow Databricks on&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot;&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://twitter.com/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://twitter.com/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw03FL8fJvOD97ytN02f5G2C&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;,&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.linkedin.com/company/databricks&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw15dLk3q8VxTfHEgCUg7NSt&quot;&gt;LinkedIn&lt;/a&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;span style=&quot;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;and&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;a style=&quot;color: rgb(255, 54, 33);&quot; href=&quot;https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-saferedirecturl=&quot;https://www.google.com/url?q=https://www.facebook.com/databricksinc&amp;amp;source=gmail&amp;amp;ust=1700237575733000&amp;amp;usg=AOvVaw39EcncitnlqV72EG2-RqXJ&quot;&gt;Facebook&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;At Databricks, we strive to provide comprehensive benefits and perks that meet the needs of all of our employees. #### 36. Machine Learning Engineer, Payments ML Accelerator at Stripe

- **Overall Score:** 4.5/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 5/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 4/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Seattle; San Francisco; New York City
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG systems, LLMs, LangChain, and computer vision — and holds a highly relevant graduate degree from a top Canadian university. However, the role's hard minimum of 7 years of industry ML experience is a critical disqualifier for a mid-level candidate who has only ~6 months of relevant internship experience. Additionally, the role requires proficiency in Scala and Spark for large-scale data infrastructure, which are entirely absent from the resume, and the job is tied to specific US cities (Seattle, SF, NYC) while the candidate prefers remote/Canada.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG and LLM engineering experience — built production-grade RAG pipeline with LangChain, BM25+dense hybrid retrieval, Milvus, FlashRank, and RAGAS evaluation
- Hands-on deep learning and computer vision experience (YOLOv8, SAHI, multimodal QA, published ACM MM '23 paper)
- Proficient in Python, the primary language for ML development
- Experience deploying ML systems to production using Docker, FastAPI, Redis, and PostgreSQL
- Relevant Master's degree in ECE with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision from University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9)
- Multi-agent LangGraph pipeline design demonstrates understanding of advanced AI architectures
- Experience with automated ML evaluation pipelines (RAGAS, ATS keyword scoring, LLM self-critique)
- AWS and Azure cloud exposure relevant to scalable ML deployment

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Scala — explicitly required, not present anywhere in resume
- Apache Spark — explicitly required for large-scale data pipelines, not found
- Streaming feature pipelines at scale (Kafka listed but no ML feature pipeline context)
- Fraud detection or payments domain ML experience
- Foundation model training or fine-tuning at scale
- 7+ years of industry ML experience (candidate has ~6 months internship experience)
- Experience with online model serving and operational stability at production scale (Stripe-level traffic)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji — emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement, hybrid BM25+dense retrieval architecture, and RAGAS evaluation pipeline as evidence of end-to-end ML deployment capability
- Discuss the ACM MM '23 publication on deep video understanding to demonstrate research depth and ability to work on novel, complex ML problems — directly relevant to Stripe's foundation model ambitions
- Emphasize the multi-agent LangGraph pipeline project with quality-gated routing as evidence of systems thinking and architecting generalizable, extensible ML workflows
- Address the experience gap proactively: frame the combination of a 3.9 GPA Master's degree, two ML internships, a research publication, and independent production projects as equivalent to broader industry exposure
- Mention Kafka in the context of streaming data familiarity, and express willingness to rapidly learn Scala and Spark given strong Python and distributed systems foundations
- Leverage the YOLOv8 industrial defect detection project (91% mAP@50) to demonstrate experience with high-stakes, production-quality ML model training and evaluation

Title: Machine Learning Engineer, Payments ML Accelerator
Company: Stripe
Location: Seattle; San Francisco; New York City
Description: &lt;div class=&quot;ace-line gutter-author-p-118095 emptyGutter&quot;&gt; &lt;div class=&quot;ace-line gutter-author-p-118095 emptyGutter&quot;&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Stripe&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone’s reach while doing the most important work of your career.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About the team&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;The Payments ML Accelerator team is developing foundational ML capabilities that drive innovation across Stripe&#39;s payment products. We build deep learning models that tackle Stripe&#39;s most complex payment challenges - from fraud detection to authorization optimization - and deliver measurable business impact. Our work combines advanced ML techniques with large-scale data infrastructure to enable rapid experimentation and seamless deployment of AI-powered solutions. As a central ML innovation hub, we work closely with product teams to identify high-impact opportunities and implement scalable solutions that can be leveraged across the organization.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you&#39;ll do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;As a machine learning engineer on our team, you’ll develop advanced ML solutions that directly impact Stripe’s payment products and core business metrics. Your role will span the entire ML lifecycle, from research and experimentation to production deployment.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You’ll work on high-leverage problems that require innovation in modeling, optimization, and system design. Where possible, you’ll look beyond point solutions - designing approaches and architectures that are reusable, extensible, and serve as foundation models for future capabilities.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The role demands strong technical judgment, deep knowledge of modern ML methods, and the ability to translate ideas into systems that deliver measurable impact. You’ll partner with product and engineering teams to identify opportunities where ML can move the needle today while setting Stripe up for long-term success.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Responsibilities:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and deploy deep learning architectures and foundation models to address problems across key payment entities such as merchants, issuers, or customers&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Identify high-impact opportunities, and drive the long-term ML roadmap through well-scoped high-leverage initiatives&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Architect generalizable ML workflows to enable rapid scaling and optimized online performance&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Deploy ML models online and ensure operational stability&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experiment with advanced ML solutions in the industry and ideate on product applications&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Explore cutting-edge ML techniques and evaluate their potential to solve business problems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work closely with ML infrastructure teams to shape new platform capabilities&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who you are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt;We are looking for ML Engineers who are passionate about using ML to improve products and delight customers. You have experience developing streaming feature pipelines, building ML models, and deploying them to production, even if it involves making substantial changes to backend code. You are comfortable with ambiguity, love to take initiative, and have a bias towards action.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Minimum requirements&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Minimum 7 years of industry experience doing end-to-end ML development on a machine learning team and bringing ML models to production&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficient in Python, Scala, and Spark&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proficient in deep learning and LLM/foundation models&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Preferred qualifications&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;MS/PhD degree in quantitative field or ML/AI (e.g. computer science, math, physics, statistics)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Knowledge about how to manipulate data to perform analysis, including querying data, defining metrics, or slicing and dicing data to evaluate a hypothesis&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience evaluating niche and upcoming ML solutions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt;

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong AI/ML fundamentals — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, and LLM-based systems — that align well with the AI engineering aspects of this role. However, the position is a Senior-level role requiring 5+ years of backend/microservices experience, CI/CD tooling expertise, and production observability systems, all of which are largely absent from the candidate's profile (currently completing a Master's degree with ~1 year of internship experience). Additionally, the role is based in Madrid, Spain (hybrid), which conflicts with the candidate's stated preference for remote work in the US or Canada, creating a significant location mismatch.

- Computer Vision & deep learning breadth: YOLOv8, SAHI pipeline, multimodal QA (DBVM), and ACM MM '23 publication demonstrate research-grade AI depth

- Production observability and monitoring experience (Datadog telemetry, tracing, alerting, on-call rotations — not mentioned)

**Interview Talking Points:**

- Discuss the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline project: frame the quality-gated routing logic as an early analog to deployment gate decision-making (allow/block outcomes based on evaluation criteria)
- Leverage the RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall) as evidence of understanding AI evaluation methodology — connect this to Datadog's need for precision/recall metrics and offline evals
- Emphasize the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video understanding to demonstrate research depth and ability to work on novel AI problems at the frontier
- Be transparent about the seniority gap but frame the MEng specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and DevOps as accelerated preparation — and express eagerness to grow into production ops and CI/CD domains
- Address the location mismatch proactively: clarify willingness to relocate to Madrid or negotiate a remote arrangement if applicable

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Senior AI Engineer - CD Visibility
Company: Datadog
Location: Madrid, Spain
Description: &lt;p&gt;At Datadog, we leverage AI across our observability platform to improve monitoring, speed up incident resolution, and ensure data reliability for cloud applications.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog’s &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.datadoghq.com/deployment_gates/&quot;&gt;Deployment Gates&lt;/a&gt; team builds customer-facing systems that decide whether software should ship to production. Deployment Gates sit directly in customers’ CI/CD pipelines and use observability data to answer one of the hardest questions in software delivery:&lt;br&gt;Given everything we know right now, is this deployment safe to proceed?&lt;em&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/em&gt;&lt;em&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/em&gt;In this role, you will:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work on the evolution of Deployment Gates from static rules to AI-driven decisions, combining Datadog telemetry to detect and block faulty changes before they impact users&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Lead the design of progressive deployment automation, starting with zero-setup, conservative AI rules and evolving toward adaptive gates that learn from incidents and organizational patterns&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design the foundation for autonomous remediation, connecting what changed in code to what broke in production, from blocking and rolling back unsafe deployments to proposing fixes and enforcing policies&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;This is a highly product‑minded engineering role: you’ll work from problem discovery and UX all the way to reliable, scalable production systems.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships that it builds, the creativity it brings to the table, and the collaboration of being together. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our employees can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What you’ll do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build AI-driven deployment gates: Design and ship decision systems that evaluate customer deployments using CI/CD context and Datadog telemetry, producing safe, explainable allow/block outcomes&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own evals and rollout: Define precision, recall, and trust metrics; build offline and online evals; validate changes in shadow mode; and safely promote improvements to enforcement&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design for robustness and safety: Implement conservative defaults, guardrails, fallbacks, and human-in-the-loop paths so gates behave predictably under noisy or incomplete data&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Partner closely with Product: Work hand-in-hand with the Product Manager to translate customer problems, adoption signals, and roadmap goals into concrete technical decisions and iterations&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Integrate across the Datadog platform: Partner with internal AI teams building the Faulty Deployment Detection pipeline, as well as teams working on LLMs and AI agents&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Own production systems: Build and operate reliable backend services that run in the critical path of customer deployments, and be on-call for those services&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who you are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;A Product‑minded engineer who ships AI to production&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have 5+ years experience with backend systems and microservices performance: tracing, latency breakdowns, concurrency, and resiliency patterns&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You are proficient in a modern programming language; strong API/service design; production ops (monitoring, alerting, on‑call rotation)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have proven experience delivering LLM/agent features to production&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You are comfortable owning user journeys, iterating from prototype → alpha → GA, and measuring impact with clear product metrics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;An End-to-end AI implementation owner: You understands the end-to-end LLM product lifecycle&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Fluent with offline/online evals for AI systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Bonus Point:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with Continuous Delivery tools (i.e. ArgoCD / Argo Rollouts, Spinnaker, Octopus Deploy)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Exposure to planning/agent frameworks, tool‑use orchestration, RAG, and retrieval/indexing for observability data&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan (ESPP)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous professional development, product training, and career pathing&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Intradepartmental mentor and buddy program for in-house networking&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;An inclusive company culture, ability to join our Community Guilds (Datadog employee resource groups)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Access to Inclusion Talks, our Internal panel discussions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Free, global mental health benefits for employees and dependents age 6+&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive global benefits&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. Learn more about #DatadogLife on &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.instagram.com/datadoghq/?hl=en&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Instagram&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/datadog&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;LinkedIn,&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.datadoghq.com/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Datadog Learning Center. &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;215&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Equal Opportunity at Datadog:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog is proud to offer &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.eeoc.gov/sites/default/files/2023-06/22-088_EEOC_KnowYourRights6.12ScreenRdr.pdf&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;equal employment opportunity&lt;/a&gt; to everyone regardless of race, color, ancestry, religion, sex, national origin, sexual orientation, age, citizenship, marital status, disability, gender identity, veteran status, and other characteristics protected by law. #### 38. Staff Machine Learning Engineer, Relevance and Personalization at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 3.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 5/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 6/10 | Location: 7/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** United States
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian is a mid-level candidate currently completing a Master's degree with strong foundations in AI/ML, RAG systems, and computer vision, but this role is a Staff-level position at Airbnb requiring 9+ years of industry experience in applied ML with a focus on large-scale search ranking, personalization, and recommendation systems. The candidate's ~1 year of industry experience (two short internships) falls dramatically short of the seniority bar. While the candidate demonstrates relevant AI/ML skills (LLMs, RAG, deep learning, computer vision), they lack critical production-scale ML infrastructure experience — specifically search/ranking systems, A/B testing frameworks, Spark, TensorFlow/PyTorch at scale, and Kubernetes — that are central to this role. The location and education dimensions are reasonable matches, but the experience and seniority gap make this a poor overall fit.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong AI/ML foundation including RAG pipelines, LLMs, LangChain/LangGraph, and computer vision (YOLO, deep learning)
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on video-language models, demonstrating academic ML credibility
- Hands-on production RAG system with measurable outcomes (35% retrieval precision improvement, 0.84 RAGAS context recall)
- Full-stack engineering capability (FastAPI, React, Docker, PostgreSQL, Redis) enabling end-to-end ML system development
- High academic achievement: 3.9 GPA at University of Waterloo MEng, Dean's Honor List at UBC
- Familiarity with Kafka (listed in skills), which is one of the preferred technologies
- Experience with data pipelines and evaluation frameworks (RAGAS, automated evaluation pipelines)
- Remote-eligible preference aligns with the job's US-remote eligibility

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 9+ years of industry ML experience (candidate has ~1 year via internships)
- Search ranking and recommendation systems experience (core domain of this role)
- Personalization and marketplace optimization at scale
- TensorFlow or PyTorch at production scale
- Spark for large-scale data processing
- Kubernetes for ML model serving and orchestration
- Airflow or equivalent workflow orchestration
- A/B testing and experimentation frameworks
- Feature engineering and feature platform development
- Model interpretability and hyperparameter optimization at scale
- Training/serving skew minimization
- Scala programming language
- Large-scale data warehouse experience (Hive or equivalent)
- Concept drift detection
- Staff/tech lead engineering experience and cross-functional leadership

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production RAG system built at Disanji — emphasize the end-to-end pipeline design, hybrid retrieval architecture, and measurable 35% precision improvement as evidence of ML engineering rigor
- Discuss the ACM MM '23 publication on video-language models to demonstrate research depth and ability to work on cutting-edge ML problems relevant to multimodal ranking signals
- Emphasize the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as evidence of systems thinking and architecting complex, multi-component ML workflows
- Connect computer vision experience (YOLOv8, SAHI) to Airbnb's use of image signals in search ranking
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively and frame the MEng at Waterloo (graduating Oct 2025) as accelerating toward the required expertise — consider targeting mid-level ML Engineer roles at Airbnb instead
- Mention Kafka familiarity as alignment with the tech stack, and express eagerness to grow into Spark, Kubernetes, and Airflow

Title: Staff Machine Learning Engineer, Relevance and Personalization
Company: Airbnb
Location: United States
Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;&quot;&gt;Airbnb was born in 2007 when two hosts welcomed three guests to their San Francisco home, and has since grown to over 5 million hosts who have welcomed over 2 billion guest arrivals in almost every country across the globe. Every day, hosts offer unique stays and experiences that make it possible for guests to connect with communities in a more authentic way.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Community You Will Join: &lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;The Relevance and Personalization team at Airbnb is responsible for search and recommendation across the entire Airbnb digital platform. Be a leader in the team working on critical, impactful projects with focus on developing end-to-end ranking algorithms and ecosystems for optimizing multiple critical business objectives.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Difference You Will Make:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;We build cutting-edge AI technologies across the end-to-end search ranking product stack w.r.t. data pipelines, feature and model innovations, serving and experimentation efficiency, leveraging rich signals from various types of data (structured, sequential, image, text, etc) at Airbnb. We collaborate closely with teams across Airbnb to develop the ranking solutions and support a healthy marketplace for hosts and guests to further Airbnb’s mission of creating a world where people can Belong Anywhere. Some past publications from the team can be found here:&lt;a href=&quot;https://sites.google.com/view/airbnb-relevance-publications/home&quot;&gt; https://sites.google.com/view/airbnb-relevance-publications/home&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;A Typical Day:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Work with large scale structured and unstructured data, build and continuously improve cutting edge Machine Learning models for Airbnb product, business and operational use cases.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Work collaboratively with cross-functional partners including software engineers, product managers, operations and data scientists, identify opportunities for business impact, understand, refine, and prioritize requirements for machine learning models, drive engineering decisions, and quantify impact.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Hands-on develop, productionize, and operate Machine Learning models and pipelines at scale, including both batch and real-time use cases.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Leverage third-party and in-house Machine Learning tools &amp;amp; infrastructure to develop reusable, highly differentiating and high-performing Machine Learning systems, enable fast model development, low-latency serving and ease of model quality upkeep.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Example projects include: feature platform, model interpretability, hyperparameter optimization, concept drift detection.&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Expertise:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;9+ years of industry experience in applied Machine Learning, inclusive MS or PhD in relevant fields.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Strong programming (Scala / Python / Java / C++ or equivalent) and data engineering skills.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Deep understanding of Machine Learning best practices (eg. training/serving skew minimization, A/B test, feature engineering, feature/model selection), algorithms (eg. neural networks/deep learning, optimization) and domains (eg. natural language processing, computer vision, personalization, search and recommendation, marketplace optimization, anomaly detection).&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Experience with 3 or more of these technologies: Tensorflow, PyTorch, Kubernetes, Spark, Airflow (or equivalent), Kafka (or equivalent), data warehouse (eg. Hive).&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Industry experience building end-to-end Machine Learning infrastructure and/or building and productionizing Machine Learning models.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Exposure to architectural patterns of a large, high-scale software applications (e.g., well-designed APIs, high volume data pipelines, efficient algorithms, models).&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;Experience with test driven development, familiar with A/B testing, incremental delivery and deployment.&lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Your Location:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;This position is US - Remote Eligible. The role may include occasional work at an Airbnb office or attendance at offsites, as agreed to with your manager. While the position is Remote Eligible, you must live in a state where Airbnb, Inc. has a registered entity. &lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;https://careers.airbnb.com/&quot;&gt;Click here for the up-to-date list of excluded states. This list is continuously evolving, so please check back with us if the state you live in is on the exclusion list.&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt; If your position is employed by another Airbnb entity, your recruiter will inform you what states you are eligible to work from.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment To Inclusion &amp;amp; Belonging:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;Airbnb is committed to working with the broadest talent pool possible. #### 39. Senior AI Engineer - APM Integrations at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 4.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 3/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 4/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Germany, Remote; Italy, Remote; Spain, Remote
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse
- **Apply URL:** https://careers.datadoghq.com/detail/7582805/?gh_jid=7582805

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates genuine strength in AI/ML engineering — particularly RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph agent workflows, and LLM evaluation — which aligns well with the AI-assisted tooling focus of this role. However, this is a Senior-level position requiring 6+ years of backend engineering experience in Go, Java, or .NET, and the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of internship/project experience and no demonstrated proficiency in Go, .NET, or production distributed systems. The location is also a mismatch: the role is Europe-remote (Germany, Italy, Spain) while the candidate prefers US/Canada remote, and Datadog operates as a hybrid workplace which may further complicate eligibility.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG pipeline experience: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3 + Milvus), FlashRank reranking, and RAGAS evaluation — directly relevant to the role's retrieval/indexing requirements
- LangGraph multi-agent workflow experience: architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing, closely matching the job's requirement to build agent workflows from plan to implementation and validation
- LLM evaluation mindset: built automated evaluation pipelines using RAGAS (0.84 context recall) and dual-layer ATS/LLM self-critique scoring, aligning with the role's emphasis on eval harnesses and preventing silent regressions
- Full-stack production deployment: FastAPI + Docker + PostgreSQL + Redis + WebSocket stack with 80%+ test coverage demonstrates production-readiness and engineering discipline
- Strong academic credentials: 3.9 GPA MEng at University of Waterloo with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps; published at ACM MM '23
- Computer Vision and deep learning breadth: YOLOv8, SAHI, multimodal QA (DBVM), and industrial defect detection show versatile ML fundamentals
- Semantic caching and streaming: Redis semantic caching and SSE/WebSocket streaming experience relevant to performance-conscious production AI systems

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Go, Java (backend), or .NET — the job explicitly requires 6+ years in one of these; candidate's backend is Python/FastAPI/Node.js
- 6+ years of backend engineering experience — candidate has ~1–2 years of internship experience and is still in graduate school
- Distributed systems expertise: microservices tracing, latency breakdowns, concurrency, resiliency patterns (e.g., circuit breakers, bulkheads)
- Observability/APM tooling: OpenTelemetry, Datadog APM, distributed tracing stacks, profilers, logs/metrics pipelines
- Production on-call operations mindset: monitoring, alerting, incident response at scale
- Developer tooling experience: IDEs, static analysis, compilers, code transformation pipelines, or code-generation systems at scale
- Semantic conventions / schema migrations and cross-language porting at scale
- Portfolio-level automation: proactive upstream breaking-change detection, tracer feature rollouts across large integration catalogs

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the RAG production deployment at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (BM25 + BGE-M3 + Milvus + FlashRank + RAGAS), the 35% precision improvement, and the 0.84 context recall — this directly maps to the role's retrieval/indexing and eval harness requirements
- Highlight the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing in the Resume Generator project as concrete evidence of building agent workflows with human-in-the-loop checkpoints — the exact architecture Datadog is building
- Emphasize the RAGAS automated evaluation pipeline as a foundation for the role's requirement to prevent silent quality regressions — draw the parallel to golden sets and scenario baselines explicitly
- Mention the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video understanding to demonstrate research rigor and ability to work on novel, ambiguous problems — relevant to a high-ownership senior role
- Address the experience gap proactively: frame the breadth of production-grade projects (Docker, FastAPI, Redis, PostgreSQL, WebSocket, 80%+ test coverage) as evidence of production engineering discipline despite fewer years
- If Go/Java is raised, note Java background from university coursework and willingness to ramp quickly — and highlight that Python/FastAPI production deployments demonstrate the same backend engineering principles
- On location: clarify work authorization and timezone flexibility if applicable, as the role is Europe-remote and this is a significant mismatch to address early

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

&lt;p&gt;IDM is the team that works on Datadog APM integrations: the parts that connect Datadog to the tools and services customers use. The team’s job is to make it easier for engineers to build and maintain integrations over time, and to keep quality and reliability high.&amp;nbsp;You will build AI-assisted tools that help with day-to-day engineering work, like drafting code changes, suggesting fixes, and checking results with tests and other automated checks. The goal is building tools that engineers can trust in real production work. You’ll also work closely with other teams, understand how they work, and build solutions that fit their process.&amp;nbsp;You will define what “good” means for these tools, measure results, and improve them over time. You’ll set up evaluation and testing so the AI output stays correct and doesn’t silently get worse. This is a senior role with high ownership, from early prototypes to production and ongoing support.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build agent workflows that take an integration need from plan to implementation and validation with humans approving at the right checkpoints&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Create systems that synthesize context from codebases, docs, specs, telemetry, and historical incidents to make changes that match Datadog conventions and customer expectations&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generate and evolve integration code and tests, including end-to-end scenarios that reflect real customer workloads and product features&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design evaluation harnesses that prevent silent regressions: golden sets, scenario baselines, semantic checks, performance thresholds, and release gating&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build portfolio-level automation: proactive updates for upstream breaking changes, tracer feature rollouts across the catalog, migrations to new schemas/semantics, and targeted coverage expansion&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Partner tightly with PM, support engineers, and integration-owning teams to make the system adoptable, trustworthy, and embedded in daily engineering workflows&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Product-minded engineer who ships AI to production&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;6+ years building backend systems (Go, Java, or .NET) with strong focus on simplicity, correctness, and performance&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven experience delivering LLM/agent features to production (prompting, tooling, evals, safety/guardrails)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Comfortable navigating ambiguity, iterating from prototype to production, and measuring impact with clear metrics&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Strong ML fundamentals&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Solid grasp of the ML lifecycle (task definition, dataset construction, modeling, evaluation, deployment, iteration) and statistics for experiments&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Fluency with offline/online evals: golden sets, automated regressions, and evaluation harnesses that prevent silent quality drift&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Distributed systems &amp;amp; observability savvy&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with microservices performance: tracing, latency breakdowns, concurrency, resiliency patterns&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Production operations mindset: monitoring, alerting, and participating in on‑call rotations where applicable&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Bonus Point:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands‑on with distributed tracing stacks (OpenTelemetry/Datadog APM), profilers, and logs/metrics pipelines&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience with planning/agent frameworks, tool‑use orchestration, RAG, and retrieval/indexing over large context&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience building developer tools (IDEs, static analysis, compilers, code transformation pipelines) or code-generation systems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Familiarity with semantic conventions / schema migrations, scenario-based testing, and cross-language porting at scale&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan (ESPP)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous professional development, product training, and career pathing&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Intradepartmental mentor and buddy program for in-house networking&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;An inclusive company culture, ability to join our Community Guilds (Datadog employee resource groups)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Access to Inclusion Talks, our Internal panel discussions&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Free, global mental health benefits for employees and dependents age 6+&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Competitive global benefits&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Remote&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. Learn more about #DatadogLife on &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.instagram.com/datadoghq/?hl=en&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Instagram&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/datadog&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;LinkedIn,&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.datadoghq.com/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Datadog Learning Center. &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;215&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Equal Opportunity at Datadog:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog is proud to offer &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.eeoc.gov/sites/default/files/2023-06/22-088_EEOC_KnowYourRights6.12ScreenRdr.pdf&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;equal employment opportunity&lt;/a&gt; to everyone regardless of race, color, ancestry, religion, sex, national origin, sexual orientation, age, citizenship, marital status, disability, gender identity, veteran status, and other characteristics protected by law. We also consider qualified applicants regardless of criminal histories, consistent with legal requirements. Here are our &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/candidate-legal-notices/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Candidate Legal Notices&lt;/a&gt; for your reference.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog endeavors to make our Careers Page accessible to all users. If you would like to contact us regarding the accessibility of our website or need assistance completing the application process, please complete &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSeoJeduEZ2NdZF_65uZmWUrCM-zee7yADXX1s6Xr9zAGq8iKA/viewform&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;this form&lt;/a&gt;. This form is for accommodation requests only and cannot be used to inquire about the status of applications.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;662&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Privacy and AI Guidelines:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;662&quot;&gt;Any information you submit to Datadog as part of your application will be processed in accordance with Datadog’s&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/applicant-candidate-privacy/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/applicant-candidate-privacy/&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;Applicant and Candidate Privacy Notice&lt;/a&gt;. #### 40. AI Engineer - FDE (Forward Deployed Engineer) - U.S. Federal Sector at Databricks

- **Overall Score:** 4.7/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 7/10 | Experience: 4/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 3/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Maryland; Virginia; Washington, D.C.
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong technical alignment with the AI/GenAI engineering requirements — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain, multi-agent systems, and production deployments — which are core to this role. However, two critical blockers severely limit this candidate's fit: (1) U.S. citizenship and eligibility for a U.S. government secret clearance are hard requirements for this federal-sector role, and the candidate appears to be a Canadian-based international student/graduate with no indication of U.S. citizenship or clearance eligibility; and (2) the role targets the Washington D.C./Maryland/Virginia metro area with on-site obligations, while the candidate is based in Canada and prefers remote/hybrid. Additionally, the candidate is a mid-level professional still completing their Master's degree (graduating Oct 2025), with limited industry experience (two short internships), whereas this role demands "extensive years of hands-on industry data science experience" and senior-level customer-facing consulting responsibilities.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong RAG pipeline expertise: built production-grade RAG system with hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3), FlashRank reranking, RAGAS evaluation achieving 0.84 context recall — directly matching a core job requirement
- Multi-agent system experience: architected a 4-agent LangGraph pipeline with quality-gated routing in a personal project
- LangChain/LangGraph proficiency: used LangChain LCEL in production at Disanji Technology Institute and LangGraph in personal projects
- Graduate degree in a quantitative discipline: Master of Engineering in ECE from University of Waterloo with 3.9 GPA, specializing in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Cloud platform experience: listed AWS and Azure in skills, relevant to production ML deployment requirement
- Computer Vision and Deep Learning background: YOLOv8 models, SAHI pipeline, published ACM MM '23 paper on video-language models
- Full-stack production deployment skills: FastAPI, Docker, PostgreSQL, Redis, WebSocket — demonstrates ability to own end-to-end production rollouts
- Automated evaluation pipeline experience: built RAGAS-based evaluation, aligning with the job's emphasis on GenAI evaluation and optimization
- Academic publication: co-authored paper at ACM MM '23, showing research depth and communication of technical concepts

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- U.S. citizenship / U.S. government secret clearance eligibility (hard requirement — not evidenced anywhere on resume)
- Experience working with U.S. federal civilian, state, or local government agencies
- Databricks Intelligence Platform experience (preferred but not mentioned)
- Apache Spark experience (preferred, not mentioned)
- DSPy framework experience (listed in job requirements alongside LangChain)
- Text2SQL experience (listed as a GenAI application type in requirements)
- LLM fine-tuning experience (listed as a GenAI application type in requirements)
- Pandas / scikit-learn / PyTorch hands-on data science tooling (not explicitly listed in resume skills)
- Customer-facing consulting or professional services experience
- Extensive years of industry data science experience (candidate has ~4 months of relevant internship experience)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with your RAG system at Disanji: emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement, hybrid BM25+BGE-M3 retrieval, and RAGAS evaluation pipeline — this directly mirrors the job's core GenAI production deployment requirement
- Highlight your LangGraph multi-agent pipeline project and explain the quality-gated routing architecture to demonstrate multi-agent system design thinking

- Discuss your AWS and Azure experience in the context of production ML deployments, and express willingness to learn Databricks and Apache Spark quickly given your strong ML/data engineering foundation
- Address the federal sector gap proactively: if you have any path to U.S. citizenship or clearance eligibility, this must be clarified upfront as it is a non-negotiable hard requirement
- Emphasize your breadth across the GenAI stack (embeddings, vector DBs, reranking, streaming, caching, evaluation) to position yourself as someone who can own end-to-end production GenAI rollouts

#### 41. Staff Applied AI and Machine Learning Engineer, Payments & Risk at Gusto, Inc.

- **Overall Score:** 4.7/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 5/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 8/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Denver, CO;San Francisco, CA;New York, NY;Los Angeles, CA;Seattle, WA;Toronto, Ontario, CAN - Remote
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian is a strong early-career candidate with solid AI/ML foundations — particularly in LLMs, RAG pipelines, and computer vision — but this role is a Staff-level position requiring 8+ years of experience with deep specialization in payments, credit risk, and fraud modeling. The candidate is currently completing a Master's degree (graduating ~Oct 2025) with roughly 1–2 years of internship experience, placing them far below the seniority threshold. While the technical skill overlap in Python, LLMs, and ML is genuine, the critical domain expertise in fintech risk modeling (logistic regression for credit risk, XGBoost fraud detection, anomaly detection at scale) is entirely absent from the resume.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong LLM and RAG pipeline experience (LangChain, LangGraph, ChromaDB, BGE-M3, RAGAS evaluation)
- Hands-on production deployment experience with FastAPI, Docker, Redis, and PostgreSQL
- Published research in multimodal AI (ACM MM '23), demonstrating academic rigor
- Master's degree in ECE from University of Waterloo with 3.9 GPA — meets the educational preference
- Python proficiency across the full ML development lifecycle
- Experience with vector databases (Milvus, ChromaDB) and hybrid retrieval systems relevant to LLM integration
- Toronto, Canada location aligns with one of the listed job locations (remote-eligible)

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Credit risk modeling (logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost for financial risk)
- Fraud detection and fraud risk modeling experience
- Statistical analysis on large-scale financial datasets
- Anomaly detection techniques in production environments
- Fintech or payments domain knowledge
- 8+ years of professional experience (candidate has ~1-2 years internship experience)
- Experience with R or other statistical modeling languages beyond Python
- Ensemble methods applied to business risk problems
- Stakeholder communication and cross-functional leadership at Staff/senior level
- Model monitoring and maintenance in production at scale

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji — emphasize the end-to-end ownership: pipeline design, hybrid retrieval, evaluation (RAGAS 0.84 recall), and Docker deployment with Redis caching, as this mirrors the 'deploy and monitor' lifecycle Gusto requires.
- Emphasize the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video understanding to demonstrate research credibility and ability to communicate complex technical findings — a key soft skill for a Staff-level role.
- Connect LangGraph multi-agent pipeline experience to Gusto's goal of integrating LLMs into risk platforms — position yourself as someone who can accelerate LLM adoption within their existing ML stack.
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively and reframe the conversation: express interest in a more junior AI/ML Engineer role at Gusto if available, or ask about growth paths within the Payments & Risk team.
- Mention the YOLOv8 defect detection work (91% mAP@50) as evidence of applied ML in a business context with measurable outcomes — closest analogue to risk model performance metrics.

Title: Staff Applied AI and Machine Learning Engineer, Payments & Risk
Company: Gusto, Inc.

Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p style=&quot;line-height: 1.2;&quot;&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Gusto&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;At Gusto, we&#39;re on a mission to grow the small business economy. We handle the hard stuff—like payroll, health insurance, 401(k)s, and HR—so owners can focus on their craft and customers. With teams in Denver, San Francisco, and New York, we’re proud to support more than 400,000 small businesses across the country, and we’re building a workplace that represents and celebrates the customers we serve. Learn more about our &lt;/span&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://gusto.com/about/careers/total-rewards&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Total Rewards philosophy&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About the Role:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Gusto’s Data Science team leverages Gusto’s rich dataset to guide product direction and decision-making. We operate full-stack, conducting analyses, prototyping and deploying predictive models and statistical tools both for internal use and for our customers.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;For this role, we are looking for a technical leader (an individual contributor) to drive machine learning and AI in the payment and risk domains.&amp;nbsp; You will build a model-driven risk platform to provide a trusted environment for Gusto Ecosystem.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;You’ll be working with an established team and seasoned payments and risk leaders in Engineering, Product, Design, Operation, Identity and Compliance. In this role, you’ll work cross functionally to build Platforms that span the entire breadth of the Payments and Risk Stacks, and use ML and AI to build a world- class, high secure platform that safeguards our users’ activities and money, and ensures unparalleled reliability.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Here’s what you’ll do day-to-day:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Build and deploy machine learning models to identify, assess and mitigate risks&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Responsible for driving research in the problem space, working with stakeholders to understand model requirements, developing the model from scratch, deploying the model alongside your engineering counterparts, and monitoring and maintaining the model’s performance over time&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Partner with Engineering, Design, and Product counterparts in Payment and Risk to solve complex cross functional problems&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop scalable frameworks and libraries that enhance and contribute to the team’s core analysis and modeling capabilities, including through the integration of LLMs to improve data processing, analysis, and insights.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Identify new opportunities to leverage data to improve Gusto’s products and help risk management team to understand business requirements&amp;nbsp; and develop tailored solutions&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Present and communicate results to stakeholders across the company&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Here’s what we&#39;re looking for:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;8+ years of experience conducting statistical analyses on large datasets and deep domain knowledge in machine learning and artificial intelligence, including familiarity with Large Language Models (LLMs) and their applications. This could mean either a MS or PhD in a quantitative field with at least 5 years experience in a business environment, or BS or Data Science Bootcamp graduate with at least 8 years of experience working as a data scientist or a machine learning engineer in a business setting.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven experience in credit risk modeling or fraud risk modeling using logistic regression, random forest, Xgboost or neural networks, along with a strong understanding of AI-based approaches and the potential of LLMs to enhance traditional models.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience applying a variety of statistical and modeling techniques using Python, R or another statistical modeling language, as indicated by familiarity with many of the following techniques - predictive modeling, anomaly detection, ensemble methods, natural language processing (NLP, optional). Basic understanding of LLMs and their applications.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong programming skills - comfortable with all phases of the data science development process, from initial analysis and model development to deployment&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Excellent communication skills - able to effectively deliver findings and recommendations to non-technical stakeholders in a clear and compelling fashion&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;PhD or Masters plus equivalent experience in a quantitative field is a plus&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Experience in the Fintech industry is a plus&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;Our cash compensation amount for this role is targeted at $225,000 - $285,000&amp;nbsp; for San Francisco, New York, and Seattle, $205,000- $255,000 in Los Angeles, $187,000 - $235,000 in Denver, and $200,000 - $250,000 CAD for Toronto, Canada. Final offer amounts are determined by multiple factors including candidate experience and expertise and may vary from the amounts listed above.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Gusto has physical office spaces in Denver, San Francisco, and New York City. Employees who are based in those locations will be expected to work from the office on designated days approximately &lt;strong&gt;2-3 days &lt;/strong&gt;per week (or more depending on role). The same office expectations apply to all Symmetry roles, Gusto&#39;s subsidiary, whose physical office is in Scottsdale.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Note: The San Francisco office expectations encompass both the San Francisco and San Jose metro areas.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;When approved to work from a location other than a Gusto office, a secure, reliable, and consistent internet connection is required. This includes non-office days for hybrid employees.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;Our customers come from all walks of life and so do we. You will act as a technical leader in this domain, focused on building industry-leader AI-powered features. We’re looking for a product-minded ML engineer with strong technical expertise, excellent communication skills, and a track record of driving impactful initiatives end to end.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Spearhead AI initiatives within Notebooks, serving as the technical owner for the design, development, and deployment of ML/AI-driven features.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape the long-term technical direction and strategy for AI workflows within Notebooks and related products.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Champion the full development cycle: from rapid prototyping and defining success metrics/evaluations to running experiments, iterating, and achieving production-ready scale and reliability.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Drive product direction with a strong user focus, always advocating for the best end-user experience.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Coordinate with other leaders and product teams interested in leveraging Notebooks in their AI-powered workflows.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop robust agent systems, including tools, retrieval and planning strategies, guardrails, prompt management/evaluations, fallbacks, and human-in-the-loop processes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Stay up to date with industry trends, guiding the adoption of new technologies and evolving best-practices.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have a BS/MS/PhD in a scientific field or equivalent experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;10+ years of relevant engineering experience, as well as experience acting as a technical lead&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven track record of leading large-scale AI/ML initiatives in a product-driven environment&lt;/li&gt;
