We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generous and competitive benefits package&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous career development and pathing opportunities&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Employee-focused best in class onboarding&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Internal mentor and cross-departmental buddy program&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Friendly and inclusive workplace culture&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. #### 43. Staff AI Engineer - Notebooks  at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 4.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 5/10 | Salary: 6/10
- **Location:** Portugal, Remote
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates genuinely strong AI/ML technical skills — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, agent systems, and full-stack deployment — that align well with the Notebooks team's AI-driven workflow vision. However, this is a Staff-level role requiring 10+ years of engineering experience and a proven track record of technical leadership at scale, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of internship/project experience. The location is also a mismatch: the role is based in Portugal (remote within Portugal), while the candidate prefers the US or Canada.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG pipeline experience (LangChain LCEL, BM25 + dense hybrid retrieval, Milvus, ChromaDB, FlashRank reranking, RAGAS evaluation)
- Practical LangGraph multi-agent system design demonstrated in personal project
- Full-stack AI deployment experience: FastAPI, Docker, Redis, PostgreSQL, WebSocket, React/TypeScript
- Published research in multimodal video understanding (ACM MM '23), showing academic AI depth
- Computer Vision expertise with YOLOv8, SAHI, and industrial defect detection pipelines
- Strong educational background: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) + BASc at UBC (GPA 3.7, Dean's Honor List)
- Exposure to evaluation frameworks (RAGAS, ATS keyword scoring, LLM self-critique) aligning with the role's emphasis on success metrics and evals
- Broad ML/AI skill set: deep learning, computer vision, reinforcement learning, LLM fine-tuning awareness

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 10+ years of engineering experience (candidate has ~1-2 years)
- Technical leadership / staff-level ownership of large-scale AI initiatives
- Mentorship and engineering culture cultivation experience
- Model fine-tuning and training at production scale
- Experience in observability, monitoring, or SaaS product environments (Datadog's domain)
- Proven track record driving cross-team product direction end-to-end
- Natural-language data analysis or notebook-style tooling experience

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the end-to-end RAG system built at Disanji: 35% retrieval precision improvement, 0.84 RAGAS context recall, and production deployment with Docker + FastAPI + Redis — this directly mirrors the Notebooks team's AI workflow goals
- Emphasize the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline in the Resume Generator project as concrete evidence of agent system design (tools, routing, guardrails, evaluation layers) matching the job's 'robust agent systems' requirement
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research rigor and ability to stay current with AI/ML trends
- Discuss the dual-layer evaluation framework (ATS scoring + LLM self-critique) as alignment with the role's emphasis on defining success metrics and running experiments
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively and frame the MEng at Waterloo (graduating Oct 2025) as accelerated, focused preparation — express eagerness to grow into a technical leadership role
- Address the location preference mismatch early: clarify openness to Portugal remote work if applicable, or note that the role lists 'Portugal, Remote' which may allow broader remote eligibility

#### 44. Senior Staff Machine Learning Engineer – GenAI at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 3.5/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 5/10 | Experience: 1/10 | Education: 2/10 | Location: 7/10 | Salary: 3/10
- **Location:** United States
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian is a mid-level candidate currently completing a Master's degree with strong foundational AI/ML skills in RAG, LangChain, LangGraph, and computer vision — all relevant to GenAI. However, this role is a Senior Staff ML Engineer position at Airbnb requiring a PhD, 10+ years of industry experience, and proven leadership at scale, none of which the candidate possesses. The technical skill overlap is real but insufficient for the seniority level demanded, and the salary range the candidate expects ($100K–$200K) falls well below the posted range of $244K–$305K, further indicating a significant level mismatch.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on RAG pipeline experience using LangChain LCEL, BM25 + dense vector hybrid retrieval, and Milvus — directly relevant to GenAI work
- LangGraph multi-agent orchestration experience (4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing) aligns with the agentic/automation preferred qualification
- Practical evaluation pipeline experience using RAGAS (0.84 context recall) demonstrates ML rigor
- Computer vision background (YOLOv8, SAHI, DBVM) and ACM MM publication show research depth
- Full-stack production deployment skills (FastAPI, Docker, Redis, PostgreSQL, WebSocket) support end-to-end ML productionization
- Strong academic record: 3.9 GPA MEng at University of Waterloo, Dean's Honor List at UBC
- Remote work preference aligns with the US-Remote Eligible designation of the role

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- PhD in Computer Science, Mathematics, Statistics, or related field (candidate has/is pursuing MEng)
- 10+ years of industry ML/AI experience (candidate has ~1 year of internship experience)
- 2+ years of dedicated GenAI industry experience at production scale
- Senior IC leadership experience guiding large ML teams and cross-functional stakeholders
- Large-scale ML infrastructure design (training/serving skew minimization, feature stores, low-latency serving)
- Experience with NLP at production scale (search, ranking, recommendation, marketplace optimization)
- Anomaly detection and real-time outlier detection systems
- Agile practice for AI production lifecycle at enterprise scale
- Experience with CRM or case management systems (e.g., Salesforce, Zendesk replacements)
- Mentorship and organizational influence at Staff/Principal level

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the hybrid retrieval architecture (BM25 + BGE-M3 dense/sparse in Milvus), 35% precision improvement, and RAGAS evaluation pipeline — this directly maps to Airbnb's AI-augmented support systems
- Showcase the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing as evidence of agentic AI experience, which is a preferred qualification — frame it in terms of orchestration design patterns applicable to Airbnb's agent co-pilot tools
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication on DBVM to demonstrate research credibility and ability to work at the intersection of video/language models, relevant to multimodal AI directions
- Emphasize full-stack deployment experience (FastAPI + Docker + Redis + PostgreSQL + WebSocket) to show ability to own end-to-end ML productionization, not just modeling
- Be transparent about the seniority gap and position yourself as a high-growth candidate — if applying, frame the conversation around a more junior AI Engineer role rather than Senior Staff

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Senior Staff Machine Learning Engineer – GenAI
Company: Airbnb
Location: United States
Description: &lt;div class=&quot;content-intro&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: helvetica, arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12pt;&quot;&gt;Airbnb was born in 2007 when two hosts welcomed three guests to their San Francisco home, and has since grown to over 5 million hosts who have welcomed over 2 billion guest arrivals in almost every country across the globe. Every day, hosts offer unique stays and experiences that make it possible for guests to connect with communities in a more authentic way.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Community you will join&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;At Airbnb, we’re reimagining how support is delivered at a global scale. The Community Support Engineering (CSE) team is leading a multi-year transformation—building intelligent, AI-augmented systems that provide timely, personalized, and always-on support experiences for our global community.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;The CS Foundations Engineering (CSFE) team powers this mission by delivering robust platforms, reusable systems, and developer-facing infrastructure. Our tools help agents deliver exceptional service, enable faster product iteration, and support Airbnb’s mission to create a world where anyone can belong anywhere—with help that’s just a moment away.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;The Difference You Will Make:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;As a machine learning engineer or scientist, your expertise will be pivotal in developing AI-powered solutions to shape the future of the Airbnb customer experience with cutting-edge AI techniques. You will drive and guide the rest of the engineers to brainstorm, design and develop AI products and features from inception to production.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;We&#39;re seeking a Senior Staff Engineer who thrives at the intersection of technical depth, architectural thinking, and mentorship.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’ll collaborate with cross-functional leaders, build resilient systems that operate globally at scale, and help evolve the foundational building blocks behind AI-powered customer support.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;While this role won’t own every project below, the below showcase the breadth and depth of work across our group, and where your leadership may be needed:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Building a unified, platform-native case management system (AirCase), abstracted via Viaduct APIs and replacing legacy CRM systems.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Designing scalable AI workflow orchestration to power agent co-pilot tools and intelligent response automation.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Modernizing our Delivery Management Console (DMC) to include AI powered real-time performance insights, outlier detection, and agent coaching tools.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Establishing shared data foundations to enable AI/ML solutions, feedback loops, and personalization across the support journey.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Delivering next generating matching capabilities (Routing) so incoming customer contacts are matched to the best agents using AI and Intelligence to deliver personalized and differentiated support experiences&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;A Typical Day:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work with large scale structured and unstructured data; explore, experiment, build and continuously improve Machine Learning models and pipelines for Airbnb product, business and operational use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Work collaboratively with cross-functional partners including product managers, operations and data scientists, to identify opportunities for business impact; understand, refine, and prioritize requirements for machine learning, and drive engineering decisions.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands-on develop, productionize, and operate Machine Learning models and pipelines at scale, including both batch and real-time use cases.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Leverage third-party and in-house Machine Learning tools &amp;amp; infrastructure to develop reusable, highly differentiating and high-performing Machine Learning systems, enable fast model development, low-latency serving and ease of model quality upkeep.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate actively with engineers to apply ML / AI in their solutions to help validate ideas and guide to the right outcomes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Partner with other ML Engineers in foundations engineering to mentor and develop initiative to make ML application a core discipline for non ML engineers.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Minimum Qualifications&lt;/strong&gt;:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Educational Background: PhD in Computer Science,&amp;nbsp; Mathematics, Statistics, or related technical field.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Industry Experience: 10+ years of experience in building, testing and shipping AI models and products from inception to production; including 2+ years of experience with GenAI.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Leadership Experience: 10+ years experience leading and guiding machine learning and AI projects that deliver sizable impact as a senior IC.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Technical Proficiency: Deep knowledge and hands-on experience with Machine Learning best practices (eg. training/serving skew minimization, feature engineering, feature/model selection), algorithms (eg. neural networks/deep learning, optimization) and domains (eg. NLP, computer vision, personalization, search and recommendation, marketplace optimization, anomaly detection)&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;Preferred Qualifications:&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Agentic and Automation&lt;/strong&gt;: Experience with AI technologies in automating processes and developing agentic solutions and frameworks.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Agile Practice for AI Production&lt;/strong&gt;: Experience with the entire AI product development lifecycle from incubation to production at scale, following agile practices in the Applied AI/ML domain.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Infrastructure Acumen: Experience building robust testing frameworks for agent behavior validation and continuous improvement, and driving architectural requirements on ML infrastructures&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #000000; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;&quot;&gt;This position is US - Remote Eligible. The role may include occasional work at an Airbnb office or attendance at offsites, as agreed to with your manager. While the position is Remote Eligible, you must live in a state where Airbnb Payments, Inc. (a subsidiary of Airbnb, Inc.) is a registered entity. Approved remote states include: Alabama, California, Florida, Georgia, Illinois, Minnesota, Mississippi, Nevada, New Jersey, New York, North Carolina, Ohio, Oregon, Pennsylvania, Tennessee, Texas, and Washington. This list is continuously evolving, so please check back with us if the state you live in is excluded. If your position is employed by another Airbnb entity, your recruiter will inform you what states you are eligible to work from.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Our Commitment To Inclusion &amp;amp; Belonging:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;Airbnb is committed to working with the broadest talent pool possible. We believe diverse ideas foster innovation and engagement, and allow us to attract creatively-led people, and to develop the best products, services and solutions. All qualified individuals are encouraged to apply.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;We strive to also provide a disability inclusive application and interview process. If you are a candidate with a disability and require reasonable accommodation in order to submit an application, please contact us at: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;a style=&quot;color: #000000;&quot; href=&quot;mailto:reasonableaccommodations@airbnb.com&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;reasonableaccommodations@airbnb.com&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400;&quot;&gt;. Please include your full name, the role you’re applying for and the accommodation necessary to assist you with the recruiting process.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-weight: 400; font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: #000000;&quot;&gt;We ask that you only reach out to us if you are a candidate whose disability prevents you from being able to complete our online application.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);&quot;&gt;How We&#39;ll Take Care of You:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p class=&quot;p1&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;font-size: 16px;&quot;&gt;Our job titles may span more than one career level. The actual base pay is dependent upon many factors, such as: training, transferable skills, work experience, business needs and market demands. The base pay range is subject to change and may be modified in the future. This role may also be eligible for bonus, equity, benefits, and Employee Travel Credits. &amp;nbsp;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;Pay Range&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$244,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$305,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

</details>

#### 45. Staff AI Engineer - Notebooks at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 4.1/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 4/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** New York, New York, USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates genuinely strong AI/ML technical skills — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, agent systems, and full-stack deployment — that are directionally aligned with what Datadog's Notebooks team is building. However, this is a Staff-level role requiring 10+ years of engineering experience and a proven track record of leading large-scale AI/ML initiatives, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of internship/project experience. The location is also a significant mismatch: the role is hybrid in New York City, while the candidate prefers remote or Canadian locations, and the salary band ($234K–$300K) exceeds the candidate's stated preference ceiling of $200K, suggesting a seniority/compensation misalignment on both ends.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on RAG pipeline experience (LangChain LCEL, BM25 + dense hybrid retrieval, Milvus, FlashRank reranking, RAGAS evaluation) directly mirrors the AI-driven workflows Datadog Notebooks is building
- LangGraph multi-agent system design demonstrated in the Resume & Cover Letter Generator project (4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing)
- Full-stack AI deployment experience: FastAPI, Docker, PostgreSQL, Redis, WebSocket, React/TypeScript — production-grade stack alignment
- Published ACM MM '23 paper on multimodal video-language models, showing research depth in AI/ML
- Strong academic credentials: 3.9 GPA MEng at University of Waterloo with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, Computer Vision, and DevOps
- Experience building automated evaluation pipelines (RAGAS, ATS keyword scoring, LLM self-critique) — aligns with Datadog's emphasis on defining success metrics and evaluations
- Computer Vision expertise (YOLOv8, SAHI) adds breadth to AI/ML profile
- Familiarity with semantic caching (Redis), streaming (SSE/WebSocket), and scalable backend patterns relevant to production AI systems

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 10+ years of engineering experience (candidate has ~1–2 years of internship/project experience)
- Technical leadership / staff-level ownership of large-scale AI/ML product initiatives
- Experience mentoring engineers and cultivating engineering culture
- Model fine-tuning and training at scale (not evidenced beyond inference/deployment)
- Observability/monitoring domain knowledge (Datadog's core product context)
- Proven track record driving cross-team AI strategy and product direction
- Experience with enterprise-scale ML infrastructure and reliability engineering

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Lead with the production RAG system at Disanji: emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement, 0.84 RAGAS context recall, and the architectural decisions (hybrid BM25 + dense retrieval, FlashRank reranking, Redis semantic caching) — this directly maps to Datadog Notebooks' NL data analysis workflows
- Highlight the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline project as evidence of agent system design thinking: quality-gated routing, dual-layer evaluation, and human-in-the-loop critique mirror exactly what the Notebooks team is building
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research credibility and ability to contribute to novel AI approaches, not just apply existing frameworks
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively but frame the trajectory: MEng at Waterloo (3.9 GPA), two AI-focused internships, a published paper, and independent production-grade projects show accelerated growth — position yourself as a high-ceiling mid-level hire
- Discuss familiarity with evaluation-driven development (RAGAS, ATS scoring, LLM self-critique) to show alignment with Datadog's emphasis on defining success metrics before shipping AI features
- If open to relocation, explicitly state willingness to relocate to New York for a hybrid arrangement — this removes a key disqualifier
- Connect your full-stack skills (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, Docker, PostgreSQL) to the need for AI engineers who can own the entire feature lifecycle, not just the model layer

<details>
<summary>Job Description (click to expand)</summary>

Title: Staff AI Engineer - Notebooks
Company: Datadog
Location: New York, New York, USA
Description: &lt;p&gt;We&#39;re looking for a Staff Software Engineer with deep experience in AI/ML to join the Notebooks team. Datadog Notebooks provide a flexible tool for ad-hoc data analysis, collaborative investigations, and technical documentation. Its fluidity makes it an ideal surface for agents and humans to interact. The Notebook team is building AI-driven workflows for natural-language data analysis, assisted graph generation, automated investigations, and more. You will act as a technical leader in this domain, focused on building industry-leader AI-powered features. We’re looking for a product-minded ML engineer with strong technical expertise, excellent communication skills, and a track record of driving impactful initiatives end to end.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Spearhead AI initiatives within Notebooks, serving as the technical owner for the design, development, and deployment of ML/AI-driven features.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape the long-term technical direction and strategy for AI workflows within Notebooks and related products.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Champion the full development cycle: from rapid prototyping and defining success metrics/evaluations to running experiments, iterating, and achieving production-ready scale and reliability.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Drive product direction with a strong user focus, always advocating for the best end-user experience.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Coordinate with other leaders and product teams interested in leveraging Notebooks in their AI-powered workflows.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop robust agent systems, including tools, retrieval and planning strategies, guardrails, prompt management/evaluations, fallbacks, and human-in-the-loop processes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Stay up to date with industry trends, guiding the adoption of new technologies and evolving best-practices.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have a BS/MS/PhD in a scientific field or equivalent experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;10+ years of relevant engineering experience, as well as experience acting as a technical lead&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven track record of leading large-scale AI/ML initiatives in a product-driven environment&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have significant experience in model deployment, development, training, fine-tuning, and evaluation&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You are passionate about mentoring others and cultivating a high-performing engineering culture.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re excited about leveraging AI tools to enhance how you code, solve problems, and build&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generous and competitive benefits package&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous career development and pathing opportunities&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Employee-focused best in class onboarding&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Internal mentor and cross-departmental buddy program&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Friendly and inclusive workplace culture&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-pay-transparency&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-input&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;description&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;Datadog offers a competitive salary and equity package, and may include variable compensation. Actual compensation is based on factors such as the candidate&#39;s skills, qualifications, and experience. In addition, Datadog offers a wide range of best in class, comprehensive and inclusive employee benefits for this role including healthcare, dental, parental planning, and mental health benefits, a 401(k) plan and match, paid time off, fitness reimbursements, and a discounted employee stock purchase plan.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;title&quot;&gt;The reasonably estimated yearly salary for this role at Datadog is:&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;pay-range&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;$234,000&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;divider&quot;&gt;&amp;mdash;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;$300,000 USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. Learn more about #DatadogLife on &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.instagram.com/datadoghq/?hl=en&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Instagram&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/datadog&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;LinkedIn,&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.datadoghq.com/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Datadog Learning Center. &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;215&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Equal Opportunity at Datadog:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog is proud to offer &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.eeoc.gov/sites/default/files/2023-06/22-088_EEOC_KnowYourRights6.12ScreenRdr.pdf&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;equal employment opportunity&lt;/a&gt; to everyone regardless of race, color, ancestry, religion, sex, national origin, sexual orientation, age, citizenship, marital status, disability, gender identity, veteran status, and other characteristics protected by law. We also consider qualified applicants regardless of criminal histories, consistent with legal requirements. Here are our &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/candidate-legal-notices/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Candidate Legal Notices&lt;/a&gt; for your reference.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog endeavors to make our Careers Page accessible to all users. If you would like to contact us regarding the accessibility of our website or need assistance completing the application process, please complete &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSeoJeduEZ2NdZF_65uZmWUrCM-zee7yADXX1s6Xr9zAGq8iKA/viewform&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;this form&lt;/a&gt;. This form is for accommodation requests only and cannot be used to inquire about the status of applications.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;662&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Privacy and AI Guidelines:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;662&quot;&gt;Any information you submit to Datadog as part of your application will be processed in accordance with Datadog’s&amp;nbsp;&lt;a class=&quot;c-link&quot; href=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/applicant-candidate-privacy/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot; data-stringify-link=&quot;https://www.datadoghq.com/legal/applicant-candidate-privacy/&quot; data-sk=&quot;tooltip_parent&quot;&gt;Applicant and Candidate Privacy Notice&lt;/a&gt;. - **Location:** New York, New York, USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian has a strong academic background and solid hands-on AI/ML experience (RAG pipelines, LangChain, LangGraph, computer vision), which partially aligns with Datadog's AI engineering needs. However, this is a Staff-level role requiring deep expertise in distributed systems, simulation environments, and synthetic data generation — areas where the candidate has little to no demonstrated experience. The seniority gap is significant: the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree, while the role demands staff-level leadership, architectural ownership, and mentorship responsibilities at a $234K–$300K compensation band.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong AI/ML foundation: hands-on RAG pipeline development with LangChain, LangGraph, ChromaDB, and hybrid retrieval (BM25 + BGE-M3)
- Published research in multimodal video understanding (ACM MM '23), demonstrating academic AI credibility
- Practical experience building and evaluating AI agent pipelines with automated evaluation frameworks (RAGAS)
- Full-stack engineering capability: FastAPI, React/TypeScript, PostgreSQL, Docker, WebSocket — relevant for building simulation tooling
- High academic achievement: 3.9 GPA in MEng at University of Waterloo with specialization in RL and LLM
- Experience with computer vision (YOLOv8, SAHI) and deep learning, which overlaps with AI agent evaluation needs
- Familiarity with cloud infrastructure (AWS, Azure) and containerization (Docker Compose, Redis)

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- Distributed systems engineering — no evidence of designing or operating large-scale distributed infrastructure
- Simulation environment design — no experience with synthetic environment construction or simulation frameworks (e.g., OpenAI Gym, Isaac Sim, custom simulators)
- Synthetic data generation — no demonstrated work in generative modeling or synthetic dataset creation pipelines
- Staff/senior-level technical leadership — no evidence of mentoring engineers, defining team architecture, or cross-functional technical direction
- Observability/monitoring domain knowledge — no experience with Datadog, Prometheus, or similar observability platforms
- Reinforcement learning in production — RL is listed as a specialization but no project evidence of applied RL agent training
- Infrastructure/backend engineering at scale — experience is limited to intern-level and project-level backend work

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline design, hybrid retrieval architecture, and measurable outcomes (35% precision improvement, 0.84 context recall) as evidence of building AI systems beyond model consumption
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research credibility and ability to work on novel AI problems — frame DBVM as experience with complex multimodal AI system design
- Connect the automated evaluation pipeline work (RAGAS, ATS keyword scoring, LLM self-critique) to Datadog's need for AI agent evaluation pipelines — this is the strongest overlap with the role's responsibilities
- Discuss the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project as evidence of AI agent orchestration experience, and draw parallels to simulation environments where agents must reason and act
- Be transparent about the seniority gap but frame the MEng specialization in Reinforcement Learning and LLM as a foundation for growing into simulation environment design — reference any RL coursework or projects
- Address the location constraint proactively: clarify willingness to relocate to New York or discuss visa/work authorization status if applying from Canada

Title: Staff AI Engineer - Simulation Environments
Company: Datadog
Location: New York, New York, USA
Description: &lt;p&gt;Datadog is building the platform that empowers engineers to observe, understand, and act on their systems. As we expand into the world of intelligent systems, our Simulation Environments team is pioneering high-fidelity, synthetic environments to safely train and evaluate AI agents. As a Staff Engineer on this team, you’ll play a foundational role in designing and scaling this simulation platform. You&#39;ll collaborate across AI research and product teams to deliver environments where AI agents can learn, reason, and take action—pushing the boundaries of observability and automation.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Design and build high-fidelity simulation environments that model complex distributed systems with realism and scalability&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop synthetic data generation tools and evaluation pipelines to train and assess AI agents across Datadog products&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Collaborate with AI research and product engineering teams to integrate models into simulation and production workflows&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Define architecture and technical direction for Datadog’s emerging simulation platform&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Mentor engineers and contribute to shaping the culture of a new, cross-functional team&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have a BS, MS, PhD, or equivalent experience in Computer Science, Engineering, or a related technical field&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Strong experience in distributed systems, backend, or infrastructure engineering&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Hands-on experience building or deploying ML/AI systems, beyond consumption of existing models&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Familiarity with synthetic data, simulation frameworks, or generative modeling approaches&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven ability to turn ambiguity into clarity and define new systems from the ground up&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Skilled communicator and team leader, especially in cross-functional and early-stage environments&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. #### 48. Staff GenAI Engineer - Application Performance Monitoring (APM) at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 4.3/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 7/10 | Location: 5/10 | Salary: 4/10
- **Location:** New York, New York, USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates genuine and impressive GenAI/ML technical skills — including RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, agentic workflows, and model evaluation — that are directly relevant to this role's technical domain. However, this is a Staff-level position requiring 10+ years of engineering experience and a proven track record of leading large-scale GenAI/ML initiatives, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1-2 years of internship/research experience. The location is also a significant mismatch: the role is hybrid in New York City, while the candidate prefers remote/Canada, and the salary band ($234K–$300K) far exceeds the candidate's stated range of $100K–$200K, suggesting a seniority and compensation level misalignment.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on RAG pipeline experience (LangChain LCEL, BM25 + dense hybrid retrieval, Milvus, ChromaDB, FlashRank reranking) directly relevant to GenAI engineering
- Practical agentic workflow experience via LangGraph multi-agent pipeline project with quality-gated routing
- Published research in multimodal video understanding at ACM MM '23, demonstrating academic ML credibility
- Full-stack deployment skills (FastAPI, Docker, PostgreSQL, Redis, WebSocket) enabling end-to-end model productionization
- Automated evaluation pipeline experience using RAGAS (0.84 context recall), showing familiarity with GenAI benchmarking
- Strong academic credentials: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Experience with computer vision models (YOLOv8, SAHI) and deep learning, broadening ML breadth

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 10+ years of engineering experience (candidate has ~1-2 years of internship/research experience)
- Staff/technical lead experience — no evidence of leading engineering teams, mentoring engineers, or driving org-wide initiatives
- Application Performance Monitoring (APM) or observability domain knowledge (distributed tracing, profiling, telemetry)
- Large-scale model training and fine-tuning experience (the role requires significant model training/fine-tuning at scale)
- Cross-functional team leadership and product direction influence at a senior level
- Experience with production ML systems at scale (millions of users / enterprise SaaS scale)
- Model deployment at scale (the role implies large-scale inference infrastructure beyond Docker Compose setups)

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end pipeline (hybrid retrieval, reranking, streaming, Redis caching) and the quantified 35% precision improvement — this directly maps to Datadog's GenAI model deployment requirements
- Discuss the LangGraph 4-agent pipeline project in depth, framing it around agentic investigation workflows — directly analogous to Datadog APM's goal of 'agentic investigations and incident troubleshooting'
- Leverage the RAGAS evaluation pipeline experience to speak to model evaluation rigor, which is a core responsibility in the job description ('build and benchmark GenAI/ML models')
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research depth and ability to work on novel ML problems, compensating for limited years of experience
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively and frame your trajectory: MEng at Waterloo (GPA 3.9), two internships, a publication, and multiple production AI projects in under 2 years shows rapid growth
- If open to relocation, explicitly state willingness to relocate to New York or work hybrid — this is a significant barrier and must be addressed upfront

#### 49. Staff AI Engineer - Notebooks  at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 4.1/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 6/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 8/10 | Location: 2/10 | Salary: 5/10
- **Location:** Madrid, Spain; Paris, France
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates strong foundational AI/ML skills — particularly in RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, and agent systems — that are directly relevant to the Notebooks team's AI-driven workflows. However, this is a Staff-level role requiring 10+ years of engineering experience and a proven track record of leading large-scale AI/ML initiatives, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of internship/project experience. Additionally, the role is located in Madrid or Paris (on-site/hybrid), which is incompatible with the candidate's preferred locations of Remote, US, or Canada.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on RAG pipeline experience using LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + dense vectors), reranking, and streaming — directly aligned with Notebooks AI workflows
- LangGraph multi-agent system design (4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing) matches the job's requirement for robust agent systems
- Automated evaluation pipeline experience (RAGAS, ATS keyword scoring, LLM self-critique) aligns with the role's emphasis on defining success metrics and evaluations
- Full-stack capability (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, PostgreSQL, Docker) supports end-to-end product development
- Strong academic background: MEng at University of Waterloo (GPA 3.9) with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models demonstrates research depth
- Computer Vision expertise (YOLOv8, SAHI, DBVM) adds breadth to AI/ML profile
- Experience with semantic caching (Redis), vector databases (Milvus, ChromaDB), and production deployment (Docker Compose, FastAPI + SSE)

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 10+ years of engineering experience (candidate has ~1-2 years of internship/project experience)
- Staff/technical lead experience — no evidence of leading engineering teams or driving org-wide technical strategy
- Large-scale production AI/ML system ownership at a product company
- Model fine-tuning and training at scale (mentioned in job but not evidenced in resume)
- Observability/monitoring platform domain knowledge (Datadog's core product context)
- Mentorship and engineering culture cultivation experience
- Experience coordinating across multiple product teams and stakeholders at a senior level

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the end-to-end ownership, 35% retrieval precision improvement, and RAGAS evaluation pipeline — this directly mirrors what the Notebooks team is building for natural-language data analysis
- Discuss the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project in depth: quality-gated routing, dual-layer evaluation (ATS + LLM self-critique), and WebSocket streaming demonstrate hands-on agent system design that aligns with the role's core responsibilities
- Leverage the ACM MM '23 publication to demonstrate research credibility and ability to work on novel AI problems — position it as evidence of technical depth beyond typical engineering roles
- Address the experience gap proactively: frame the combination of a 3.9 GPA MEng at Waterloo, two AI-focused internships, and independent full-stack AI projects as equivalent to broader industry exposure, and express eagerness to grow into a Staff-level scope
- If open to relocation, explicitly state willingness to relocate to Madrid or Paris — this is a critical blocker and must be addressed upfront to be considered for this role

Title: Staff AI Engineer - Notebooks 
Company: Datadog
Location: Madrid, Spain; Paris, France
Description: &lt;p&gt;We&#39;re looking for a Staff Software Engineer with deep experience in AI/ML to join the Notebooks team. Datadog Notebooks provide a flexible tool for ad-hoc data analysis, collaborative investigations, and technical documentation. Its fluidity makes it an ideal surface for agents and humans to interact. The Notebook team is building AI-driven workflows for natural-language data analysis, assisted graph generation, automated investigations, and more. You will act as a technical leader in this domain, focused on building industry-leader AI-powered features. We’re looking for a product-minded ML engineer with strong technical expertise, excellent communication skills, and a track record of driving impactful initiatives end to end.&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;At Datadog, we place value in our office culture - the relationships and collaboration it builds and the creativity it brings to the table. We operate as a hybrid workplace to ensure our Datadogs can create a work-life harmony that best fits them.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;What You’ll Do:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Spearhead AI initiatives within Notebooks, serving as the technical owner for the design, development, and deployment of ML/AI-driven features.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Shape the long-term technical direction and strategy for AI workflows within Notebooks and related products.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Champion the full development cycle: from rapid prototyping and defining success metrics/evaluations to running experiments, iterating, and achieving production-ready scale and reliability.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Drive product direction with a strong user focus, always advocating for the best end-user experience.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Coordinate with other leaders and product teams interested in leveraging Notebooks in their AI-powered workflows.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Develop robust agent systems, including tools, retrieval and planning strategies, guardrails, prompt management/evaluations, fallbacks, and human-in-the-loop processes.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Stay up to date with industry trends, guiding the adoption of new technologies and evolving best-practices.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Who You Are:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have a BS/MS/PhD in a scientific field or equivalent experience&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;10+ years of relevant engineering experience, as well as experience acting as a technical lead&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Proven track record of leading large-scale AI/ML initiatives in a product-driven environment&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have significant experience in model deployment, development, training, fine-tuning, and evaluation&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You are passionate about mentoring others and cultivating a high-performing engineering culture.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You’re excited about leveraging AI tools to enhance how you code, solve problems, and build.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;You have demonstrated ability to use AI coding tools in day-to-day workflows and validate, critique, and refine AI-generated output.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Bonus: you’re motivated to push the boundaries of how AI can improve software engineering best practices and contribute to building AI-enabled products.&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Datadog values people from all walks of life. We understand not everyone will meet all the above qualifications on day one. That&#39;s okay. If you’re passionate about technology and want to grow your skills, we encourage you to apply.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Benefits and Growth:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt; &lt;li&gt;Generous and competitive benefits package&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;New hire stock equity (RSUs) and employee stock purchase plan&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Continuous career development and pathing opportunities&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Employee-focused best in class onboarding&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Internal mentor and cross-departmental buddy program&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Friendly and inclusive workplace culture&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Benefits and Growth listed above may vary based on the country of your employment and the nature of your employment with Datadog.&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style=&quot;color: white;&quot;&gt;#LI-Hybrid&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;content-conclusion&quot;&gt;&lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;About Datadog:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog (NASDAQ: DDOG) is a global SaaS business, delivering a rare combination of growth and profitability. We are on a mission to break down silos and solve complexity in the cloud age by enabling digital transformation, cloud migration, and infrastructure monitoring of our customers’ entire technology stacks. Built by engineers, for engineers, Datadog is used by organizations of all sizes across a wide range of industries. Together, we champion professional development, diversity of thought, innovation, and work excellence to empower continuous growth. Join the pack and become part of a collaborative, pragmatic, and thoughtful people-first community where we solve tough problems, take smart risks, and celebrate one another. Learn more about #DatadogLife on &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.instagram.com/datadoghq/?hl=en&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Instagram&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.linkedin.com/company/datadog&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;LinkedIn,&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.datadoghq.com/&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;Datadog Learning Center. &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p data-renderer-start-pos=&quot;215&quot;&gt;&lt;strong data-renderer-mark=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Equal Opportunity at Datadog:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Datadog is proud to offer &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.eeoc.gov/sites/default/files/2023-06/22-088_EEOC_KnowYourRights6.12ScreenRdr.pdf&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;equal employment opportunity&lt;/a&gt; to everyone regardless of race, color, ancestry, religion, sex, national origin, sexual orientation, age, citizenship, marital status, disability, gender identity, veteran status, and other characteristics protected by law. #### 50. Senior Staff GenAI Engineer - Application Performance Monitoring (APM) at Datadog

- **Overall Score:** 3.9/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 5/10 | Experience: 2/10 | Education: 7/10 | Location: 4/10 | Salary: 3/10
- **Location:** New York, New York, USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian demonstrates genuine technical aptitude in GenAI/ML — including RAG pipelines, LangChain/LangGraph, agentic workflows, and model evaluation — which aligns directionally with the role's technical domain. However, this is a Senior Staff-level position requiring 10+ years of engineering experience and 4+ years leading cross-team technical initiatives, whereas the candidate is a mid-level engineer still completing their Master's degree with roughly 1–2 years of internship/project experience. The role is also hybrid in New York City, while the candidate is based in Canada and prefers remote/hybrid work, and the salary band ($272K–$340K) far exceeds the candidate's stated range of $100K–$200K.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Hands-on RAG pipeline experience with LangChain LCEL, hybrid retrieval (BM25 + dense vectors), reranking, and streaming — directly relevant to GenAI work at Datadog
- Built and evaluated end-to-end ML systems including automated RAGAS evaluation pipelines, demonstrating product-minded ML engineering
- Experience with agentic workflows via LangGraph (4-agent pipeline with quality-gated routing)
- Published research at ACM MM '23 on multimodal video-language models, showing academic depth in GenAI/ML
- Strong full-stack engineering skills (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, Docker, PostgreSQL, WebSocket) enabling end-to-end product delivery
- Proficiency in Python, the dominant language for ML/AI engineering
- Computer Vision experience (YOLOv8, SAHI) adds breadth to ML skill set
- High academic performance (GPA 3.9 MEng, 3.7 BEng, Dean's Honor List) from strong institutions (UBC, UWaterloo)

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 10+ years of engineering experience (candidate has ~1–2 years of internship/project experience)
- 4+ years leading cross-team technical initiatives at a senior/staff level
- Experience with Application Performance Monitoring (APM), distributed tracing, or observability tooling
- Proven track record of leading large-scale GenAI/ML initiatives in a product-driven environment
- Mentorship and engineering culture leadership at scale
- Experience with model training and fine-tuning at production scale (not just inference/RAG)
- Benchmarking and deploying ML models at enterprise/cloud scale
- Cross-functional technical leadership and long-term strategy ownership
- Experience with Kafka or large-scale streaming infrastructure in production ML contexts

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji: emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement, RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall), and Redis semantic caching as evidence of rigorous, metrics-driven ML engineering
- Discuss the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project in depth — quality-gated routing, dual-layer ATS + LLM self-critique evaluation, and full-stack delivery — as a direct analog to Datadog's agentic investigation workflows
- Reference the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video-language models to demonstrate research credibility and depth in the GenAI space
- Acknowledge the seniority gap proactively and frame the conversation around rapid growth trajectory, strong academic foundation, and the breadth of end-to-end systems built in a short time
- Express genuine interest in APM/observability as a domain and connect it to your experience building evaluation and monitoring pipelines for AI systems

#### 51. Senior Staff Machine Learning Engineer, Communication & Connectivity at Airbnb

- **Overall Score:** 3.5/10
- **Breakdown:** Skills: 5/10 | Experience: 1/10 | Education: 7/10 | Location: 6/10 | Salary: 4/10
- **Location:** Remote - USA
- **Employment Type:** Not specified
- **Source:** greenhouse

**Analysis:** Ruiqi Tian is a mid-level candidate currently completing a Master's degree with strong foundational AI/ML skills (RAG, LLMs, LangChain, computer vision), but this role demands a Senior Staff Engineer with 12+ years of software engineering experience and 2+ years in a Principal/Senior Staff capacity — a gap of roughly 8–10 years. While the candidate's technical toolkit overlaps meaningfully with the job's ML focus (LLMs, recommendation systems, scalable pipelines), the seniority, leadership, and organizational mentorship requirements are far beyond their current career stage. Additionally, the role requires US residency for remote eligibility, and the candidate is currently based in Canada, which may present a legal/eligibility barrier.

**Strengths (Why it fits):**
- Strong hands-on LLM and RAG experience (LangChain, LangGraph, ChromaDB, BGE-M3, RAGAS evaluation)
- Production-grade RAG system deployment with FastAPI, Docker, Redis, and streaming — directly relevant to ML systems at scale
- Published research in multimodal video understanding (ACM MM '23), demonstrating applied ML research capability
- Full-stack AI product development experience (FastAPI, React/TypeScript, WebSocket, PostgreSQL)
- High academic achievement: GPA 3.9 MEng at University of Waterloo with specialization in LLM, Reinforcement Learning, and Computer Vision
- Experience with hybrid retrieval systems (BM25 + dense/sparse vectors, Milvus) and reranking pipelines
- Familiarity with cloud platforms (AWS, Azure) and DevOps tooling (Docker, Git, Kafka)

**Gaps (Why it may not fit):**
- 12+ years of software engineering experience (candidate has ~2-3 years total)
- 2+ years in a Principal or Senior Staff Engineer role with ownership of large-scale systems
- Experience leading, mentoring, and growing engineering teams across multiple organizations
- Recommendation engines and ranking systems at production scale
- Intent detection models for messaging/notification platforms
- Experience driving ML strategy and roadmap from inception to production at an organizational level
- Demonstrated cross-functional leadership with Data and Product engineering teams
- Experience with large-scale messaging/notification ML systems

**Interview Talking Points:**
- Highlight the production-grade RAG system built at Disanji Technology Institute — emphasize the 35% retrieval precision improvement, RAGAS evaluation pipeline (0.84 context recall), and full deployment stack (Docker, FastAPI, Redis) as evidence of building scalable ML systems.
- Discuss the ACM MM '23 publication on multimodal video understanding to demonstrate research depth and ability to contribute to novel ML system design.
- Emphasize the 4-agent LangGraph pipeline project with quality-gated routing and dual-layer ATS + LLM evaluation as a demonstration of agentic AI architecture thinking.
- Frame the hybrid retrieval system (BM25 + BGE-M3 dense/sparse vectors in Milvus + FlashRank reranking) as directly relevant to the ranking and retrieval systems Airbnb uses in messaging/notification personalization.
- Acknowledge the seniority gap honestly and position yourself as a high-growth candidate who could be considered for a more junior ML Engineer role, if one exists at Airbnb.
